Sprint 11Q does NOT build trade selection, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, ML, LLM, or live trading. The output of Sprint 11Q is a SETUP ASSESSMENT, not a trade. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11P public API signature was changed, and all 1014 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): CandlePattern (Sprint 11O) and MarketContext (Sprint 11P). Sprint 11Q adds the genuinely-new confluence combination layer on top.
- New models `src/engine/models/setup_confluence.py` (frozen+slots): enums SetupDirection (BULLISH/BEARISH/NEUTRAL/UNKNOWN), SetupClassification (NO_SETUP/WATCH/POTENTIAL_SETUP), EvidenceAlignment (ALIGNED/CONFLICTING/NEUTRAL/ABSENT), EvidenceSource (TREND/STRUCTURE/CANDLE/LOCATION/RANGE); models EvidenceItem, SetupEvidence (each of the 5 sources as its own EvidenceItem + supporting/conflicting convenience tuples; `.all` property in canonical order), SetupAssessment (index, timestamp, direction, classification, confluence_score, evidence, candle/structure/trend/location/regime_evidence labels, reason; properties has_conflict/is_potential_setup). Optional fields use None/UNKNOWN so "unobserved" is never silently "aligned/false". No business logic in models.
- New config `src/engine/config/setup_confluence_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): SetupConfluenceConfig ÔÇö min_supporting_for_watch=1, min_supporting_for_potential_setup=3, conflicting_blocks_potential_setup=True, neutral_candle_contributes=False, range_caps_classification=True, min_structure_for_evidence=2. No magic numbers in engines. Validation: watch>=1, potential>=1, potential>=watch, min_structure>=1.
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/setup_confluence.py`: SetupConfluenceEngine(config).assess(patterns, market_context, index, timestamp) -> SetupAssessment. Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe (reads ONLY the already-computed CandlePattern + MarketContext objects, both derived from candles[:T+1]; inspects no candles directly). intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.setup_confluence import SetupConfluenceEngine`.
  - DESIGN PRINCIPLE: interpretable evidence, NOT numeric aggregation. Builds one EvidenceItem per independent source (trend/structure/candle/location/range), each with its own direction + alignment relative to the candidate setup direction. Conflicts remain visible (bullish trend + bearish reversal candle -> CANDLE recorded CONFLICTING, not silently merged into "bullish").
  - Evidence rules (explicit, documented): TREND from MarketTrendState (BULLISH->BULLISH, BEARISH->BEARISH, NEUTRAL/RANGE->NEUTRAL, UNKNOWN->ABSENT); STRUCTURE from recent_structure sequence (HH/HL->BULLISH, LH/LL->BEARISH, mixed->NEUTRAL, <min_structure_for_evidence->ABSENT); CANDLE from patterns at T (HAMMER/BULLISH_ENGULFING->BULLISH, SHOOTING_STAR/BEARISH_ENGULFING->BEARISH, DOJI/INSIDE_BAR->NEUTRAL, both directional directions present->CONFLICTING, no pattern->ABSENT); LOCATION evaluated RELATIVE to candidate direction (BULLISH candidate + NEAR_SUPPORT/ABOVE_RESISTANCE->ALIGNED, + NEAR_RESISTANCE/BELOW_SUPPORT->CONFLICTING; BEARISH mirror; INSIDE_RANGE->NEUTRAL; UNKNOWN->ABSENT; no candidate->NEUTRAL); RANGE (IN_RANGE->NEUTRAL caps classification; NOT_IN_RANGE->does not conflict; UNKNOWN->ABSENT).
  - Candidate direction = majority among directional sources (trend/structure/candle); ties/no-votes -> UNKNOWN. Location evaluated relative to candidate. Confluence score = count of ALIGNED independent sources (int [0,5]) ÔÇö a count, NOT a probability/weighted score.
  - Classification: no candidate + no aligned -> NO_SETUP; range_caps_classification && IN_RANGE -> cap at WATCH; conflicting_blocks_potential_setup && conflict -> cap at WATCH; score>=min_potential -> POTENTIAL_SETUP; score>=min_watch -> WATCH; else NO_SETUP. POTENTIAL_SETUP = descriptive coherent candidate worth further evaluation, NOT profitable/predictive/guaranteed/recommendation.
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): PipelineConfig EXTENDED with enable_setup_confluence: bool = True + setup_confluence_config. HistoricalEvaluationPipeline constructs a SetupConfluenceEngine (only when enabled) and at each evaluation index T computes setup_assessment_at_t = assess(patterns_at_t, market_context_at_t, t, timestamp), attaching it to PipelineEvaluationPoint.setup_assessment. The setup assessment is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated/eligible_decisions/signals_validated/completed_trades + per-point signal_state/decision_direction/decision_status/suppressed + per-signal entry/stop/target prices identical enable vs disable; pipeline-point setup_assessment == standalone assess(prefix)). Disabling (enable_setup_confluence=False) reproduces the exact pre-11Q pipeline (setup_assessment=None).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field: PipelineEvaluationPoint.setup_assessment: SetupAssessment | None = None (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). PipelineResult unchanged.
- `src/engine/reporting/setup_confluence.py`: SetupAssessmentFormatter().format(assessment) -> str / .format_sequence(assessments) -> str. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Setup Assessment, Direction, Classification, Confluence, Supporting, Conflicting, Candle Evidence, Trend, Structure, Location, Regime, Evidence detail, Reason. Every report ends with the explicit warning "Setup classifications are descriptive technical evidence, not predictions or guarantees of profitability." (once per single format; once at end for a sequence). No predictive language (no "guaranteed"/"profitable"/"will rise"/"recommendation").
- scripts/test_setup_confluence.py: demo ÔÇö deterministic bullish/bearish/range/insufficient datasets; prints full setup assessment reports for strong bullish confluence (BULLISH+POTENTIAL_SETUP), strong bearish confluence (BEARISH+POTENTIAL_SETUP), conflicting evidence (bullish structure + bearish shooting star -> CANDLE conflicting, capped at WATCH), neutral/range market (capped at WATCH), insufficient structure (NO_SETUP); demonstrates future-leakage safety (PASS: prefix/full-series agrees at T; PASS: future mutation leaves setup(T) unchanged); demonstrates existing pipeline signal/trade behaviour unchanged (signals/trades before==after, PASS) + setup assessment attached to evaluation points + classification distribution. Explicitly states setups are NOT predictions. Demo exits successfully.
- tests/test_setup_confluence.py: 78 tests (evidence model basics frozen/slots/defaults/properties/members; config defaults+validation+immutability; trend evidence bullish/bearish/neutral/range/unknown; structure HH/HL/LH/LL/mixed/insufficient/min_structure_config; candle none-absent/hammer-bullish/shooting-star-bearish/doji-neutral/neutral-contributes-on-off/conflicting-directional-patterns; location near-support-aligned-bullish/near-resistance-conflicting-bullish/near-resistance-aligned-bearish/near-support-conflicting-bearish/inside-range-neutral/unknown-absent/no-candidate-neutral; range in-range/not-in-range/unknown; classification strong-bullish-potential/strong-bearish-potential/conflicting-recorded-not-bullish/conflicting-blocks-potential-default/conflict-not-block-when-config-off/neutral-market/insufficient-structure-no-setup/candle-only-not-potential/structure-only-not-potential/range-capped-at-watch/range-caps-off-allows-potential/watch-threshold/no-setup-none/no-setup-none-context; future-leakage prefix==full/future-mutation-unchanged/uses-only-patterns-at-index; pipeline integration attached/disabled-none/signals-unchanged/per-point-state-unchanged/signal-prices-unchanged/pipeline==standalone-prefix/classification-distribution; reporting returns-str/sections/warning/no-predictive-language/sequence/sequence-warning-once/determinism; determinism+immutability repeated/supporting-conflicting-subsets/score==supporting-count/frozen-slots/index-timestamp).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + a pipeline integration, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineEvaluationPoint + additive PipelineConfig fields). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. Setup assessment is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never predictive, never a trade signal, never implies live-trading readiness; POTENTIAL_SETUP is a coherent candidate, not a profitability/probability claim. No Sprint 11A-11P regression.

## Sprint 11R ÔÇö Trade Candidate Generation (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11Q setup/confluence): a structured, descriptive TRADE CANDIDATE derived from the existing candle-pattern evidence (Sprint 11O), market-context evidence (Sprint 11P) and setup/confluence assessment (Sprint 11Q). Sprint 11R does NOT build trade validation, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, ML, LLM, or live trading. The output of Sprint 11R is a TRADE CANDIDATE, not a trade. It is the third step of the separated concern pipeline: (1) MARKET OBSERVATION (11O/11P) -> (2) SETUP IDENTIFICATION (11Q) -> (3) TRADE CANDIDATE GENERATION (11R) -> (4) TRADE VALIDATION (future) -> (5) SIGNAL/EXECUTION (future). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11Q public API signature was changed, and all 1092 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): SetupAssessment (Sprint 11Q classification + confluence_score + structured EvidenceItem supporting/conflicting views) and MarketContext (Sprint 11P support_resistance levels + range state + trend). Sprint 11R adds the candidate promotion gate + entry/stop/target derivation + risk/reward computation + conservative setup-type classification on top.
- New models `src/engine/models/trade_candidate.py` (frozen+slots): enums CandidateDirection (LONG/SHORT/NONE ÔÇö deliberately separate from SignalDirection since a candidate is not yet a signal), CandidateStatus (NO_CANDIDATE/WATCH/CANDIDATE), SetupType (TREND_CONTINUATION/BREAKOUT/STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION/RANGE_REJECTION/SETUP_CANDIDATE ÔÇö only the justifiable types are populated; SETUP_CANDIDATE is the conservative generic fallback, RANGE_REJECTION reserved for a future explicit range-setup policy); model TradeCandidate (timestamp, evaluation_index, direction, status, setup_type, setup_classification [reused 11Q SetupClassification], entry_reference, stop_reference, target_reference, risk_distance, reward_distance, risk_reward_ratio, confluence_score, supporting_evidence/conflicting_evidence [reused 11Q EvidenceItem tuples], candle_evidence/market_trend/market_structure/location/range_context [reused 11Q labels], reason; properties is_candidate, geometry_complete). `__post_init__` validates internal consistency: a CANDIDATE must carry a directional intent (not NONE); directional geometry ordering (LONG requires stop < entry < target, SHORT the mirror); risk/reward consistency with entry/stop/target and direction; positive risk/reward when populated; ratio == reward/risk. The engine never produces inconsistent states; the check guards against hand-construction bugs. Optional fields use None so "unavailable"/"invalid" is never silently a real value.
- New config `src/engine/config/trade_candidate_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): TradeCandidateConfig ÔÇö min_confluence_for_candidate=3 (mirrors 11Q min_supporting_for_potential_setup default so the candidate layer does not silently weaken the setup layer's evidence bar), allow_range_setups=False (conservative ÔÇö a range is not treated as a directional trade setup by default; when True a POTENTIAL_SETUP that is IN_RANGE may still be promoted), min_risk_reward_ratio=None (optional gate; a candidate whose geometry is complete but whose R:R falls below the threshold is downgraded to WATCH; incomplete geometry is NOT rejected by this gate ÔÇö reported honestly instead).
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/trade_candidates.py`: TradeCandidateEngine(config).generate(assessment, market_context, index, timestamp, close_price) -> TradeCandidate. Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe (reads ONLY the already-computed SetupAssessment + MarketContext, both derived from candles[:T+1], plus the trigger close price as a scalar ÔÇö it inspects no candles directly). intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.trade_candidates import TradeCandidateEngine`.
  - DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö reuse, do not re-invent: NO separate scoring system. The candidate layer reuses the 11Q SetupClassification + confluence_score verbatim plus the structured EvidenceItem supporting/conflicting views.
  - STATUS MAPPING (reuses 11Q classification): NO_SETUP -> NO_CANDIDATE (direction mapped from setup direction, likely NONE, no geometry); WATCH -> WATCH (direction mapped from setup direction, NO geometry ÔÇö a watch is not a trade candidate); POTENTIAL_SETUP -> apply the conservative promotion gate.
  - PROMOTION GATE (POTENTIAL_SETUP is NOT automatically a CANDIDATE): requires (a) directional bias LONG/SHORT (not NEUTRAL/UNKNOWN -> WATCH), (b) confluence_score >= min_confluence_for_candidate (else WATCH), (c) NO disqualifying conflicting evidence (len(conflicting)>0 -> WATCH ÔÇö no automatic candidate), (d) not range-blocked when allow_range_setups=False (IN_RANGE -> WATCH). Only when ALL pass -> CANDIDATE.
  - ENTRY/STOP/TARGET METHODOLOGY (objective, point-in-time, no future candles): entry = trigger candle close (close_price scalar); LONG stop = nearest support (sr.support) if support < entry else None (below a recent confirmed swing low); SHORT stop = nearest resistance (sr.resistance) if resistance > entry else None (above a recent confirmed swing high); LONG target = next resistance (sr.resistance) if resistance > entry else None (opposing structural level); SHORT target = next support (sr.support) if support < entry else None. If a level is absent or on the wrong side of the entry, the reference is explicitly None (unavailable) rather than invented.
  - RISK/REWARD: LONG risk = entry - stop, reward = target - entry; SHORT risk = stop - entry, reward = entry - target. If entry/stop/target any None -> all None. If risk <= 0 or reward <= 0 (non-positive / wrong-side geometry) -> all None (rejected, never manufactured). ratio = reward/risk only when both > 0. geometry_complete property = entry/stop/target present AND risk>0 AND reward>0 AND ratio present.
  - SETUP TYPE (conservative, deterministic, evaluated in order): LONG + ABOVE_RESISTANCE -> BREAKOUT; LONG + NEAR_SUPPORT + trend aligned -> TREND_CONTINUATION; LONG + NEAR_SUPPORT + trend not aligned -> STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION; else SETUP_CANDIDATE. SHORT mirror (BELOW_SUPPORT -> BREAKOUT; NEAR_RESISTANCE + trend aligned -> TREND_CONTINUATION; NEAR_RESISTANCE + trend not aligned -> STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION; else SETUP_CANDIDATE). Non-candidate points -> SETUP_CANDIDATE (generic). The system never pretends to distinguish a setup type it cannot reliably determine from the 11O-11Q evidence.
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): PipelineConfig EXTENDED with enable_trade_candidates: bool = True + trade_candidate_config. HistoricalEvaluationPipeline constructs a TradeCandidateEngine (only when enabled) and at each evaluation index T computes trade_candidate_at_t = generate(setup_assessment_at_t, market_context_at_t, t, timestamp, close=visible[-1].close), attaching it to PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_candidate. The trade candidate is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated/eligible_decisions/signals_validated/completed_trades + per-point signal_state/decision_direction/decision_status/suppressed + per-signal entry/stop/target prices identical enable vs disable; pipeline-point trade_candidate == standalone generate(prefix)). Disabling (enable_trade_candidates=False) reproduces the exact pre-11R pipeline (trade_candidate=None).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field: PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_candidate: TradeCandidate | None = None (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). PipelineResult unchanged.
- `src/engine/reporting/trade_candidates.py`: TradeCandidateFormatter().format(candidate) -> str / .format_sequence(candidates) -> str. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Trade Candidate, Index, Timestamp, Direction, Status, Setup, Setup Class, Entry, Stop, Target, Risk, Reward, Risk/Reward, Confluence, Supporting Evidence, Conflicting Evidence, Candle Evidence, Market Trend, Market Structure, Location, Range Context, Reason. Every report ends with the explicit warning "Trade candidates are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and are NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability." (once per single format; once at end for a sequence). Unavailable refs shown as "unavailable". No predictive language. Following 11O/11P/11Q reporting convention, TradeCandidateFormatter is imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching SetupAssessmentFormatter/MarketContextFormatter).
- scripts/test_trade_candidates.py: demo ÔÇö strong bullish setup -> LONG CANDIDATE, strong bearish setup -> SHORT CANDIDATE, conflicting evidence -> WATCH (no automatic candidate), insufficient structure/context -> NO_CANDIDATE, invalid stop/target -> safely incomplete (no fabricated R:R), point-in-time leakage test (PASS: prefix==full at T), future candle mutation test (PASS: candidate(T) unchanged; PASS: stop/target/entry/direction/setup unchanged after mutation), existing pipeline behaviour before/after (signals/trades unchanged, PASS; candidate attached; status distribution; sample pipeline CANDIDATE report). Explicitly states candidates are NOT predictions. Demo exits successfully.
- tests/test_trade_candidates.py: 72 tests (model validation ÔÇö enum members/frozen+slots/is_candidate/geometry_complete valid LONG/SHORT/no-candidate/candidate-must-have-direction/long-stop-below-entry/short-stop-above-entry/long-target-above-entry/invalid-risk-rejected/invalid-reward-rejected/invalid-rr-rejected/risk-inconsistent; config ÔÇö defaults/min-confluence-below-one-rejected/min-rr-non-positive-rejected/min-rr-positive-accepted/frozen; candidate generation ÔÇö no-assessment-no-candidate/bullish-potential->LONG-candidate/bearish-potential->SHORT-candidate/no-setup->NO_CANDIDATE/watch->WATCH-no-geometry/conflicting-no-automatic-candidate/insufficient-confluence->watch/neutral-direction->watch/range-conservatively-handled/range-allowed-when-configured/missing-entry-when-close-none/missing-stop-when-no-support/missing-target-when-no-resistance/no-market-context-incomplete/min-rr-gate-downgrades-poor-ratio/min-rr-gate-does-not-reject-incomplete/evidence-preserved; setup type ÔÇö long-above-resistance-BREAKOUT/long-near-support-trend-aligned-TREND_CONTINUATION/long-near-support-trend-not-aligned-STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION/long-other-generic/short-below-support-BREAKOUT/short-near-resistance-TREND_CONTINUATION/non-candidate-generic; risk/reward ÔÇö long/short/long-stop-wrong-side-invalidates/short-stop-wrong-side-invalidates/long-target-wrong-side-invalidates; point-in-time ÔÇö prefix==full/future-mutation-unchanged/geometry-direction-setup-stable/uses-only-assessment-and-context-at-index; pipeline integration ÔÇö candidate-attached/disabled-none/signals-unchanged/per-point-state-unchanged/signal-prices-unchanged/pipeline==standalone-prefix/status-distribution-has-candidates/no-future-leak; reporting ÔÇö returns-str/required-sections/warning/no-predictive-language/unavailable-shown/sequence/determinism; determinism+immutability ÔÇö same-input-same-output/frozen/repeated-pipeline-runs/supporting-evidence-is-subset-of-assessment).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + a pipeline integration, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineEvaluationPoint + additive PipelineConfig fields). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. A trade candidate is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. Entry/stop/target derived ONLY from structural levels available at the evaluation point (no future candles); unavailable refs reported as None explicitly rather than invented; non-positive risk/reward rejected (None) rather than manufactured. No Sprint 11A-11Q regression.

## Sprint 11S ÔÇö Trade Candidate Ranking & Decision Intelligence (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11R trade candidate generation): a deterministic, descriptive DECISION over one or more trade candidates that ranks them by technical-evidence strength/completeness and identifies the strongest (PREFERRED) candidate. Sprint 11S does NOT build trade validation, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, probability of success, ML, LLM, broker integration, order execution, live trading, or portfolio management. The output of Sprint 11S is a DECISION/RANKING, not a trade. It is the fourth step of the separated concern pipeline: (1) MARKET OBSERVATION (11O/11P) -> (2) SETUP IDENTIFICATION (11Q) -> (3) TRADE CANDIDATE GENERATION (11R) -> (4) CANDIDATE RANKING/DECISION (11S) -> (5) TRADE VALIDATION (future) -> (6) SIGNAL/EXECUTION (future). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11R public API signature was changed, and all 1164 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11R TradeCandidate (direction, status, setup_type, confluence_score, supporting_evidence/conflicting_evidence, entry/stop/target/risk/reward/risk_reward_ratio, geometry_complete, candle/market_trend/market_structure/location/range_context labels). Sprint 11S adds the decision-scoring + classification + deterministic ranking layer on top. It does NOT recompute candle patterns, market context, setups or candidates.
- Decision classification is DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from Sprint 11R's CandidateStatus (NO_CANDIDATE/WATCH/CANDIDATE): DecisionClassification = REJECTED / WATCH / QUALIFIED / PREFERRED. 11R describes candidate *generation*; 11S describes candidate *decision/ranking*. The two are related but not identical: a generated CANDIDATE may, after ranking, be classified REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED.
- New models `src/engine/models/trade_decision.py` (frozen+slots): `DecisionClassification` (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED; `rank_value` property for deterministic ordering), `DecisionScoreComponent` (name, points, max_points, reason ÔÇö fully auditable), `DecisionScore` (total int [0,100], max_total, components tuple, reason ÔÇö the "Decision Score" = deterministic technical-evidence strength/completeness, NOT a probability), `TradeDecision` (timestamp, evaluation_index, candidate ref-by-reference, direction, classification, score, geometry_complete, confluence_score, supporting_count, conflicting_count, risk_reward_ratio, rationale; properties decision_score/is_preferred), `RankedDecision` (1-based rank + decision), `TradeDecisionRanking` (timestamp, evaluation_index, decisions tuple ordered strongest-first, candidate_count, preferred RankedDecision|None, rationale; properties has_preferred/is_empty).
- New config `src/engine/config/trade_decision_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `DecisionWeights` (trend=15/structure=15/candle=10/location=10/geometry=20/risk_reward=15/no_conflict=15, sum=100; non-negative, positive total) and `TradeDecisionConfig` (weights, preferred_threshold=80, qualified_threshold=60, watch_threshold=40 with ordering watch<=qualified<=preferred and bounds [0,max]; conflict_max_classification=QUALIFIED default; watch_status_max_classification=QUALIFIED default; require_geometry_for_preferred=True; neutral_fraction=0.5 in [0,1]; good_risk_reward_ratio=2.0, min_risk_reward_ratio=1.0 with min<=good and both positive). No magic numbers in the engine.
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/trade_decision.py`: `TradeDecisionEngine(config).decide(candidate, index, timestamp) -> TradeDecision` and `.rank(candidates, index, timestamp) -> TradeDecisionRanking`. Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe (reads ONLY the already-computed TradeCandidate, itself derived from candles[:T+1]; inspects no candles directly). intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.trade_decision import TradeDecisionEngine`.
  - DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö descriptive evidence strength, NOT probability: the Decision Score is an integer [0,100] = transparent sum of named components (trend/structure/candle/location/geometry/risk_reward/no_conflict). It is NEVER a probability of success or profitability prediction.
  - DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö reuse, do not re-invent: reconstructs each evidence source's alignment relative to the candidate direction from the 11R candidate's supporting/conflicting EvidenceItem subsets plus per-source labels (ALIGNED if in supporting; CONFLICTING if in conflicting; ABSENT if label is the absence sentinel e.g. "UNKNOWN"/"none"; else NEUTRAL). NO 11R model modified.
  - SCORING: each directional source contributes a share of its weight (ALIGNED=full; NEUTRAL=neutral_fraction*weight; CONFLICTING/ABSENT=0). Geometry: complete=full; entry-only (no stop/target)=neutral_fraction share; none=0. Risk/reward: ratio>=good=full; >=min=half; <min or absent=0. No-conflict: 0 conflicts=full; else 0.
  - CLASSIFICATION: base from score thresholds, then CAPS (each can only reduce): (a) no directional bias (NONE) / NO_CANDIDATE status / no entry -> REJECTED; (b) 11R WATCH status -> capped at watch_status_max_classification; (c) conflicting evidence -> capped at conflict_max_classification (so conflict can never automatically produce PREFERRED by default); (d) require_geometry_for_preferred && incomplete geometry -> capped at QUALIFIED. PREFERRED requires score>=preferred_threshold AND no disqualifying cap.
  - RANKING (multiple candidates, core requirement): deterministic sort by _ranking_key (strongest-first): (1) classification rank desc, (2) decision score desc, (3) confluence score desc, (4) geometry-complete first, (5) risk/reward ratio desc (absent last), (6) fewer conflicting sources, (7) direction order LONG<SHORT<NONE, (8) evaluation index asc, (9) entry reference asc. Ranks are 1-based and UNIQUE (no two share a rank). preferred = rank-1 IFF PREFERRED ÔÇö a winner is NEVER manufactured (None when none PREFERRED). No random behaviour; identical inputs always produce identical rankings.
- New reporting `src/engine/reporting/trade_decision.py`: `TradeDecisionFormatter().format(decision) -> str` / `.format_ranking(ranking) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Single format sections: Trade Decision, Index, Timestamp, Candidate Status, Direction, Decision, Decision Score, Geometry Complete, Confluence, Supporting/Conflicting Evidence, Entry/Stop/Target, Risk/Reward, Score Components (auditable per-component breakdown), Rationale. Ranking format: ranking table (Rank/Direction/Decision/Score) + preferred summary + per-decision detail + rationale. Every report ends with the explicit warning "Trade decisions are descriptive technical-evidence outputs and are NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability." No probability/guaranteed-profit/will-win language. Following 11O-11R reporting convention, TradeDecisionFormatter is imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching TradeCandidateFormatter/SetupAssessmentFormatter/MarketContextFormatter).
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): PipelineConfig EXTENDED with enable_trade_decision: bool = True + trade_decision_config. HistoricalEvaluationPipeline constructs a TradeDecisionEngine (only when enabled) and at each evaluation index T computes trade_decision_at_t = decide(trade_candidate_at_t) (only when a trade candidate exists), attaching it to PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_decision. The trade decision is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated/eligible_decisions/signals_validated/completed_trades + per-point signal_state/decision_direction/decision_status/suppressed + per-signal entry/stop/target prices identical enable vs disable; pipeline-point trade_decision == standalone decide(prefix)). Disabling (enable_trade_decision=False) reproduces the exact pre-11S pipeline (trade_decision=None).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field: PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_decision: TradeDecision | None = None (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). PipelineResult unchanged.
- scripts/test_trade_decision.py: demo ÔÇö strong candidate -> PREFERRED (score 100), weak candidate -> WATCH (score 51), conflicting evidence -> QUALIFIED not PREFERRED (score 75, conflict recorded), incomplete geometry -> safely represented (no fabricated risk/reward), multiple candidates -> deterministic ranking with preferred identified, point-in-time leakage proof (PASS: prefix==full at T), future mutation proof (PASS: decision(T) unchanged; PASS: direction/score/classification/geometry stable), existing pipeline regression (signals=4 trades=3 before==after, PASS; decision attached; classification distribution; sample pipeline decision report). Explicitly states decisions are NOT predictions/probabilities. Demo exits successfully.
- tests/test_trade_decision.py: 87 tests (model validation ÔÇö enum members/rank_value ordering/frozen+slots/decision-score bounds/score-component bounds/ranking properties/is_preferred; configuration ÔÇö defaults/weights-sum-100/weights-frozen+validated/threshold-ordering/threshold-bounds/neutral-fraction-bounds/risk-reward-band-ordering/cap-classifications-validated; scoring ÔÇö strong-max-score/aligned-full-weight/neutral-partial/absent-zero/conflicting-zero+no-conflict-zero/geometry-complete-full/geometry-partial-entry-only/geometry-none-zero/risk-reward-good-full/risk-reward-acceptable-partial/risk-reward-poor-zero/risk-reward-absent-zero/score-within-bounds/neutral-fraction-config; classification ÔÇö strong-preferred/weak-watch-or-rejected/no-direction-rejected/no-candidate-status-rejected/no-entry-rejected/watch-status-capped/score-threshold-qual; conflict ÔÇö conflicting-not-preferred/conflict-reduces-score/conflict-cap-configurable/conflict-recorded-in-count; geometry ÔÇö incomplete-honest/incomplete-caps-preferred/geometry-not-required-when-configured/partial-credit; risk-reward ÔÇö good-full/acceptable-partial/poor-zero/configurable-bands; ranking ÔÇö deterministic-order/preferred-identified/no-preferred-when-none-strong/empty/single-rank1/example-shape; tie-breaking ÔÇö identical-scores-secondary-keys/confluence/geometry/no-random/repeated-calls; point-in-time ÔÇö prefix==full/future-mutation/several-future-candles/geometry-direction-score-stable/uses-only-candidate-at-index; pipeline integration ÔÇö decision-attached/disabled-none/signals-unchanged/regression-signals-4-trades-3/per-point-state-unchanged/signal-prices-unchanged/pipeline==standalone-prefix/no-future-leak/classification-distribution; reporting ÔÇö returns-str/sections/warning/no-probability-language/ranking-table/ranking-empty/determinism/unavailable-shown; determinism+immutability ÔÇö same-input/repeated-pipeline-runs/frozen-models/ranking-frozen/supporting-evidence-subset).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + a pipeline integration, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineEvaluationPoint + additive PipelineConfig fields). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. A trade decision is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. The Decision Score is deterministic technical-evidence strength/completeness, NOT a probability. Conflicting evidence matters (caps PREFERRED by default). Incomplete geometry represented honestly (never fabricated); a winner is never manufactured without PREFERRED evidence. No Sprint 11A-11R regression.

## Sprint 11T ÔÇö Trade Opportunity Filtering & Ranking (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11S trade candidate ranking/decision): a deterministic, descriptive TRADE OPPORTUNITY filter/ranker that answers "Given all trade candidates available at a point in time, which opportunities are actually worth surfacing?" Sprint 11T does NOT build trade validation, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, probability of success, ML, LLM, broker integration, order execution, live trading, or portfolio management. The output of Sprint 11T is a TRADE OPPORTUNITY view, not a trade. It is the fifth step of the separated concern pipeline: (1) MARKET OBSERVATION (11O/11P) -> (2) SETUP IDENTIFICATION (11Q) -> (3) TRADE CANDIDATE GENERATION (11R) -> (4) CANDIDATE RANKING/DECISION (11S) -> (5) TRADE OPPORTUNITY FILTER (11T) -> (6) TRADE VALIDATION (future) -> (7) SIGNAL/EXECUTION (future). All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11S public API signature was changed, and all 1251 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11S `TradeDecision` (classification, decision_score, geometry_complete, confluence_score, supporting_count, conflicting_count, risk_reward_ratio, candidate ref). Sprint 11T adds the opportunity-eligibility + ranking + status layer on top. It does NOT recompute candidates, decisions, scores, classifications, geometry or risk/reward. The `TradeDecision` is retained BY REFERENCE.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö DISTINCT from Sprint 11S: 11S answers "How strong is this candidate?" (per-candidate decision classification + score). 11T answers "Should this candidate actually be surfaced as the best available trade opportunity at this evaluation point?" (eligibility filter + relative ranking + opportunity status). `OpportunityStatus` (NO_OPPORTUNITY/WATCH/ALTERNATIVE_OPPORTUNITY/BEST_OPPORTUNITY) is DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from `DecisionClassification` (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED).
- New models `src/engine/models/opportunity.py` (frozen+slots): enums `OpportunityStatus` (NO_OPPORTUNITY/WATCH/ALTERNATIVE_OPPORTUNITY/BEST_OPPORTUNITY; `rank_value` property), `EligibilityStatus` (ELIGIBLE/INELIGIBLE); models `EligibilityReason` (gate, passed, reason ÔÇö auditable per-gate), `TradeOpportunity` (timestamp, evaluation_index, decision ref-by-reference, direction, rank [1-based among eligible, 0 for ineligible], status, eligibility, eligibility_reasons tuple, decision_classification, decision_score, geometry_complete, confluence_score, supporting_count, conflicting_count, risk_reward_ratio, rejection_reason, ranking_reason; properties is_eligible/is_best/is_surfaced; `__post_init__` invariants: best=rank1, alternative=rank2+, ineligible=rank0, eligible=nonzero rank, NO_OPPORTUNITY implies ineligible), `TradeOpportunityRanking` (timestamp, evaluation_index, opportunities tuple ordered strongest-first, candidate_count, eligible_count, best|None, rationale; properties has_best/is_empty/surfaced).
- New config `src/engine/config/trade_opportunity_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `RankingPriority` enum (ELIGIBILITY/DECISION_CLASSIFICATION/DECISION_SCORE/GEOMETRY_COMPLETENESS/RISK_REWARD/CONFLUENCE/CONFLICT_FREE/SETUP_STRENGTH/DETERMINISTIC_TIEBREAK) + `RANKING_PRIORITY_ORDER` tuple (canonical order, documented). `TradeOpportunityConfig`: allowed_candidate_statuses (default excludes NO_CANDIDATE), allowed_decision_classifications (default excludes REJECTED), min_decision_score=40, require_geometry=False (eligibility gate), min_risk_reward_ratio=None (gate applied only when ratio available; missing NOT rejected), disqualify_on_conflict=False, require_no_conflict_for_best=True, require_geometry_for_best=True, max_surfaced_opportunities=None, min_confluence_for_best=0, ranking_order. Validation: non-negative score, positive R:R when set, non-negative confluence, max_surfaced>=1, non-empty allowed sets.
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/trade_opportunity.py`: `TradeOpportunityEngine(config).evaluate(decision, index, timestamp) -> TradeOpportunity` (standalone eligibility + provisional status) and `.rank(decisions, index, timestamp) -> TradeOpportunityRanking`. Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe (reads ONLY the already-computed TradeDecision, itself derived from candles[:T+1]; inspects no candles directly). intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.trade_opportunity import TradeOpportunityEngine`.
  - ELIGIBILITY: a candidate must pass ALL active gates (candidate_status, decision_classification, decision_score, optional geometry, optional risk_reward [missing NOT rejected], optional conflict disqualification). Each gate recorded as an `EligibilityReason`. NO_CANDIDATE never eligible; REJECTED never eligible.
  - RANKING (deterministic, strongest-first, direction-symmetric): `_ranking_key` = (decision classification strength desc, decision score desc, geometry complete first, risk/reward desc [absent last], confluence desc, fewer conflicts, more supporting, direction order LONG<SHORT<NONE [final tie-break only], evaluation index asc, entry asc). No randomness; direction only matters through evidence until the final tie-break.
  - BEST-OPPORTUNITY CAPS: the strongest eligible candidate is BEST ONLY when it clears `require_no_conflict_for_best`, `require_geometry_for_best`, `min_confluence_for_best`. If the strongest eligible fails a best cap, NO best is surfaced (`best=None`) ÔÇö a weaker candidate is NEVER promoted to best over a stronger one that was merely capped (no silent substitution).
  - ALTERNATIVE eligibility uses the SAME caps: an alternative must itself be best-quality (clean, complete geometry, sufficient confluence); a conflicting/incomplete-geometry/low-confluence eligible candidate is a WATCH opportunity, never an alternative. An alternative is surfaced ONLY when a strictly stronger best exists; never manufactured when only one eligible opportunity exists.
  - STATUS ASSIGNMENT (in eligible rank order): rank-1 = BEST (if best cleared caps); ranks 2..min(cap,Ôł×) = ALTERNATIVE; remainder eligible = WATCH; ineligible = NO_OPPORTUNITY (rank 0, last). `max_surfaced_opportunities` counts best as 1 surfaced.
  - LONG/SHORT SYMMETRY: direction never biases ranking; a SHORT never outranks a LONG (or vice versa) on direction alone ÔÇö only through evidence/scores; the final tie-breaker orders LONG before SHORT only as a deterministic last resort when ALL evidence keys are identical.
- New reporting `src/engine/reporting/trade_opportunity.py`: `TradeOpportunityFormatter().format(opportunity) -> str` / `.format_ranking(ranking) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Trade Opportunity, Index, Timestamp, Rank, Direction, Status, Decision, Score, Entry, Stop, Target, Risk/Reward, Geometry, Confluence, Supporting Evidence, Conflicting Evidence, Eligibility, (Rejection Reason), Ranking Reason. Every report ends with the explicit warning "Trade opportunities are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and are NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability." Unavailable refs shown as "unavailable"; incomplete geometry shown as "INCOMPLETE". No predictive claims (no "most profitable"/"highest probability"/"will rise"/"will fall"). Following 11O-11S reporting convention, TradeOpportunityFormatter is imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching TradeDecisionFormatter/TradeCandidateFormatter/SetupAssessmentFormatter/MarketContextFormatter).
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): PipelineConfig EXTENDED with enable_trade_opportunity: bool = True + trade_opportunity_config. HistoricalEvaluationPipeline constructs a TradeOpportunityEngine (only when enabled) and at each evaluation index T computes trade_opportunity_at_t = evaluate(trade_decision_at_t) (only when a trade decision exists), attaching it to PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_opportunity. The trade opportunity is NOT fed into the confluence/decision/signal engines, so existing signal/decision/validation behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated/eligible_decisions/signals_validated/completed_trades + per-point signal_state/decision_direction/decision_status/suppressed + per-signal entry/stop/target prices identical enable vs disable; pipeline-point trade_opportunity == standalone evaluate(prefix)). Disabling (enable_trade_opportunity=False) reproduces the exact pre-11T pipeline (trade_opportunity=None).
- `src/engine/models/pipeline.py` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field: PipelineEvaluationPoint.trade_opportunity: TradeOpportunity | None = None (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). PipelineResult unchanged.
- scripts/test_trade_opportunity.py: demo ÔÇö 8 deterministic scenarios (strong single -> BEST; multiple -> one outranks; LONG vs SHORT symmetric; incomplete geometry -> WATCH; conflicting -> WATCH; no candidate -> NO_OPPORTUNITY; poor R:R handled honestly default+gated; tie-breaking direction-symmetric), point-in-time leakage proof (PASS: prefix==full at T), future mutation proof (PASS: opportunity(T) unchanged; PASS: status/rank/direction/eligibility stable), existing pipeline regression (signals=4 trades=3 before==after, PASS; opportunity attached; status distribution {NO_OPPORTUNITY, WATCH}; sample pipeline opportunity report). Explicitly states opportunities are NOT predictions. Demo exits successfully.
- tests/test_trade_opportunity.py: 88 tests (model validation ÔÇö enum members/rank_value ordering/frozen+slots/best-must-be-rank1/alternative-must-be-rank2+/ineligible-must-be-rank0/eligible-must-have-nonzero-rank/negative-rank-rejected/properties/ranking-surfaced; configuration ÔÇö defaults/ranking-priority-order-canonical/frozen/min-score-non-negative/rr-positive-when-set/confluence-non-negative/max-surfaced>=1/non-empty-allowed-sets; eligibility ÔÇö strong-eligible/no-candidate-ineligible/rejected-ineligible/below-min-score-ineligible/require-geometry-gate/min-rr-gate-applied-when-available/min-rr-gate-skipped-when-unavailable/disqualify-on-conflict/auditable-reasons; filtering ÔÇö no-candidate-never-opportunity/rejected-never-opportunity/poor-rr-filtered-when-gated/conflict-disqualified-when-configured; ranking ÔÇö single-strong-best/multiple-one-outranks/no-best-when-strongest-fails-cap/alternative-never-manufactured-single/surfaced-cap/surfaced-cap-one/ranks-unique/order-best-alt-watch-ineligible/empty; tie-breaking ÔÇö identical-scores-direction-tiebreak/identical-inputs-repeated/no-randomness/score-breaks-tie-before-direction; LONG/SHORT symmetry ÔÇö direction-only-through-evidence/short-can-outrank-long/no-long-bias-when-short-stronger; geometry+R:R ÔÇö incomplete-never-best-default/incomplete-never-alternative/missing-rr-not-fabricated/complete-geometry-best/require-geometry-cap-toggle; conflict ÔÇö conflict-never-best-default/conflict-never-alternative/conflict-recorded-in-reason/no-conflict-best-allowed; candidate counts ÔÇö zero/single/multiple; point-in-time ÔÇö prefix==full/future-mutation/several-future-candles/status-rank-direction-stable/uses-only-candidate-at-index; pipeline integration ÔÇö opportunity-attached/disabled-none/signals-unchanged/regression-signals-4-trades-3/per-point-state-unchanged/signal-prices-unchanged/pipeline==standalone-prefix/no-future-leak/classification-distribution; reporting ÔÇö returns-str/required-sections/warning/no-predictive-language/ranking-table/empty/unavailable-shown/determinism; determinism+immutability ÔÇö same-inputs/repeated-pipeline-runs/frozen-models/slots/decision-retained-by-reference).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + a pipeline integration, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineEvaluationPoint + additive PipelineConfig fields). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. A trade opportunity is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. LONG/SHORT treated symmetrically (direction only through evidence + final tie-break). Incomplete geometry + missing R:R represented honestly (never fabricated); a best opportunity is never manufactured without an eligible candidate clearing the best caps; an alternative is never manufactured without a strictly stronger best. No Sprint 11A-11S regression.

## Sprint 11U ÔÇö Multi-Timeframe Trade Opportunity Research & Market Scanner (added)
This sprint builds the next TRADING-INTELLIGENCE layer (after Sprint 11T trade opportunity filtering): a deterministic, descriptive MARKET OPPORTUNITY SCANNER that accepts a collection of instrument / timeframe datasets and produces a ranked set of trade opportunities ACROSS instruments and timeframes. It is the multi-timeframe market-research / scanning milestone ÔÇö the first move from single-series opportunity evaluation toward multi-timeframe + multi-instrument market research. Sprint 11U does NOT build trade validation, BUY/SELL signals, profitability prediction, ML, LLM, broker integration, order execution, live trading, portfolio management, or an arbitrary timeframe graph. The output of Sprint 11U is a RANKED MARKET SCAN, not a trade. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11T public API signature was changed, and all 1339 prior tests still pass.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11P `MarketContext` (higher-timeframe context + lower-timeframe context), the Sprint 11S `TradeDecision` (lower / execution-timeframe decision) and the Sprint 11T `TradeOpportunity` (lower / execution-timeframe opportunity) ÔÇö all retained BY REFERENCE, never modified. The Sprint 11U scanner reuses the existing candle-pattern / market-context / setup-confluence / trade-candidate / trade-decision / trade-opportunity engines via a `ScanEngines` bundle; it implements NO new candidate / decision / opportunity / scoring logic.
- New models `src/engine/models/market_scan.py` (frozen+slots): enums `TimeframeRole` (CONTEXT_TIMEFRAME / SETUP_TIMEFRAME), `MTFAlignment` (ALIGNED / CONFLICTING / NEUTRAL / UNKNOWN ÔÇö DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from the Sprint 11Q `EvidenceAlignment`; RANGE / NEUTRAL higher context is never silently interpreted as bullish or bearish), `ScanStatus` (OPPORTUNITIES_FOUND / WATCH_ONLY / NO_OPPORTUNITY / INCOMPLETE ÔÇö missing data is NEVER a directional conclusion). Models: `InstrumentTimeframe` (instrument + timeframe + role identity), `TimeframeSlice` (point-in-time slice of one timeframe ÔÇö context or setup ÔÇö carrying the reused `MarketContext` / `TradeDecision` / `TradeOpportunity` by reference + a `ready` flag), `InstrumentScanResult` (the per-instrument MTF result combining higher + lower slices, alignment, `complete`, direction, decision classification, decision score, risk/reward, an EXPLICIT stored `eligible` flag that survives serialization, and a descriptive reason), `RankedScanOpportunity` (1-based market-level rank among eligible + the InstrumentScanResult; `__post_init__` invariants: eligible must carry 1-based rank, ineligible rank 0, no negative rank), `MarketScanResult` (scan_id, timestamp, instruments, timeframes, status, results, ranked, best, alternatives, rejected, rationale; properties has_best/is_empty/eligible_count).
- New config `src/engine/config/market_scan_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `ScanRankingPriority` enum + `SCAN_RANKING_PRIORITY_ORDER` tuple (canonical market-level ranking order, distinct from Sprint 11T's per-context ranking), `TimeframeRoleConfig`, `MarketScanConfig` (context_timeframe="1D", setup_timeframe="15M", min_history=10, require_context_timeframe=True, require_completed_context_candle=True [the core look-ahead protection], require_opportunity_for_eligibility=True, max_surfaced_opportunities=None, label, metadata). Validation: min_history>=0, max_surfaced>=1 when set, context/setup timeframes must differ. `_ALIGNMENT_STRENGTH` ordering: ALIGNED>NEUTRAL>CONFLICTING>UNKNOWN (missing evidence weakest, never treated as aligned).
- New engines (extend `src/engine/intelligence/`, intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via full paths):
  - mtf_alignment.py: `MTFAlignmentEngine().align(higher_context, lower_direction) -> MTFAlignment`. Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe (reads ONLY the already-computed `MarketContext` + a direction string; inspects no candles). Rules: None higher context -> UNKNOWN; UNKNOWN trend -> UNKNOWN; non-directional lower ("" / "NONE") -> UNKNOWN; RANGE / NEUTRAL observed context -> NEUTRAL (the higher context neither supports nor opposes the setup; NEVER silently bullish/bearish); BULLISH context + LONG -> ALIGNED, + SHORT -> CONFLICTING; BEARISH context + SHORT -> ALIGNED, + LONG -> CONFLICTING. Missing evidence is never fabricated.
  - market_scanner.py: `MarketScanner(config).scan(datasets, evaluation_time=None, engines=None) -> MarketScanResult` + `InstrumentDataset` (instrument + context_candles + setup_candles) + `ScanEngines` (a bundle of the reused Sprint 11O-11T engines + the Sprint 11U alignment engine; `ScanEngines.default()` builds a default bundle). Deterministic, pure, future-leakage safe. The scanner builds the higher-context slice (completed-HTF-candle only) + the lower-setup slice (latest completed setup candle) per instrument, computes alignment, and assembles a deterministic market-level ranked result.
- HIGHER-TIMEFRAME COMPLETION / LOOK-AHEAD POLICY (one of the most important 11U requirements, STRUCTURAL): for evaluation time T (the close of the latest completed setup-timeframe candle), the higher-timeframe context uses ONLY the latest higher-timeframe candle that CLOSED STRICTLY BEFORE T (`_latest_completed_before`, timestamp < cutoff). An in-progress higher-timeframe candle (timestamp >= T) is NEVER used, even if it exists in the dataset. The higher context is then built from context candles up to and including that completed HTF candle (a function of `context_candles[:htf_index+1]` only). Proven by tests: (1) a HTF candle AT the eval time is excluded (INCOMPLETE when no earlier HTF candle); (2) an earlier completed HTF candle is used even when a later in-progress one exists (alignment correct); (3) future HTF mutation leaves scan(T) unchanged; (4) future LTF mutation leaves scan(T) unchanged; (5) multi-instrument + multi-timeframe future mutation stable.
- MARKET-LEVEL RANKING POLICY (DISTINCT from Sprint 11T; 11T ranks WITHIN one evaluation context, 11U ranks ACROSS instruments/timeframes), applied in order, ties by the next key, finally a direction-symmetric deterministic tie-break; no randomness: (1) ELIGIBILITY ÔÇö ineligible/incomplete never rank as surfaced opportunities; (2) MTF_ALIGNMENT ÔÇö ALIGNED > NEUTRAL > CONFLICTING > UNKNOWN; (3) OPPORTUNITY_STATUS ÔÇö stronger Sprint 11T status first; (4) DECISION_CLASSIFICATION ÔÇö stronger Sprint 11S classification first; (5) DECISION_SCORE ÔÇö higher first; (6) GEOMETRY_COMPLETENESS ÔÇö complete first; (7) RISK_REWARD ÔÇö higher first (absent last; never fabricated); (8) CONFLUENCE ÔÇö higher first; (9) CONFLICT_FREE ÔÇö fewer conflicts first; (10) DETERMINISTIC_TIEBREAK ÔÇö instrument name asc, then direction order (LONG < SHORT < NONE), then setup timeframe asc. Direction only matters as a final, direction-symmetric last resort; it never makes a SHORT outrank a LONG (or vice versa) on evidence. No winner manufactured: `best=None` when no instrument is eligible; alternatives surfaced ONLY when a strictly stronger best exists (subject to `max_surfaced_opportunities` cap, best counts as 1 surfaced).
- Serialization `src/engine/intelligence/market_scan_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__datetime__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `SCANNER_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`, `serialize_scan`/`serialize_scan_bytes`/`deserialize_scan`/`parse_scan_header`/`canonical_scan_json`. Serialization scope (intentional, matching Sprint 11K discipline): the lightweight scan projections are persisted IN FULL (scan id, timestamp, instruments, timeframes, status, per-instrument InstrumentScanResult projection incl. the stored `eligible` flag); the heavy per-engine reference outputs (`higher_context`/`lower_context`/`decision`/`opportunity`) are NOT persisted and reconstruct as `None` (regenerable by rerunning the scan). Loader validates schema_version before model reconstruction; future versions rejected. The stored `eligible` flag preserves the eligibility verdict across serialization (so the `RankedScanOpportunity` invariant survives) ÔÇö this is why `eligible` is a stored field, not a derived property.
- Reporting `src/engine/reporting/market_scan.py`: `MarketScanFormatter().format(result) -> str` / `.format_instrument(result) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Renders the spec-requested Market Opportunity Scan report: header (scan id, evaluation time, instruments, timeframes, status), ranking table (Rank/Instrument/Direction/MTF/Decision/Score/R:R), Best Opportunity block (with wrapped reason), Alternative Opportunities, Rejected/Ineligible/Incomplete, Scan Rationale, and an explicit WARNING that market scan results are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability. No predictive language. Following 11O-11T reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching TradeOpportunityFormatter/TradeDecisionFormatter/etc.).
- Pipeline integration (ADDITIVE, behaviour-preserving): `PipelineResult` EXTENDED with one optional defaulted field `market_scan: object | None = None` (backward-compatible; existing test helpers construct with keyword args and never pass it). The pipeline itself does NOT run scanner logic (the scanner is a separate multi-instrument / multi-timeframe concern); the field lets a caller bundle a scan run alongside the single-series pipeline result. Disabling (market_scan=None) reproduces the exact pre-11U pipeline. Existing signal / trade behaviour is UNCHANGED (regression-tested: signals_generated / completed_trades identical; the demo confirms signals=4 trades=3 unchanged).
- `scripts/test_market_scanner.py`: demo ÔÇö 10 required scenarios (aligned timeframes, conflicting timeframes, multiple instruments, missing higher-timeframe data -> INCOMPLETE, no setup, multiple valid opportunities, deterministic market-level ranking, higher-timeframe candle completion / look-ahead protection, future candle mutation protection [HTF + LTF + multi-instrument], market scan with no opportunities) + point-in-time leakage proof (prefix == full series at T) + serialization round trip + existing pipeline regression (signals/trades unchanged). Prints the descriptive Market Opportunity Scan report. Explicitly states scans are NOT predictions. Demo exits successfully.
- `tests/test_market_scanner.py`: 80 tests (model validation ÔÇö enums/frozen+slots/defaults/invariants/immutable; configuration ÔÇö defaults/validation/ranking-priority-canonical/frozen; MTF alignment ÔÇö bullish+long aligned, bearish+short aligned, bullish+short conflicting, bearish+long conflicting, range/neutral neutral, unknown unknown, none context unknown, none lower unknown, range never silently bullish, pure function; timeframe safety ÔÇö latest completed HTF, incomplete HTF excluded, HTF-at-eval-time excluded, earlier completed HTF used, no HTF data incomplete, future HTF mutation unchanged, future LTF mutation unchanged, min_history incomplete; scanning ÔÇö one/multiple/multiple-opportunities-ranked/no-opportunity/watch-only/incomplete-input/scan-id-deterministic; ranking ÔÇö deterministic/aligned-vs-conflicting/score-ordering/geometry-complete-first/risk-reward-ordering/deterministic-instrument-tiebreak/long-short-symmetry-no-direction-bias; leakage ÔÇö full-vs-prefix/future-mutation/multi-instrument-future-mutation/multi-timeframe-future-mutation; pipeline ÔÇö signals-unchanged/completed-trades-unchanged/scanner-independent-of-pipeline; serialization ÔÇö round-trip/drops-heavy-outputs/deterministic-bytes/schema-version/future-schema-rejected/best-preserved; reporting ÔÇö returns-str/sections/warning/no-predictive-language/empty/determinism/format-instrument; determinism+immutability ÔÇö same-inputs/repeated/frozen/rerun-serialization-loop/different-configs-different-id; alignment edge cases ÔÇö range-neutral-never-bullish/unknown-context-unknown/pure-function; scan identity ÔÇö different-instruments-different-id/same-instruments-same-id-regardless-of-order/prefixed).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is intelligence + an optional pipeline-result attachment, not an architectural layer); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + one optional defaulted field on PipelineResult). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/arbitrary-timeframe-graph. A market scan result is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. Missing timeframe/context data represented honestly as INCOMPLETE (never a directional conclusion); conflicting timeframes represented honestly as CONFLICTING (never silently downgraded); RANGE/NEUTRAL higher context represented as NEUTRAL (never silently bullish/bearish). No future higher-timeframe candle leakage (completed-HTF-candle-only, structurally enforced); no future lower-timeframe leakage; multi-instrument + multi-timeframe mutation tests pass. A best opportunity is never manufactured without an eligible instrument. No Sprint 11A-11T regression.
## Sprint 11V ÔÇö Historical Market Data Integration & End-to-End Validation (added)
This sprint is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer. It is the integration + validation milestone that proves the EXISTING intelligence pipeline (Sprints 11A-11U) can consume realistic historical OHLCV data exactly as if it were seeing the market sequentially in real time, without using future information. The output is a reproducible, point-in-time-correct, analyst-style ranked market scan. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11U public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 1419 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: `realistic historical OHLCV -> normalized market data -> multi-instrument / multi-timeframe -> existing intelligence pipeline -> market scanner -> ranked analyst-style opportunities`, while proving NO FUTURE LEAKAGE, NO FABRICATED DATA, DETERMINISTIC REPLAY, DETERMINISTIC RANKING, HTF COMPLETION PROTECTION, MULTI-INSTRUMENT ISOLATION, SERIALIZATION CORRECTNESS and EXISTING PIPELINE REGRESSION.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): `OHLCVCandle` (canonical candle, whose `__post_init__` rejects impossible OHLC), `DataValidator` (dataset validation semantics), `MarketScanner` + `InstrumentDataset` + `ScanEngines` (the Sprint 11U scanner, which already implements point-in-time HTF/LTF truncation, MTF alignment, deterministic market-level ranking and serialization), and `MarketScanFormatter`. The replay layer implements NO candle, structure, setup, candidate, decision, opportunity, alignment or ranking logic ÔÇö it delegates to the scanner per evaluation timestamp.
- New models `src/engine/models/historical.py` (frozen+slots): enums `DataQuality` (VALID/INVALID/INCOMPLETE ÔÇö the explicit valid/invalid/incomplete distinction) and `HistoricalNormalizationError` (categorical issue reasons). Models `HistoricalRecord` (raw OHLCV record pending normalization, optional fields so partial records are reported not raised), `NormalizationIssue` (one auditable issue with severity/instrument/timeframe/error/reason), `TimeframeSeries` (one instrument's normalized candles for one timeframe + quality + candle_count; `__post_init__` validates non-empty instrument/timeframe), `HistoricalInstrumentData` (instrument -> {timeframe -> TimeframeSeries}; a requested-but-missing timeframe is an explicit INCOMPLETE series, never silently omitted), `HistoricalDataset` (multi-instrument / multi-timeframe normalized dataset carrying all VALID series + every INVALID/INCOMPLETE issue; properties instruments/valid_count/invalid_count/incomplete_count; context_candles/setup_candles accessors). No business logic in models.
- New data adapter `src/engine/data/historical_adapter.py`: `HistoricalAdapterConfig` (frozen+slots, validated: timeframes default `("1D","15M")`, min_history=10, dedupe=True; rejects empty/duplicate timeframes, negative min_history) + `HistoricalDataAdapter(config).normalize(records) -> HistoricalDataset`. Accepts `HistoricalRecord` objects OR equivalent mappings (vendor-independent, no network dependency). Normalization is deterministic: group by (instrument,timeframe); reject records missing instrument/timeframe/timestamp (INVALID issues); build canonical `OHLCVCandle` (rejects impossible OHLC / high<low / negative volume -> INVALID issues with categorized error); drop exact-timestamp duplicates as INVALID (never silently merged; when dedupe=False a duplicate raises ValueError matching strict DataValidator); sort chronologically; mark VALID when candles >= min_history else INCOMPLETE; emit an explicit INCOMPLETE series + EMPTY_SERIES/MISSING_TIMEFRAME issue for every requested timeframe that produced no records; final `DataValidator.validate_dataset` as a defensive guard. Invalid data is REJECTED per existing convention; incomplete data is represented EXPLICITLY ÔÇö never fabricated into a price/trend/structure/setup/stop/target/risk-reward.
- New deterministic fixtures `src/engine/data/historical_fixtures.py`: LOCAL + DETERMINISTIC multi-instrument / multi-timeframe OHLCV (NO network / vendor dependency). Instruments NIFTY/RELIANCE/TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK, each with a `1D` context series + `15M` setup series (bullish/bearish/flat/conflicting shapes). `historical_records()` -> flat `HistoricalRecord` batch; `historical_candles_by_instrument()` -> raw candles keyed by instrument then timeframe. Invalid/incomplete helpers (`invalid_high_below_low`, `invalid_missing_timestamp`, `invalid_negative_volume`, `invalid_open_outside_range`, `duplicate_timestamp_records`, `short_history_records`) for data-quality tests. Fixtures provide RAW OHLCV only ÔÇö NO encoded trends/structures/setups/stops/targets/rankings; all intelligence is produced by existing engines at replay time.
- New replay engine `src/engine/intelligence/historical_replay.py` (intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.historical_replay import HistoricalReplayEngine`): `HistoricalReplayEngine(config).replay(dataset, evaluation_times, engines=None) -> ReplayResult`. ORCHESTRATION ONLY ÔÇö at each evaluation time T it builds per-instrument `InstrumentDataset` objects from the historical dataset and delegates to `MarketScanner.scan(datasets, evaluation_time=T, engines=...)`. The scanner's `_latest_completed_before` (context, timestamp < T) and `_latest_completed_at_or_before` (setup, timestamp <= T) truncation guarantee NO future candle is read ÔÇö the replay preserves that guarantee by feeding the SAME candle tuples + the explicit evaluation_time. `ReplayEvaluationPoint` (evaluation_time + scan) and `ReplayResult` (replay_id = "replay-"+sha256[:16] of canonical identity [instruments/timeframes/eval-times/label/metadata], instruments, timeframes, evaluation_times, points, rationale; is_empty property). Deterministic: no wall-clock time, no unordered iteration for ranking. `evaluation_times_from_setup_candles(dataset, setup_timeframe, count, min_history)` helper builds a deterministic chronological sequence of SHARED setup timestamps across instruments (intersection), skipping warmup candles. Missing timeframe -> empty candle tuple -> scanner reports INCOMPLETE/UNKNOWN (never a directional conclusion). NO profitability/backtest metrics (no win rate / profit factor / Sharpe / PnL / expectancy / portfolio returns / trade outcome evaluation ÔÇö those belong to a later sprint).
- New reporting `src/engine/reporting/historical_replay.py`: `HistoricalReplayFormatter().format(result) -> str` (replay-level analyst report) / `.format_scan(scan) -> str` (single scan). Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Renders the analyst-style end-to-end report: header (replay id, instruments, timeframes, evaluation points), per-evaluation-point blocks each with per-instrument detail (Complete/Eligible/Direction/MTF Alignment; Higher Timeframe Context trend/range/structure/support-resistance; Setup Timeframe trend/range/structure/setup-type/decision/score/confluence/geometry/Entry-Stop-Target-R:R; Opportunity status; timestamp; reason), Best Opportunity block (instrument/direction/MTF/decision/score/R:R/geometry), Alternative Opportunities, Rejected/Ineligible/Incomplete, Scan Rationale, Replay Rationale, and an explicit WARNING that historical replay / market scan results are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability. No predictive language (no "will rise"/"will fall"/"guaranteed profit"/"most profitable"/"highest probability"). Following 11O-11U reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching MarketScanFormatter/TradeOpportunityFormatter/etc.). Reads only the EXISTING intelligence outputs retained by reference on the scan result (higher/lower `MarketContext`, `TradeDecision`, `TradeOpportunity`) ÔÇö no intelligence recomputed.
- POINT-IN-TIME CORRECTNESS (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): (A) prefix/full-series equality ÔÇö `scan(prefix through T) == scan(full series)` at T for alignment/direction/decision_score; (B) future mutation ÔÇö deeply mutating setup/HTF candles AFTER T leaves scan(T) unchanged (HTF, LTF, multi-instrument, multi-timeframe all tested); (C) replay correctness ÔÇö sequential replay at T produces the SAME result as a direct point-in-time evaluation at T (status + ranked instrument order), and sequential replay uses only candles <= T per point (verified against truncated-prefix scans). No component uses `candles > T` for candle evidence / swings / structure / trend / support-resistance / setup / candidate / decision / opportunity / ranking / MTF alignment.
- HTF CANDLE COMPLETION: an in-progress higher-timeframe candle (timestamp >= T) is NEVER used; the context uses only the latest HTF candle that CLOSED strictly before T. With no completed HTF candle before T the instrument is INCOMPLETE and its alignment is UNKNOWN ÔÇö never a bullish/bearish conclusion fabricated from absent data (tested: HTF-at-eval-time excluded; no-completed-HTF -> INCOMPLETE/UNKNOWN).
- MULTI-INSTRUMENT ISOLATION: scanning one instrument never produces another instrument's result; per-instrument candle/context/setup/candidate/decision/opportunity/ranking state is independent (tested).
- SERIALIZATION: the end-to-end historical scan round-trips through the EXISTING Sprint 11U `serialize_scan`/`deserialize_scan` (the replay reuses the scanner's serialization; no new serializer). Preserved across the round trip: scan_id, evaluation_time, instruments, timeframes, status, rank, direction, decision_classification, decision_score, MTF alignment, opportunity (via the stored `eligible` flag). The heavy per-engine reference outputs (higher/lower context, decision, opportunity) reconstruct as None (regenerable by rerunning) ÔÇö matching the 11U scope discipline. Deterministic bytes.
- `scripts/test_historical_replay.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 12 requirements (historical data loaded; multiple instruments processed; multiple timeframes processed; end-to-end scan generated; best opportunity identified when available; incomplete data handled honestly; HTF incomplete candle protection; prefix/full-series equality; future mutation protection [HTF+LTF+multi-instrument]; replay determinism + replay==direct point-in-time eval; serialization round trip; existing pipeline signals/trades regression baseline 4/3 unchanged). Prints the full analyst-style end-to-end report. Explicitly states results are NOT predictions. Ends with `Sprint 11V demo completed successfully.` Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_historical_replay.py`: 66 tests across 14 sections (A normalization ÔÇö valid/chronological/deterministic/timezones/identity/mappings; B invalid ÔÇö high<low/missing-timestamp/negative-volume/open-outside-range/duplicate-dropped/duplicate-strict-raises/missing-instrument/missing-timeframe/no-contamination; C incomplete ÔÇö empty/insufficient-history/missing-requested-timeframe/not-directional; D multiple instruments ÔÇö all-present/isolation/scanning-no-contamination; E multiple timeframes ÔÇö context+setup/extensible-to-1H-5M/mtf-alignment-reused; F HTF completion ÔÇö in-progress-excluded/no-completed->incomplete-UNKNOWN/incomplete-not-directional; G point-in-time ÔÇö prefix==full/future-setup-mutation/future-htf-mutation/multi-instrument-future-mutation/multi-timeframe-future-mutation; H replay ÔÇö matches-direct-point-in-time/deterministic/no-wall-clock/empty-times/sequential-information-availability; I deterministic ranking ÔÇö repeated/no-random/unique-ranks; J serialization ÔÇö round-trip-identity/ranking-direction-decision-score-preserved/best+opportunity-preserved/deterministic-bytes; K end-to-end ÔÇö full-pipeline/best-identified/incomplete-honest/replay-id-deterministic/watch->opportunities-progression; L regression ÔÇö signals-trades-unchanged/baseline-4-3/market_scan-field-optional; M reporting ÔÇö returns-str/required-sections/warning/no-predictive-language/determinism/format-scan/empty; N immutability+quality ÔÇö frozen-slots/frozen-series/requires-instrument+timeframe/config-validation/no-fabricated-data-on-missing). Small deterministic fixtures only; NO network dependency.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is data ingestion + replay orchestration + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence logic (every intelligence value is read from the reused Sprint 11A-11U engines); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; data/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. A historical replay / market scan result is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. Invalid data REJECTED (existing convention); incomplete data represented EXPLICITLY (never a directional conclusion, never fabricated). No future leakage (structurally enforced + regression-tested for setup/HTF/multi-instrument/multi-timeframe). Deterministic replay + deterministic ranking (no wall-clock, no unordered iteration). Serialization round-trips through the existing scanner serializer. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3). No Sprint 11A-11U regression.

## Sprint 11W ÔÇö Historical Opportunity Outcome Evaluation (added)
This sprint is the forward-only HISTORICAL OUTCOME EVALUATION layer. Sprint 11V answers "What opportunity did the existing intelligence pipeline identify at time T?"; Sprint 11W answers "What happened to that opportunity AFTER T?". The outcome evaluator inspects future candles ONLY AFTER the evaluation timestamp and NEVER allows future information to influence the decision/opportunity generated at T. It is NOT a new intelligence/scoring layer, NOT ML, NOT predictive modelling, NOT live trading, NOT broker execution. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11V public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 1485 prior tests still pass.
- Architecture: `Historical data -> Existing intelligence pipeline -> MarketScan -> TradeOpportunity -> 11W Outcome Evaluator -> Historical Outcome -> Replay Outcome Report`. The decision/opportunity at T is taken VERBATIM from the Sprint 11V replay (computed using only candles up to T); the outcome evaluator then consumes ONLY setup-timeframe candles that closed strictly after T. The two concerns (what the engine knew at T vs. what happened afterwards) are kept strictly separate.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11V `ReplayResult` / `MarketScanResult` / `InstrumentScanResult` (which carry the Sprint 11S `TradeDecision` + Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` + Sprint 11T `TradeOpportunity` by reference). The outcome layer implements NO candle-pattern / market-context / setup / candidate / decision / opportunity / scanner / ranking logic; every value is read from the reused engine outputs.
- New models `src/engine/models/historical_outcome.py` (frozen+slots): enum `OutcomeStatus` (TARGET_HIT / STOP_HIT / BOTH_TOUCHED / EXPIRED / NO_GEOMETRY / INSUFFICIENT_DATA) ÔÇö DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from Sprint 11T `OpportunityStatus` and Sprint 11S `DecisionClassification`; `is_determinate` / `is_ambiguous` properties. `OutcomeSubject` ÔÇö a lightweight, serializable projection of an opportunity at T (instrument, direction, evaluation_timestamp, entry, stop, target, decision_classification, decision_score, opportunity_status, rank, scan_id, setup_timeframe) that DECOUPLES the evaluator from the heavy Sprint 11R/11S/11T reference objects (dropped on scan serialization); `has_geometry` / `is_directional` properties. `HistoricalOutcome` (subject ref-by-reference + outcome_status, outcome_timestamp, exit_price, bars_held, mfe, mae, mfe_r, mae_r, realized_r, risk, reason; `__post_init__` invariants: NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no fabricated exit/R/bars; BOTH_TOUCHED carries no fabricated realized_r). `ReplayOutcomePoint`, `ReplayOutcomeReport` (deterministic report_id = "outcomes-"+sha256[:16]).
- New config `src/engine/config/historical_outcome_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `OutcomeConfig` ÔÇö `max_holding_bars` (positive int; the deterministic evaluation horizon; neither target nor stop reached within it -> EXPIRED), `min_history`, `label`, `metadata`. No magic numbers in the engine.
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/historical_outcome.py` (intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.historical_outcome import OutcomeEvaluator`):
  - `OutcomeEvaluator(config).evaluate(subject, future_candles) -> HistoricalOutcome`. Deterministic, pure, forward-only. The evaluator NEVER mutates the subject. STRUCTURAL forward-only guarantee: `_forward_window` keeps ONLY candles with `timestamp > evaluation_timestamp` truncated to `max_holding_bars` ÔÇö even if the caller accidentally supplies the at-T candle or earlier candles, they are never inspected; candles beyond the horizon are never inspected. When the anchor is None, no forward window is defined.
  - GEOMETRY VALIDATION: non-directional OR missing entry/stop/target OR non-positive risk (incl. stop==entry zero risk) -> NO_GEOMETRY. No outcome/exit/MFE/MAE/R fabricated.
  - LONG/SHORT SYMMETRY (no direction-specific hacks): touches use OHLC not close. LONG: target touched when `high >= target`, stop touched when `low <= stop`. SHORT (mirror): target touched when `low <= target`, stop touched when `high >= stop`. MFE/MAE: LONG favorable=`high-entry`, adverse=`entry-low`; SHORT favorable=`entry-low`, adverse=`high-entry`; clamped `>= 0`.
  - SAME-CANDLE AMBIGUITY (mandatory): tracks `first_target_bar` and `first_stop_bar` (first candle touching each). When they are the SAME bar -> BOTH_TOUCHED (a winner/loser is NEVER manufactured; `exit_price=None`, `realized_r=None`). When target touched strictly before stop (or stop never) -> TARGET_HIT at the target level. When stop touched strictly before target (or target never) -> STOP_HIT at the stop level. When neither within the horizon -> EXPIRED.
  - EXPIRATION: neither level reached within `max_holding_bars` -> EXPIRED; `exit_price` = close of the last in-window candle (mark-to-close); `realized_r` = mark-to-close R. Determinate.
  - R-MULTIPLE: `risk = abs(entry - stop)` (when valid + positive). LONG `R = (exit-entry)/risk`; SHORT `R = (entry-exit)/risk`. `realized_r` computed for TARGET_HIT / STOP_HIT / EXPIRED (determinate exits); `None` for BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous ÔÇö never fabricated), NO_GEOMETRY, INSUFFICIENT_DATA. `mfe_r = mfe/risk`, `mae_r = mae/risk` (R-normalized excursion) when risk valid; None otherwise.
  - MFE/MAE computed over the FULL forward window (all candles after T within the horizon) ÔÇö descriptive excursion of the opportunity over the evaluation horizon; never uses data beyond the horizon.
  - `bars_held`: candles evaluated up to and including the determining candle (1-based count); for EXPIRED = window length; 0 for NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA.
  - Subject builders (decouple from heavy reference objects): `build_outcome_subject(scan_result, scan_id, setup_timeframe) -> OutcomeSubject | None` (extracts entry/stop/target/direction from the Sprint 11R candidate reached via the Sprint 11S decision; None when no decision/candidate); `build_outcome_subjects(scan, setup_timeframe, eligible_only=True) -> tuple[OutcomeSubject, ...]`; `future_candles_after(candles, evaluation_timestamp) -> tuple` (strictly-greater filter; empty when anchor None).
  - `HistoricalOutcomeEngine(config, evaluator=None).evaluate_replay(replay_result, dataset) -> ReplayOutcomeReport` ÔÇö ORCHESTRATION ONLY. For each Sprint 11V `ReplayEvaluationPoint`, builds subjects from the LIVE scan results (eligible opportunities only), fetches the setup-timeframe candles strictly after T via `future_candles_after`, and delegates to `OutcomeEvaluator`.
