The decisions/opportunities are taken VERBATIM from the replay (never recomputed, never mutated). Also `evaluate_scan(scan, dataset, evaluation_time=None) -> tuple[HistoricalOutcome, ...]`. Deterministic report_id; no wall-clock.
- Serialization `src/engine/intelligence/historical_outcome_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__datetime__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `OUTCOME_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_outcome`/`serialize_outcome_bytes`/`deserialize_outcome`/`parse_outcome_header`/`canonical_outcome_json` + `serialize_outcome_report`/`deserialize_outcome_report`/`canonical_outcome_report_json`. The outcome model is ALREADY decoupled from heavy reference objects via `OutcomeSubject`, so the round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (identity, instrument, direction, timestamps, outcome status, exit, MFE/MAE absolute + R-normalized, realized R, risk, bars_held, ranking fields, scan_id). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- Reporting `src/engine/reporting/historical_outcome.py`: `HistoricalOutcomeFormatter().format(outcome) -> str` / `.format_point(point) -> str` / `.format_report(report) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Historical Replay Outcome Report, Scan ID, Instrument, Direction, Evaluation Time, Decision, Score, Opportunity Status, Rank, Entry, Stop, Target, Risk, Outcome, Outcome Time, Exit Price, Bars Held, MFE, MAE, MFE (R), MAE (R), Realized R, Reason. Unavailable values shown as "unavailable". Every report ends with the explicit WARNING that historical outcome results are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and are NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability. No predictive language (no "will definitely win" / "predicts" / "accuracy is guaranteed"). Following the 11O-11V reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching HistoricalReplayFormatter/MarketScanFormatter/etc.).
- POINT-IN-TIME / LEAKAGE PROTECTION (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): (A) decision stability ÔÇö the decision/opportunity at T is taken verbatim from the Sprint 11V replay and is NEVER recalculated or mutated by outcome evaluation; (B) forward-only window ÔÇö the evaluator inspects ONLY candles with `timestamp > evaluation_timestamp`, structurally filtered (the at-T candle and earlier candles are never inspected even if supplied); (C) horizon cap ÔÇö candles beyond `max_holding_bars` are never inspected, so mutating candles after the horizon does NOT change the outcome; (D) future mutation after T may change the OUTCOME but NEVER the subject (decision at T); (E) instrument isolation ÔÇö each instrument's outcome is evaluated from its own future candles; another instrument's data never bleeds in; (F) HTF protection intact ÔÇö the Sprint 11V higher-timeframe incomplete-candle protection is untouched (the outcome layer operates only on eligible opportunities the scan already surfaced; outcome count == eligible count).
- `scripts/test_historical_outcome.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 14 requirements (LONG target hit; LONG stop hit; SHORT target hit; SHORT stop hit; BOTH_TOUCHED ambiguity LONG+SHORT; incomplete geometry -> NO_GEOMETRY; expiration -> EXPIRED; MFE/MAE absolute+R; multiple opportunities each evaluated independently; point-in-time protection [decision at T stable + forward-only window + at-T candle excluded]; future mutation outside horizon; deterministic repeated evaluation; serialization round trip; existing pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged) + a full replay outcome report (Sprint 11V -> 11W integration) + report serialization round trip. Explicitly states results are NOT predictions. Ends with `Sprint 11W demo completed successfully.` Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_historical_outcome.py`: 92 tests across 18 sections (A model+subject basics ÔÇö enum members/frozen+slots/invariants/properties/delegates-to-subject/no-geometry-rejects-fabricated/both-touched-rejects-fabricated-R; B config ÔÇö defaults/positive-max/non-negative-min/frozen; C LONG outcomes ÔÇö target/stop/expired/ohlc-not-close-target/ohlc-not-close-stop; D SHORT outcomes ÔÇö target/stop/expired/ohlc-target/ohlc-stop/symmetric-same-geometry; E both-touched ÔÇö LONG-single-candle/SHORT-single-candle/first-touch-same-candle/target-then-both-is-target/stop-then-both-is-stop; F geometry ÔÇö missing-target/missing-stop/missing-entry/non-directional/zero-risk/no-future-inspection; G insufficient+boundaries ÔÇö no-future/at-T-excluded/helper-strict/helper-none-anchor; H horizon ÔÇö exact-horizon-target/beyond-horizon-expired/mutation-after-horizon/horizon-truncates-mfe-mae; I MFE/MAE ÔÇö LONG/SHORT/none-when-no-geometry/full-window/r-normalized; J realized R ÔÇö target/stop/expired/short-target/short-stop/both-touched-none/no-geometry-none; K multiple opportunities ÔÇö independent/identity-preserved; L instrument isolation; M point-in-time ÔÇö strictly-future-only/future-mutation-changes-outcome-not-subject/decision-stability-prefix==full/no-input-mutation; N determinism ÔÇö repeated/all-statuses/no-randomness; O serialization ÔÇö round-trip-target/identity-fields/both-touched/expired/no-geometry/schema-version/future-schema-rejected/deterministic-bytes/report-round-trip; P replay integration ÔÇö all-points/uses-only-future/decision-at-T-unchanged/HTF-protection-intact/outcome-count==eligible-count/deterministic/multiple-opportunities; Q reporting ÔÇö returns-str/sections/warning/no-predictive-language/unavailable-shown/format-report-str/determinism; R subject builders ÔÇö from-scan-result/none-when-no-decision/eligible-only/all-when-not-eligible-only).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is outcome evaluation + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every intelligence value is read from the reused Sprint 11A-11V engines); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; data/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. A historical outcome is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. Incomplete/invalid geometry -> NO_GEOMETRY (never fabricated); missing future data -> INSUFFICIENT_DATA (never a directional conclusion); same-candle ambiguity -> BOTH_TOUCHED (never a manufactured winner/loser); realized R never fabricated for ambiguous outcomes. No future leakage (structurally enforced: strictly-after-anchor filter + horizon cap; regression-tested for at-T-exclusion, post-horizon-mutation, future-mutation-changes-outcome-not-decision, instrument isolation). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3). No Sprint 11A-11V regression.

## Sprint 11X ÔÇö Historical Performance Analytics (added)
This sprint is the downstream HISTORICAL PERFORMANCE ANALYTICS layer. Sprint 11W answers "What happened to this opportunity AFTER T?"; Sprint 11X answers "How did the opportunities historically produced by the engine perform, in aggregate?". The analytics layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED Sprint 11W `HistoricalOutcome` objects and aggregates them into descriptive performance statistics. It NEVER re-evaluates outcomes, NEVER re-runs the pipeline, NEVER re-reads candles, NEVER uses future information, NEVER introduces ML / predictive models / parameter optimization / live trading. This is measurement / analytics only. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11W public API signature was changed, no existing engine was modified, and all 1485 prior tests still pass.
- Architecture: `Historical data -> Existing intelligence pipeline -> MarketScan -> TradeOpportunity -> Sprint 11W Outcome -> Sprint 11X Performance Analytics -> Historical Performance Report`. The analytics layer sits BELOW the outcome layer (consumes its outputs); it is NOT a new orchestration package.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11W `HistoricalOutcome` / `OutcomeSubject` / `OutcomeStatus`. The analytics layer implements NO candle-pattern / market-context / setup / candidate / decision / opportunity / scanner / ranking / outcome-evaluation logic; every value is read from the reused outcome objects. `OutcomeSubject` is retained BY REFERENCE and never mutated.
- ADDITIVE enrichment of Sprint 11W `OutcomeSubject` (semantic-preserving): two optional defaulted string fields `setup_type` (default `""`) and `mtf_alignment` (default `""`) added so the analytics layer can group outcomes by setup type and MTF alignment WITHOUT re-reading the heavy Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` / Sprint 11U `InstrumentScanResult` reference objects (which are dropped on scan serialization). `build_outcome_subject` now captures `candidate.setup_type.name` and `scan_result.alignment.name` verbatim. These projections NEVER influence outcome evaluation semantics (the `OutcomeEvaluator` ignores them). Legacy 3-positional-arg `OutcomeSubject(instrument, direction, ts)` construction still works; the Sprint 11W serializer iterates `__dataclass_fields__` so the round trip remains lossless (verified: all 92 Sprint 11W tests still pass).
- New models `src/engine/models/historical_performance.py` (frozen+slots): enum `BreakdownDimension` (INSTRUMENT / DIRECTION / DECISION / OPPORTUNITY_STATUS / MTF_ALIGNMENT / SETUP_TYPE / OPPORTUNITY_RANK). `HistoricalPerformanceStatistics` (overall + per-group core stats: counts total/resolved/target_hits/stop_hits/expired/both_touched/no_geometry/insufficient_data; rates win_rate/loss_rate/expiration_rate/ambiguous_rate; R metrics total/average/median_realized_r, gross_positive_r/gross_negative_r, profit_factor, valid_r_count; excursion averages average_mfe/average_mae/average_mfe_r/average_mae_r). `HistoricalPerformanceGroup` (key + statistics), `HistoricalPerformanceBreakdown` (dimension + deterministically-ordered groups tuple), `HistoricalPerformanceAnalytics` (analytics_id = "perf-"+sha256[:16] of canonical SORTED outcome identities, overall, breakdowns tuple, outcome_count, label, metadata, rationale; `is_empty` property). The names are DELIBERATELY DISTINCT from the Sprint 11E `PerformanceAnalytics` model (which aggregates signal-validation results from the single-series pipeline).
- NO FABRICATED VALUES (preserves Sprint 11W semantics exactly): unavailable metrics remain `None`; counts remain `0`; the distinction between "observed zero" and "not available" is preserved. (1) Win/loss rate = `target_hits / (target_hits + stop_hits)` ÔÇö EXPIRED / BOTH_TOUCHED / NO_GEOMETRY / INSUFFICIENT_DATA are EXCLUDED from the denominator; `None` when denominator is zero. (2) R aggregates (total/average/median/gross positive/gross negative/profit factor) computed ONLY over outcomes with a valid `realized_r` (TARGET_HIT / STOP_HIT / EXPIRED); BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous, `realized_r=None`), NO_GEOMETRY, INSUFFICIENT_DATA are EXCLUDED. (3) Profit factor = `gross_positive_r / abs(gross_negative_r)`; `None` when there is no valid negative R (never a fabricated finite number / never +inf). (4) MFE/MAE averages computed ONLY over outcomes where the excursion value is available; unavailable never converted to zero. BOTH_TOUCHED is NEVER treated as a target hit or a stop hit; NO_GEOMETRY never contributes to R metrics.
- New config `src/engine/config/performance_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization parameters): `PerformanceAnalyticsConfig` ÔÇö `label`, `metadata`, `rounding_precision` (non-negative when set; used ONLY by the formatter for human readability; underlying statistics retain full precision).
- New engine `src/engine/intelligence/performance_analytics.py` (intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.performance_analytics import PerformanceAnalyticsEngine`): `PerformanceAnalyticsEngine(config).analyze(outcomes, label="", metadata=None) -> HistoricalPerformanceAnalytics`. Pure, deterministic, downstream-only. The input outcomes are NEVER mutated. Stateless across calls. Computes overall statistics + 7 breakdowns (INSTRUMENT / DIRECTION / MTF_ALIGNMENT / SETUP_TYPE / DECISION / OPPORTUNITY_STATUS / OPPORTUNITY_RANK). Group statistics reuse the SAME `_compute_statistics` function as overall (no duplicated logic).
- DETERMINISTIC ORDERING (no unordered set/dict iteration, no wall-clock, no randomness): DIRECTION canonical [LONG, SHORT]; DECISION [PREFERRED, QUALIFIED, WATCH, REJECTED]; OPPORTUNITY_STATUS [BEST_OPPORTUNITY, ALTERNATIVE_OPPORTUNITY, WATCH, NO_OPPORTUNITY]; MTF_ALIGNMENT [ALIGNED, NEUTRAL, CONFLICTING, UNKNOWN]; SETUP_TYPE [TREND_CONTINUATION, BREAKOUT, STRUCTURE_CONTINUATION, RANGE_REJECTION, SETUP_CANDIDATE]; INSTRUMENT sorted lexicographically; OPPORTUNITY_RANK numeric ascending (rank 1 first). The empty-string "unavailable" sentinel sorts LAST in every dimension (unavailable metadata reported after observed values ÔÇö never invented). Two identical outcome collections always produce identical analytics; the analytics_id hashes SORTED outcome identities so a shuffled input yields the same id.
- Serialization `src/engine/intelligence/performance_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`/`__dataclass__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `PERFORMANCE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_performance`/`serialize_performance_bytes`/`deserialize_performance`/`parse_performance_header`/`canonical_performance_json`. The analytics result is a pure aggregate (already decoupled from heavy reference objects), so the round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (analytics_id, overall statistics, every breakdown + group, outcome_count, label, metadata, rationale). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- Reporting `src/engine/reporting/performance.py`: `PerformanceReportFormatter(precision=2).format(analytics) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Historical Performance Report header (analytics id, label, metadata), Overall Performance (Opportunities Evaluated, Valid/Resolved, TARGET_HIT/STOP_HIT/EXPIRED/BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA counts, Win Rate, Loss Rate, Expiration Rate, Ambiguous Rate, Total/Average/Median Realized R, Valid R Observations, Gross Positive R, Gross Negative R, Profit Factor, Average MFE/MAE/MFE(R)/MAE(R)), Performance by Instrument / Direction / MTF Alignment / Setup Type / Decision / Opportunity Status / Opportunity Rank (each group: Count, Target/Stop/Expired/Both Touched/No Geometry, Win Rate, Average/Median/Total Realized R, Average MFE/MAE/MFE(R)/MAE(R)), Rationale. Unavailable values shown as "unavailable". Every report ends with the explicit WARNING that historical performance results are descriptive technical-analysis outputs and are NOT predictive signals or guarantees of profitability. No predictive language. Following the 11O-11W reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching HistoricalOutcomeFormatter/MarketScanFormatter/etc.).
- POINT-IN-TIME / LEAKAGE SAFETY (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the analytics layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED outcomes and adds NO future information. (1) Repeated evaluation produces identical output. (2) Mutating candles outside the historical evaluation/outcome horizon does NOT change the analytics (the analytics never reads candles). (3) Outcomes are taken verbatim and never mutated. (4) No future information is introduced into performance aggregation. The point-in-time correctness established in Sprint 11V and 11W is preserved unchanged.
- `scripts/test_performance_analytics.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 13 requirements (mixed outcomes aggregated; overall metrics; instrument breakdown; LONG vs SHORT; MTF alignment comparison; decision/opportunity breakdown; rank breakdown; BOTH_TOUCHED handled as ambiguous; NO_GEOMETRY handled honestly; serialization round trip; deterministic repeated evaluation; future mutation protection; existing pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged) + a full end-to-end 11V->11W->11X integration report + report serialization round trip. Explicitly states results are NOT predictions. Ends with `Sprint 11X demo completed successfully.` Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_performance_analytics.py`: 105 tests across 34 sections (A empty; B single target hit; C single stop hit; D expired; E both-touched exclusion; F no-geometry exclusion; G mixed; H total R; I average R; J median R odd+even; K gross positive/negative; L profit factor; M no-loss/no-win/zero-R/zero-denominator edge cases; N-O-P-Q MFE/MAE/MFE-R/MAE-R aggregation + ignores-unavailable + all-unavailable-none; R-X breakdowns instrument/direction/decision/opportunity-status/mtf-alignment/setup-type/rank + group core stats; Y deterministic ordering instrument-sorted/direction-canonical/rank-numeric/dimension-order; Z repeated-equality/shuffled-same-overall+id/generator-input; AA serialization round-trip identity/overall/breakdowns/counts-metadata/unavailable-preserved/schema-version/future-schema-rejected/deterministic-bytes/empty/r-metrics-preserved; BB future-mutation-unchanged/no-future-info/outcomes-not-mutated; CC backward-compat legacy-construction/keyword-construction/evaluator-ignores-new-fields/outcome-serialization-round-trips-new-fields/build_outcome_subject-captures-setup-type+alignment; DD pipeline regression baseline-4-3 + rerun-identical; EE reporting returns-str/required-sections/warning/unavailable/empty/deterministic/no-predictive-language/precision-validation; FF config defaults/frozen/negative-precision-rejected/label-metadata-used/label-override; GG immutability frozen+slots+enum-members; HH end-to-end from real 11V->11W replay outcome report).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is analytics + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value is read from the reused Sprint 11A-11W engines); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine modified (only ONE additive semantic-preserving enrichment of the Sprint 11W `OutcomeSubject` model ÔÇö two optional defaulted string fields ÔÇö plus additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. Historical performance analytics are DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness. Unavailable metrics remain None (never fabricated); BOTH_TOUCHED excluded from win/loss + R; NO_GEOMETRY excluded from R; profit factor None when no valid negative R; observed zero never confused with not-available. No future leakage (analytics consumes already-computed outcomes only; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness, sorted identities in id). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3). No Sprint 11A-11W regression.


## Sprint 11Y ÔÇö Historical Evidence / Validation Layer (added)
This sprint is the HISTORICAL EVIDENCE / VALIDATION layer. Sprint 11X answers "How did the opportunities historically produced by the engine perform, in aggregate?"; Sprint 11Y answers "What historical evidence is strong enough, weak enough, or insufficient to be useful to downstream intelligence?". The evidence layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED Sprint 11W HistoricalOutcome objects, REUSES the Sprint 11X statistics computation (_compute_statistics/_dimension_key/canonical-ordering helpers imported from engine.intelligence.performance_analytics ÔÇö NO trading/outcome/performance logic duplicated), and classifies the STRENGTH of the available historical evidence per cohort. It NEVER re-evaluates outcomes, NEVER re-runs the pipeline, NEVER re-reads candles, NEVER uses future information, NEVER performs statistical hypothesis tests, NEVER introduces ML/predictive models. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11X public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 1682 prior tests still pass.
- Architecture: Historical data -> Existing intelligence pipeline -> MarketScan -> TradeOpportunity -> Sprint 11W Outcome -> Sprint 11X Performance Analytics -> Sprint 11Y Evidence/Validation -> Historical Evidence Report. The evidence layer sits BELOW the 11X analytics layer (consumes its statistics); it is NOT a new orchestration package.
- CRITICAL DISTINCTION ÔÇö "observed historical result" vs "evidence strength": the reused HistoricalPerformanceStatistics (Sprint 11X) describe the OBSERVED result (win rate, realized R, profit factor, MFE/MAE). The EvidenceStrength classification describes whether that observed result is backed by ENOUGH historical observation to be reliable/meaningful evidence. These are NOT the same: a 1-trade +2R cohort has observed win rate 100% but INSUFFICIENT evidence; 100 trades at a modest consistent result may have STRONG evidence. The demo proves this concretely (TCS|BREAKOUT has STRONG evidence of an UNFAVOURABLE observed result ÔÇö win rate 0.30, profit factor 0.86).
- SAMPLE SIZE IS A HARD GATE: a cohort whose total sample < min_sample_total is ALWAYS INSUFFICIENT, regardless of how favourable its observed win rate/realized R is. A small sample is never promoted to stronger evidence merely because its observed result is impressive. No statistical "significant" terminology is used (no hypothesis test is performed); rationales explicitly state "no statistical hypothesis test was performed".
- New models src/engine/models/historical_evidence.py (frozen+slots): enum EvidenceStrength (INSUFFICIENT/WEAK/MODERATE/STRONG; rank_value strongest-first; is_sufficient property). CohortSpec (ordered tuple of 1-2 BreakdownDimension values; __post_init__ rejects empty/>2 dims to prevent combinatorial explosion; is_composite/label properties). HistoricalEvidenceCohort (spec/key/reused HistoricalPerformanceStatistics/strength/sample_count/resolved_count/valid_r_count/rationale; is_sufficient property). HistoricalEvidenceBreakdown (spec + deterministically-ordered cohorts tuple). HistoricalEvidenceSummary (overall reused statistics + strength + counts + rationale). HistoricalEvidenceReport (evidence_id="evidence-"+sha256[:16], summary, breakdowns, cohort_count, sufficient_cohort_count, insufficient_cohort_count, label, metadata, config_snapshot, rationale; is_empty property).
- New config src/engine/config/historical_evidence_config.py (frozen+slots, validated, DOCUMENTED thresholds): EvidenceConfig ÔÇö sample gates min_sample_total=30/min_resolved=10/min_valid_r=10; strong gates strong_min_sample=50/strong_min_resolved=30/strong_min_valid_r=30 (all strong >= corresponding min); descriptive-metric CONTEXT (never hard gates) favorable_win_rate=0.55 (in [0,1])/favorable_profit_factor=1.3 (>0)/favorable_avg_r=0.1 (>=0); label/metadata. __post_init__ validates all ordering/bound invariants. snapshot() returns sorted auditable (name,str(value)) pairs. Threshold meaning documented on every field.
- New engine src/engine/intelligence/historical_evidence.py (intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via from engine.intelligence.historical_evidence import HistoricalEvidenceEngine): HistoricalEvidenceEngine(config, specs=None).evaluate(outcomes, label="", metadata=None, specs=None) -> HistoricalEvidenceReport. Pure, deterministic, stateless, downstream-only. REUSES _compute_statistics/_dimension_key/_canonical_for/_ordered_keys from Sprint 11X (imported ÔÇö zero duplication). CLASSIFICATION: total < min_sample_total -> INSUFFICIENT (hard gate); else resolved<min_resolved OR valid_r<min_valid_r -> WEAK; else total>=strong_min_sample AND resolved>=strong_min_resolved AND valid_r>=strong_min_valid_r -> STRONG; else MODERATE. Observed-metric context (win rate/profit factor/avg R favourability, MFE/MAE) appears in the rationale ONLY as descriptive context ÔÇö never upgrades strength.
- SUPPORTED COHORT SPECS (controlled, no combinatorial explosion): 7 single-dimension specs (INSTRUMENT/DIRECTION/MTF_ALIGNMENT/SETUP_TYPE/DECISION/OPPORTUNITY_STATUS/OPPORTUNITY_RANK, mirroring 11X) + 5 documented composite pairs (SETUP_TYPE+DIRECTION, SETUP_TYPE+MTF_ALIGNMENT, DECISION+OPPORTUNITY_RANK, SETUP_TYPE+DECISION, INSTRUMENT+SETUP_TYPE) = SUPPORTED_COHORT_SPECS (12 specs). Composite cohort keys join dimension values with "|" (deterministic). Caller may override specs to evaluate a subset.
- DETERMINISTIC ORDERING: single-dimension cohorts reuse the 11X canonical order (DIRECTION LONG<SHORT; DECISION PREFERRED<QUALIFIED<WATCH<REJECTED; OPPORTUNITY_STATUS BEST<ALTERNATIVE<WATCH<NO; MTF ALIGNED<NEUTRAL<CONFLICTING<UNKNOWN; SETUP_TYPE canonical; INSTRUMENT lexicographic; OPPORTUNITY_RANK numeric ascending). Composite cohorts order by first-dimension canonical then second-dimension canonical, then lexicographic, with the "" unavailable sentinel LAST. No unordered set/dict iteration; no wall-clock; no randomness.
- HONEST OUTCOME HANDLING (preserves Sprint 11W/11X semantics exactly via reused statistics): BOTH_TOUCHED ambiguous ÔÇö never win/loss, realized_r None so never contributes to R aggregates (win_rate None when no target/stop); NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no fabricated values (R/Profit Factor/MFE None); EXPIRED mark-to-close (contributes valid R); TARGET_HIT/STOP_HIT resolved favourable/adverse.
- Serialization src/engine/intelligence/historical_evidence_serialization.py: deterministic, self-describing JSON (__enum__/__dataclass__/__tuple__ type tags), EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1. serialize_evidence/serialize_evidence_bytes/deserialize_evidence/parse_evidence_header/canonical_evidence_json. The evidence result is a pure aggregate (already decoupled from heavy reference objects via reused statistics), so the round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (evidence_id, summary, breakdowns, cohorts, strengths, statistics, config_snapshot, counts, label, metadata, rationale). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- Reporting src/engine/reporting/historical_evidence.py: HistoricalEvidenceFormatter(precision=2).format(report) -> str. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Historical Evidence Report header (evidence id, label, metadata, thresholds snapshot), Overall Evidence Summary (strength, sample/resolved/valid-R counts, reused observed-result stats block, rationale), Cohort Summary (total/usable/insufficient cohort counts), Evidence by <each cohort spec> (per cohort: strength, sample/resolved/valid-R, reused stats block, rationale), Rationale. Unavailable values shown as "unavailable". Every report ends with the explicit WARNING "Historical evidence is descriptive and does not guarantee future performance." No predictive language. Following the 11O-11X reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching PerformanceReportFormatter/HistoricalOutcomeFormatter/etc.).
- POINT-IN-TIME / LEAKAGE SAFETY (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the evidence layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED outcomes only and adds NO future information. (1) Repeated evaluation identical. (2) Mutating/inspecting unrelated future candles does NOT change evidence (the engine never reads candles ÔÇö regression-tested). (3) Outcomes taken verbatim, never mutated. (4) No future information introduced. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- scripts/test_historical_evidence.py: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 10 requirements (strong cohort; weak cohort; insufficient cohort; multiple dimensions; BOTH_TOUCHED ambiguous; NO_GEOMETRY honest; deterministic repeated analysis; serialization round trip; existing pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged; full Sprint 11Y report) + a full end-to-end 11V->11W->11X->11Y integration. Explicitly states evidence is NOT predictive. Ends with "Sprint 11Y demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
- tests/test_historical_evidence.py: 94 tests across 31 sections (A basic aggregation/empty/default-specs; B-H single-dimension evidence instrument/direction/mtf/setup-type/decision/opportunity-status/opportunity-rank; I composite cohorts + key-join + spec-validation; J insufficient sample; K strong/moderate/weak; L both-touched; M no-geometry; N expired; O missing-metadata/unavailable-sentinel/rank-0; P missing-metrics; Q determinism; R shuffle-invariance id+summary+breakdowns; S no-mutation outcomes+list; T serialization full/id/summary/breakdowns/config-snapshot/strengths/bytes/deterministic/header/future-schema; U deterministic-id prefix/different-inputs/label/specs; V stable-ordering instrument-sorted/direction-canonical/rank-numeric/composite-first-dim-dominates; W config-affects-classification lower-min-promotes/raise-strong-prevents/snapshot-captured; X future-unrelated-unchanged/no-lookahead; Y 11X-unchanged importable/schema/perf-engine-unaffected; Z pipeline-regression baseline-4-3 + e2e-chain; AA reporting returns-str/sections/warning/no-predictive/empty/deterministic/strength-shown/precision; EE no-statistical-language/no-hypothesis-test-explicit; BB config-validation all invariants + frozen; CC immutability frozen/slots/strength-rank-value/is-sufficient; engine edge-cases empty-specs/specs-override/label-override/metadata-override/cohort-counts-consistent/observed-vs-evidence-distinct).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is evidence/validation + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-11X engines; statistics computation reused not duplicated); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. Historical evidence is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim. Sample size is a HARD GATE (small sample never strong regardless of observed win rate); observed result vs evidence strength kept distinct; BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); unavailable metrics remain None. No future leakage (evidence consumes already-computed outcomes only; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness, sorted identities in id, canonical cohort ordering). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Sprint 11X analytics unchanged. No Sprint 11A-11X regression.

## Sprint 11Z ÔÇö Evidence-Conditioned Strategy Intelligence (added)
This sprint is the EVIDENCE-CONDITIONED STRATEGY INTELLIGENCE layer. Sprint 11X answers "How did the opportunities historically produced by the engine perform, in aggregate?"; Sprint 11Y answers "What historical evidence is strong enough, weak enough, or insufficient to be useful to downstream intelligence?"; Sprint 11Z answers "What does the historical evidence actually tell us about this type of opportunity?" ÔÇö a conservative, descriptive STRATEGY-LEVEL interpretation built ON TOP of the already-computed Sprint 11X performance statistics and Sprint 11Y evidence-strength classifications. The strategy layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED Sprint 11X/11Y evidence, REUSES the reused statistics + the reused evidence strength + the reused cohort specs (NO trading/outcome/performance/evidence-classification logic duplicated), and produces structured, conservative strategy assessments. It NEVER re-evaluates outcomes, NEVER re-runs the pipeline, NEVER re-reads candles, NEVER uses future information, NEVER performs statistical hypothesis tests, NEVER introduces ML/predictive models, NEVER generates trading signals / BUY/SELL recommendations / price predictions. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11Y public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 1776 prior tests still pass.
- Architecture: Historical data -> Existing intelligence pipeline -> MarketScan -> TradeOpportunity -> Sprint 11W Outcome -> Sprint 11X Performance Analytics -> Sprint 11Y Evidence/Validation -> Sprint 11Z Strategy Intelligence -> Strategy Intelligence Report. The strategy layer sits BELOW the 11Y evidence layer (consumes its already-computed reports); it is NOT a new orchestration package.
- CRITICAL ARCHITECTURE ÔÇö three strictly separate concerns, surfaced separately in the models and the report: (1) the OBSERVED historical result (reused HistoricalPerformanceStatistics from 11X); (2) the EVIDENCE STRENGTH (reused EvidenceStrength from 11Y); (3) the STRATEGY INTERPRETATION (Sprint 11Z StrategyAssessmentStatus + interpretation/limitations text). These are NEVER conflated.
- CORE REUSE CONTRACT ÔÇö the deterministic one-to-one mapping EVIDENCE_TO_STRATEGY from the reused Sprint 11Y EvidenceStrength to the Sprint 11Z StrategyAssessmentStatus: INSUFFICIENT->INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE, WEAK->LIMITED_EVIDENCE, MODERATE->SUPPORTIVE_EVIDENCE, STRONG->STRONGER_HISTORICAL_SUPPORT. Sprint 11Z does NOT re-classify evidence ÔÇö it interprets the already-classified strength. A tiny cohort with an impressive observed win rate is NEVER promoted to strong historical support, because the underlying 11Y strength hard-gates on sample size (11Z inherits that gate unchanged). The status mapping is FIXED (not configurable) so the reuse contract cannot be silently weakened; the config carries only descriptive-context thresholds for interpretation wording (favorable_win_rate/profit_factor/avg_r) + lookup_max_dimensions + label/metadata.
- New models src/engine/models/strategy_intelligence.py (frozen+slots): enums StrategyAssessmentStatus (INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE/LIMITED_EVIDENCE/SUPPORTIVE_EVIDENCE/STRONGER_HISTORICAL_SUPPORT; rank_value strongest-first; is_actionable property), CohortMatchStatus (MATCHED/NO_MATCH); EVIDENCE_TO_STRATEGY dict (the fixed reuse contract); OpportunityProfile (instrument/direction/setup_type/mtf_alignment/decision/opportunity_status/rank ÔÇö all optional, ""/0 unavailable sentinel matching 11X/11Y; available_dimensions() returns the usable non-empty pairs deterministically; is_empty property); StrategyEvidenceAssessment (assessment_id="strat-"+sha256[:16], spec CohortSpec ref, cohort_key, observed_performance HistoricalPerformanceStatistics ref, evidence_strength EvidenceStrength ref, assessment_status, sample/resolved/valid_r counts, interpretation, limitations, label, metadata; has_evidence/is_supported properties); CohortComparisonMetric (name, value_a, value_b, delta (None when either unavailable ÔÇö never fabricated), note descriptive); CohortComparison (comparison_id="compare-"+sha256[:16] SYMMETRIC in the two cohort keys + assessment ids, spec, cohort_a_key/cohort_b_key, cohort_a_present/cohort_b_present, assessment_a/assessment_b, metrics tuple (7 deterministic rows), notes tuple (descriptive), disclaimer; both_present property); OpportunityEvidenceLookup (lookup_id="lookup-"+sha256[:16], profile, match_status, matched_spec, matched_cohort HistoricalEvidenceCohort ref, assessment, limitations; matched/is_empty properties). Optional fields use None so unavailable is never silently a real value. No business logic in models.
- New config src/engine/config/strategy_intelligence_config.py (frozen+slots, validated): StrategyIntelligenceConfig ÔÇö favorable_win_rate=0.55 (in [0,1]) / favorable_profit_factor=1.3 (>0) / favorable_avg_r=0.1 (>=0) (DESCRIPTIVE-METRIC CONTEXT for interpretation wording ONLY ÔÇö never upgrade the status, never override the reused evidence strength) + lookup_max_dimensions=2 (in [1,2], mirrors the Sprint 11Y controlled composite-cohort limit so the lookup never expands beyond supported cohort specs) + label/metadata. snapshot() returns sorted auditable pairs. The core EVIDENCE_TO_STRATEGY mapping is intentionally NOT configurable (fixed reuse contract).
- New engine src/engine/intelligence/strategy_intelligence.py (intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via from engine.intelligence.strategy_intelligence import StrategyIntelligenceEngine): StrategyIntelligenceEngine(config=None). Public API: assess_cohort(cohort, label="", metadata=None) -> StrategyEvidenceAssessment; assess(report, spec, key, label="", metadata=None) -> StrategyEvidenceAssessment | None; compare(report, spec, key_a, key_b) -> CohortComparison; lookup(report, profile, label="", metadata=None) -> OpportunityEvidenceLookup. Pure, deterministic, stateless, downstream-only. REUSES the 11Y report's already-computed cohorts (finds a cohort by (spec,key) in the report's breakdowns) and the 11X statistics embedded in those cohorts (referenced by identity, never recomputed). The reused statistics/strength objects are retained BY REFERENCE and never mutated (regression-tested: a.observed_performance is cohort.statistics; a.evidence_strength is cohort.strength).
  - ASSESSMENT: assess_cohort reads the reused stats + strength, applies EVIDENCE_TO_STRATEGY, builds descriptive interpretation (carries observed-metric favourability context but NEVER upgrades the status) + descriptive limitations (sample size, no statistical hypothesis test, no future guarantee, explicit BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA mention when present). assess(report, spec, key) finds the cohort and delegates; returns None when no such cohort (honest fallback ÔÇö no fabricated assessment).
  - COMPARISON (DESCRIPTIVE, no statistical superiority): compare(report, spec, key_a, key_b) looks up both cohorts; when either is absent, records cohort_a_present/cohort_b_present explicitly and ALL metrics are None (never fabricated). When both present, exposes 7 deterministic metric rows (Opportunity Count, Resolved Count, Valid R Count, Win Rate, Average Realized R, Total Realized R, Profit Factor) with value_a/value_b/delta (delta None when either unavailable) + descriptive note. Notes are descriptive relative observations ("cohort A has more observations"; "cohort A has stronger evidence") ÔÇö NEVER "statistically superior"/"statistically significant"; the disclaimer explicitly states "No statistical procedure is available to claim one cohort is superior". The comparison_id is SYMMETRIC in the two cohort keys + assessment ids (comparing A vs B == comparing B vs A's id); the metrics remain ordered per the caller's (A,B) order.
  - CURRENT OPPORTUNITY EVIDENCE LOOKUP: lookup(report, profile) reuses the Sprint 11Y SUPPORTED_COHORT_SPECS and the already-computed cohorts. Deterministic spec selection: the most specific satisfiable spec (most matching non-empty profile dimensions, capped at lookup_max_dimensions, tie-broken by SUPPORTED_COHORT_SPECS order) ÔÇö so a profile with setup_type+direction selects the composite SETUP_TYPE+DIRECTION spec, not the single SETUP_TYPE spec. Builds the expected key using the SAME convention as the 11X _dimension_key ("" for unavailable, str(rank) for non-zero rank) so the lookup key is compatible with the already-computed 11Y cohort keys. MATCHED -> matched_cohort + assessment; NO_MATCH -> matched_cohort=None, assessment=None, explicit limitations ("No supported cohort spec is satisfiable" / "No historical cohort matches ... key ... in the evidence report"). No evidence is fabricated. The lookup NEVER matches on invented metadata (a spec is a candidate only when EVERY dimension it requires has a non-empty profile value).
- Serialization src/engine/intelligence/strategy_intelligence_serialization.py: deterministic, self-describing JSON (__enum__/__dataclass__/__tuple__ type tags), STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1. serialize_assessment/serialize_assessment_bytes/deserialize_assessment/parse_strategy_header/canonical_assessment_json + serialize_comparison/serialize_comparison_bytes/deserialize_comparison/canonical_comparison_json + serialize_lookup/serialize_lookup_bytes/deserialize_lookup/canonical_lookup_json. The strategy results are pure aggregates already decoupled from heavy reference objects (the reused stats/strength/cohort are embedded by value), so the round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (ids, cohort spec/key, reused statistics, evidence strength, assessment status, interpretation, limitations, comparison metrics/notes/disclaimer, lookup profile/match/assessment/limitations). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- Reporting src/engine/reporting/strategy_intelligence.py: StrategyIntelligenceFormatter(precision=2).format(assessment) -> str / .format_comparison(comparison) -> str / .format_lookup(lookup) -> str. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). STRICTLY distinguishes the three concerns as labeled sections: format sections (Assessment Identity, Cohort, Observed Historical Result, Evidence Strength, Strategy Interpretation, Limitations); comparison sections (Cohort Comparison Report header, Metric Comparison table, Evidence Strength Comparison, Descriptive Notes, Disclaimer); lookup sections (Current Opportunity Evidence Lookup header, Opportunity Profile, Matched Historical Cohort, Strategy Assessment, Limitations). Unavailable values shown as "unavailable"; missing cohort shown explicitly. Every report ends with the explicit WARNING "Historical evidence is descriptive and does not guarantee future performance." No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/trading-recommendation language. Following the 11O-11Y reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py, matching HistoricalEvidenceFormatter/PerformanceReportFormatter/etc.).
- HONEST FALLBACKS (preserves 11W/11X/11Y semantics exactly via reused statistics): no matching cohort (lookup) -> CohortMatchStatus.NO_MATCH with matched_cohort=None/assessment=None; insufficient sample -> StrategyAssessmentStatus.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE (propagated from EvidenceStrength.INSUFFICIENT); unavailable metric -> None (never fabricated); BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous, realized_r None) never contributes to win/loss or R aggregates and is mentioned in limitations; NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no fabricated R values and are mentioned in limitations; missing cohort in comparison -> cohort_*_present=False and all metrics None. Observed zero never confused with not-available.
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the strategy layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED Sprint 11X/11Y evidence and adds NO future information. (1) The public API takes a HistoricalEvidenceReport, never candles (signature inspected). (2) Works with OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate patched to raise (must not re-evaluate outcomes). (3) Works with HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate patched to raise (must not re-run pipeline). (4) Mutating unrelated future candles does NOT change the assessment (the engine never reads candles). (5) Outcomes / evidence report / profile taken verbatim, never mutated. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): identical inputs always produce identical outputs; the assessment_id hashes the canonical (spec/key/reused-stats/strength/status/label/metadata) identity; the comparison_id is symmetric in the two cohort keys + assessment ids; the lookup_id hashes the profile's available dimensions + matched spec/key/strength (sorted). Shuffled-equivalent outcomes produce the same assessment id (via the 11Y sorted-identity evidence id and the cohort-based assessment id). Metric rows + notes are deterministically ordered. Config-affects-wording-not-status (the descriptive-context thresholds change interpretation text only, never the status).
- scripts/test_strategy_intelligence.py: demo ÔÇö visibly proves A-F (A a sufficiently supported historical cohort; B an insufficient cohort ÔÇö small sample never strong despite 100% win rate; C a current opportunity evidence lookup + an honest NO_MATCH lookup; D a comparison between two cohorts; E serialization round trip for assessment/comparison/lookup/no-match-lookup; F explicit limitations/disclaimer) + determinism (repeated assessment identical; shuffled outcomes same id) + input immutability (outcomes not mutated) + existing pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged. Prints full strategy intelligence / comparison / lookup reports. Explicitly states evidence is NOT predictive. Ends with "Sprint 11Z demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
- tests/test_strategy_intelligence.py: 117 tests across 26 sections (A model construction/properties/members/rank-ordering/is-actionable/evidence-to-strategy-bijective/profile-available-dimensions/rank/empty/has-evidence/is-supported/matched/is_empty; B valid assessment assess_cohort/assess/counts-match/none-for-missing/reuses-by-reference; C classification propagation strong/moderate/weak/insufficient + status-matches-strength-for-each-strength; D lookup single-dimension/prefers-most-specific-composite/deterministic/rank-dimension; E missing-cohort lookup no-characteristic/key-absent/deterministic-id; F comparison both-present/7-metrics/exposes-evidence-strength/descriptive-deltas/symmetric-id; G missing cohort in comparison missing-a/missing-both/all-None; H deterministic ordering metrics/notes; I deterministic ids assessment/comparison/lookup/different-cohort/deterministic; J serialization round trip assessment/comparison/lookup-matched/lookup-no-match/bytes/deterministic/header/future-schema-rejected for all three; K input immutability outcomes/evidence-report/profile/reused-statistics-by-reference; L BOTH_TOUCHED win-rate-unavailable/mentioned-in-limitations; M NO_GEOMETRY metrics-unavailable/mentioned-in-limitations; N INSUFFICIENT_DATA metrics-unavailable/mentioned-in-limitations; O no-look-ahead no-candles-param/outcome-evaluator-patched-to-raise/pipeline-patched-to-raise/no-future-candles-inspected; P repeated evaluation assessment/lookup/comparison identical; Q shuffled-input determinism same-assessment-id/same-lookup-id; R 11X analytics unchanged performance-works/strategy-does-not-touch; S 11Y evidence unchanged evidence-works/strategy-does-not-touch; T pipeline regression baseline-4-3/strategy-does-not-touch; U reporting returns-str/required-sections/distinct-observed-evidence-interpretation/warning/unavailable/comparison/lookup/no-match/deterministic/precision-negative; V no statistical-superiority-language comparison-notes/assessment; W config validation defaults/frozen/slots/win-rate-bounds/profit-factor/avg-r/lookup-max-bounds/snapshot-sorted/lookup-max-limits-specificity; X model immutability frozen+slots assessment/comparison/profile/metric/lookup; Y sample-hard-gate small-sample-never-strong/insufficient-not-actionable/supported-requires-meaningful-sample; Z honest fallbacks missing-assessment-None/missing-lookup-no-assessment/comparison-missing-None/unavailable-win-rate-propagates/no-fabricated-R; end-to-end 11V->11W->11X->11Y->11Z chain from real fixture replay).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is strategy interpretation + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from the reused Sprint 11A-11Y engines; statistics + evidence classification reused not duplicated; the core status mapping is a fixed one-to-one reuse contract); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. A strategy assessment is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a buy/sell recommendation. Sample size is a HARD GATE (inherited from 11Y; small sample never strong regardless of observed win rate); the three concerns (observed result / evidence strength / strategy interpretation) kept distinct; BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); unavailable metrics remain None; comparison is descriptive only (no statistical-superiority claim; no winner manufactured). No future leakage (strategy consumes already-computed 11X/11Y evidence only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness, sorted/symmetric identities in ids). Serialization round-trips losslessly for all three result types. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); Sprint 11X analytics unchanged; Sprint 11Y evidence unchanged. No Sprint 11A-11Y regression.


## Sprint 12A ÔÇö Decision Intelligence Foundation (added)
This sprint is the FIRST Decision Intelligence sprint. It builds the structured decision-intelligence FOUNDATION layer that connects: CURRENT OPPORTUNITY -> OPPORTUNITY PROFILE -> HISTORICAL COHORT/EVIDENCE -> OBSERVED PERFORMANCE -> EVIDENCE STRENGTH -> STRATEGY INTERPRETATION -> DECISION INTELLIGENCE CONTEXT. Sprint 12A sits BELOW 11Z (consumes its already-computed outputs): 11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A. It is intelligence + reporting, NOT a new orchestration package (matching 11O-11Z convention); intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.decision_intelligence import DecisionIntelligenceEngine`. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-11Z public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 1893 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: answer "Given the characteristics of this CURRENT opportunity, what historical evidence is relevant, how strong is that evidence, and how should that evidence be presented to the existing decision process?". It is the FOUNDATION + INTEGRATION CONTRACT ÔÇö NOT the final automated decision-maker. 12B will handle deeper integration after this foundation is reviewed.
- CRITICAL DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö six strictly separate concerns, surfaced separately (NEVER collapsed into one score): (1) MARKET/OPPORTUNITY ANALYSIS (reused 11O-11U); (2) EXISTING DECISION/SCORING LOGIC (reused 11S/11T, represented WITHOUT alteration); (3) HISTORICAL OBSERVED PERFORMANCE (reused 11X HistoricalPerformanceStatistics); (4) EVIDENCE STRENGTH (reused 11Y EvidenceStrength); (5) STRATEGY INTERPRETATION (reused 11Z StrategyAssessmentStatus + interpretation/limitations); (6) DECISION INTELLIGENCE (new 12A DecisionContextStatus + factors + rationale). Sprint 12A does NOT replace the existing scoring system, does NOT alter existing signal decisions, does NOT recompute statistics, does NOT re-classify evidence, does NOT re-interpret strategy, does NOT re-evaluate outcomes, does NOT re-read candles, does NOT use future information. It is an additional EXPLAINABLE/AUDITABLE INFORMATION layer.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 11Z `OpportunityEvidenceLookup` (via `StrategyIntelligenceEngine.lookup`), which embeds the matched `HistoricalEvidenceCohort`, the reused 11X `HistoricalPerformanceStatistics`, the reused 11Y `EvidenceStrength` and the 11Z `StrategyEvidenceAssessment`. The 12A engine reuses the 11Z lookup verbatim (by reference); the surfaced observed_performance/evidence_strength/strategy_interpretation on the context ARE the SAME objects inside the lookup assessment (regression-tested: `ctx.observed_performance is lookup.assessment.observed_performance`).
- `src/engine/models/decision_intelligence.py` (frozen+slots): enum `DecisionContextStatus` (EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED/EVIDENCE_LIMITED/INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE/EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE ÔÇö describes the EVIDENCE CONTEXT, NOT the future trade outcome; rank_value/evidence_available/is_sufficient properties); the FIXED one-to-one `ASSESSMENT_TO_CONTEXT` mapping from the reused 11Z StrategyAssessmentStatus to DecisionContextStatus (STRONGER_HISTORICAL_SUPPORT/SUPPORTIVE_EVIDENCE -> EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED; LIMITED_EVIDENCE -> EVIDENCE_LIMITED; INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE -> INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE; NO_MATCH -> EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE ÔÇö the core reuse contract, NOT configurable); enum `DecisionEvidenceFactor` (HISTORICAL_SUPPORT_PRESENT/FAVORABLE_HISTORICAL_CHARACTERISTICS/HISTORICAL_CAUTION_PRESENT/UNFAVORABLE_HISTORICAL_CHARACTERISTICS/INSUFFICIENT_HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE/EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE ÔÇö descriptive TOKENS, NOT numerical weights, NOT a predictive score; rank_value ordering); `DecisionIntelligenceFactor` (factor + reason); `ExistingDecisionSummary` (read-only PROJECTION of the existing 11S/11T decision ÔÇö direction/decision_classification/decision_score/opportunity_status/rank/geometry_complete/confluence_score/risk_reward_ratio/entry/stop/target; empty-sentinel convention; has_decision property; REPRESENTS the existing decision WITHOUT changing it); `DecisionIntelligenceContext` (context_id="di-"+sha256[:16], profile, existing_decision, lookup, decision_context_status, factors, observed_performance/evidence_strength/strategy_interpretation surfaced as references to the lookup assessment objects, rationale, limitations, label, metadata; has_evidence/evidence_supported/matched/matched_cohort properties).
- `src/engine/config/decision_intelligence_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated): `DecisionIntelligenceConfig` ÔÇö DESCRIPTIVE-METRIC CONTEXT thresholds for factor wording ONLY (favorable_win_rate=0.55/favorable_profit_factor=1.3/favorable_avg_r=0.1/adverse_avg_r=0.0/adverse_profit_factor=1.0 with adverse<=favorable; label/metadata). The core ASSESSMENT_TO_CONTEXT mapping is intentionally NOT configurable (fixed reuse contract). The thresholds NEVER upgrade the decision context status, NEVER override the reused evidence strength. snapshot() for auditability.
- `src/engine/intelligence/decision_intelligence.py`: `DecisionIntelligenceEngine(config).build(profile, existing_decision, lookup, label, metadata) -> DecisionIntelligenceContext` (primary API ÔÇö takes an ALREADY-COMPUTED 11Z OpportunityEvidenceLookup) and `.build_from_report(report, profile, existing_decision, strategy_engine=None, label, metadata)` (convenience ÔÇö delegates the lookup to the reused/default StrategyIntelligenceEngine then delegates to build). Pure, deterministic, stateless, downstream-only. FACTOR DERIVATION: NO_MATCH -> EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE factor; EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED -> HISTORICAL_SUPPORT_PRESENT; EVIDENCE_LIMITED -> HISTORICAL_CAUTION_PRESENT; INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE -> INSUFFICIENT_HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE; FAVORABLE/UNFAVORABLE characteristic factors emitted ONLY when evidence is_sufficient (so a tiny cohort extreme win rate never produces a misleading characteristic ÔÇö sample-size hard gate inherited from 11Y). Factors ordered by rank_value.
- `src/engine/intelligence/decision_intelligence_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON, DECISION_INTELLIGENCE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1. serialize_context/serialize_context_bytes/deserialize_context/parse_decision_intelligence_header/canonical_context_json. CRITICAL: enums carry BOTH __enum__ (member name) AND __enum_class__ (class name) so the deserializer resolves the EXACT enum class unambiguously ÔÇö this matters because 12A enums share member names (DecisionContextStatus.EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE vs DecisionEvidenceFactor.EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE; DecisionContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE vs StrategyAssessmentStatus.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE). Lossless round trip. Schema-version checked before reconstruction; future versions rejected.
- `src/engine/reporting/decision_intelligence.py`: `DecisionIntelligenceFormatter(precision=2).format(context) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections: Decision Intelligence Report header, Current Opportunity, Existing Decision (represented without alteration), Historical Evidence, Observed Performance, Evidence Strength, Strategy Interpretation, Decision Context (status + factors), Limitations, Rationale. WARNING: "Historical evidence is descriptive and does not guarantee future performance. The existing decision / scoring logic is not modified by this context." No predictive/buy/sell/enter/exit/hold/probability/statistical-significance language. Following 11O-11Z convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- NO PIPELINE INTEGRATION: 12A is a foundation/information layer. It does NOT modify PipelineConfig, does NOT attach to PipelineResult, does NOT alter signals/decisions ÔÇö so existing pipeline behavior is 100% unchanged (signals=4, trades=3 regression trivially passes). NO AUTOMATED BUY/SELL DECISION: produces NO BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendations and NO automated order generation.
- scripts/test_decision_intelligence.py: demo ÔÇö visibly proves the 9 required demonstrations (supportive -> EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED; insufficient -> INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE [small sample not supported despite 100% win rate]; missing cohort -> EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE [no fabricated metrics]; weak -> EVIDENCE_LIMITED [HISTORICAL_CAUTION_PRESENT]; rationale; serialization round trip for all 4 variants; determinism [repeated + shuffled same id]; no-look-ahead [OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise; outcomes not mutated]; pipeline regression [signals=4, trades=3]). Ends with "Sprint 12A demo completed successfully (28 checks passed)." Demo exits 0.
- tests/test_decision_intelligence.py: 124 tests across 31 sections (A model construction; B current-opportunity-integration; C 11Z-lookup-reuse; D evidence-supported; E evidence-limited; F insufficient; G missing-cohort; H unavailable-metrics; I BOTH_TOUCHED; J NO_GEOMETRY; K INSUFFICIENT_DATA; L deterministic-ids; M factor-ordering; N repeated; O shuffled; P serialization; Q input-immutability; R no-look-ahead; S rationale; T limitations; U existing-decision-unchanged; V existing-scoring-unchanged; W existing-ranking-unchanged; X 11X-unchanged; Y 11Y-unchanged; Z 11Z-unchanged; AA reporting; AB config-validation; AC immutability; AD sample-hard-gate; AE honest-fallbacks; AF end-to-end 11V->11W->11X->11Y->11Z->12A chain).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package; NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-11Z engines; core status mapping is a fixed one-to-one reuse contract); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged; intelligence/__init__.py remains empty. No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters. No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API. A decision-intelligence context is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee, never a buy/sell/enter/exit/hold recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. Sample size is a HARD GATE (inherited from 11Y). Six concerns kept distinct; BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly; unavailable metrics remain None; insufficient evidence never transformed into a positive/negative trading decision. No future leakage (consumes already-computed 11Z evidence only; proven by patching outcome evaluator AND pipeline to raise). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; shuffle-invariant via 11Y/11Z sorted identities embedded in lookup_id). Serialization round-trips losslessly (class-qualified enum tags resolve shared member names). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 11X/11Y/11Z unchanged. No Sprint 11A-11Z regression.


## Sprint 12B ÔÇö Controlled Decision Intelligence Integration (added)
This sprint is the CONTROLLED INTEGRATION BOUNDARY layer. Sprint 12A introduced the decision-intelligence FOUNDATION (an evidence-aware, descriptive `DecisionIntelligenceContext` presented to the existing decision process WITHOUT altering it). Sprint 12B creates the explicit, auditable INTEGRATION BOUNDARY that attaches that decision-intelligence context to an EXISTING DECISION while preserving the existing decision EXACTLY as-is. The integration boundary is intentionally a CONTEXT / AUDIT layer, NOT a new decision algorithm. It is intelligence + reporting, NOT a new orchestration package (matching 11O-12A convention); `intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.decision_intelligence_integration import DecisionIntelligenceIntegrationEngine`. Architecture (unchanged in direction, 12B sits BELOW 12A): 11X Ôćĺ 11Y Ôćĺ 11Z Ôćĺ 12A Ôćĺ 12B. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12A public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified, and all 2017 prior tests still pass.
- Goal: answer "How can the existing decision pipeline consume decision-intelligence context in a controlled, auditable way WITHOUT the existing decision being modified, re-scored, re-ranked, re-classified, upgraded or replaced?". The integration explicitly distinguishes three concerns and keeps them separate: Existing Decision Ôćĺ Decision Intelligence Context (reused 12A, by reference) Ôćĺ Integrated Decision Context (12B). The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE; decision intelligence is CONTEXTUAL EVIDENCE, NOT a replacement decision. The integration makes it IMPOSSIBLE to accidentally interpret evidence-supported context as a new trading signal.
- CRITICAL DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö the existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE and preserved EXACTLY: `IntegratedDecisionContext.existing_decision` carries the ORIGINAL existing decision object BY REFERENCE (never modified, never re-scored, never re-ranked, never re-classified, never upgraded, never downgraded). Tests prove `integrated.existing_decision is original_decision` (reference identity preserved) and that the existing decision classification/score are unchanged after integration with EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED context (QUALIFIED stays QUALIFIED, never becomes PREFERRED). Evidence-supported context is NEVER interpreted as an upgrade (or downgrade) of the existing decision. There is NO BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation, NO probability, NO score adjustment, NO hidden weight.
- Reuses EXISTING intelligence (NOT duplicated): the Sprint 12A `DecisionIntelligenceContext` (retained BY REFERENCE, never mutated), which embeds the matched `HistoricalEvidenceCohort`, the reused 11X `HistoricalPerformanceStatistics`, the reused 11Y `EvidenceStrength`, the 11Z `StrategyAssessmentStatus` + `StrategyEvidenceAssessment`, the 12A `DecisionContextStatus` + factors + rationale + limitations, and the reused `OpportunityProfile` + `ExistingDecisionSummary`. The 12B engine reuses the 12A context verbatim; it does NOT recompute statistics, re-classify evidence, re-interpret strategy, rebuild cohort matching, re-evaluate outcomes, re-read candles, or use future information. Surfaced `evidence_status`/`strategy_interpretation`/`observed_performance`/`evidence_strength`/`contextual_factors` on the integration ARE the SAME objects inside the 12A context (regression-tested: `integ.observed_performance is di.observed_performance`).
- `src/engine/models/decision_intelligence_integration.py` (frozen+slots): enum `IntegrationStatus` (INTEGRATED/CONTEXT_ONLY/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID ÔÇö mutually exclusive, `rank_value`/`is_attached`/`is_integrated` properties); model `IntegratedDecisionContext` (integration_id="int-"+sha256[:16], existing_decision Any [original object, by reference], existing_decision_summary ExistingDecisionSummary [read-only projection for audit+serialization], profile OpportunityProfile, decision_intelligence DecisionIntelligenceContext|None [by reference], integration_status, evidence_status DecisionContextStatus|None, strategy_interpretation StrategyAssessmentStatus|None, observed_performance HistoricalPerformanceStatistics|None, evidence_strength EvidenceStrength|None, contextual_factors tuple [reused from 12A], rationale, limitations, label, metadata; properties has_decision_intelligence/has_existing_decision/has_evidence/evidence_supported). Optional fields use None so unavailable is never a real value. No business logic in models.
- `src/engine/config/decision_intelligence_integration_config.py` (frozen+slots, validated, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters): `DecisionIntelligenceIntegrationConfig` ÔÇö `strict` (default True; when True raises ValueError on an inconsistent opportunity profile between the existing decision and the DI context ÔÇö an AUDIT GUARD that prevents silently integrating a DI context computed for a DIFFERENT opportunity; when False records the inconsistency in rationale/limitations; the guard NEVER modifies the existing decision) + label/metadata + snapshot(). The `IntegrationStatus` assignment is a FIXED deterministic function of (existing-decision presence, DI presence, reused 12A DecisionContextStatus/lookup match) and is NOT configurable.
- `src/engine/intelligence/decision_intelligence_integration.py`: `DecisionIntelligenceIntegrationEngine(config).integrate(existing_decision, decision_intelligence, profile=None, label="", metadata=None) -> IntegratedDecisionContext`. Pure, deterministic, stateless, downstream-only. Public API takes NO candle/future-market-data argument. STATUS DERIVATION (deterministic, mutually exclusive, priority order): (1) no existing decision Ôćĺ INVALID (nothing to integrate into); (2) existing decision + no DI supplied (None) Ôćĺ UNAVAILABLE (no evidence fabricated); (3) existing decision + DI with NO available evidence (12A EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE / NO_MATCH) Ôćĺ CONTEXT_ONLY (informational context only; existing decision unchanged); (4) existing decision + DI with available evidence (matched cohort, 12A EVIDENCE_SUPPORTED/EVIDENCE_LIMITED/INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE) Ôćĺ INTEGRATED. EXISTING-DECISION PROJECTION: `_to_decision_summary` derives an `ExistingDecisionSummary` from the original object DEFENSIVELY via getattr (accepts an ExistingDecisionSummary [returned as-is, identity preserved], a Sprint 11S TradeDecision [extracts classification/score/geometry/etc.], a Sprint 11T TradeOpportunity, or None [empty summary]); the original object is NEVER mutated and is retained by reference. PROFILE CONSISTENCY GUARD: detects non-empty dimensions where the integration profile disagrees with the DI context profile; raises (strict) or records (non-strict); never modifies inputs. DETERMINISTIC ID: "int-"+sha256[:16] of canonical (existing-decision projection + 12A context_id + status + profile + factors + label + metadata); because the embedded 12A context_id is already shuffle-invariant (via 11Y/11Z), the integration id is shuffle-invariant for equivalent evidence.
- `src/engine/intelligence/decision_intelligence_integration_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`+`__enum_class__`/`__dataclass__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `INTEGRATION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_integration`/`serialize_integration_bytes`/`deserialize_integration`/`parse_integration_header`/`canonical_integration_json`. REUSES the Sprint 12A serializer's `_to_json` encoder and `_DATACLASSES`/`_ENUMS` maps (extended with `IntegrationStatus`) so every embedded 12A/11X/11Y/11Z model reconstructs exactly. Serialization scope (intentional, matching 11U/11W/11X/11Y/11Z/12A discipline): the lightweight integration projections are persisted IN FULL (integration_id, IntegrationStatus, ExistingDecisionSummary projection [by value], OpportunityProfile, the reused 12A DecisionIntelligenceContext [embedded by value: profile/match-status/matched-cohort/strategy-assessment/reused-statistics/strength/interpretation/12A-status/factors/rationale/limitations/label/metadata], surfaced evidence_status/strategy_interpretation/observed_performance/evidence_strength [by value], contextual_factors, rationale, limitations, label, metadata). The heavy ORIGINAL existing-decision object (e.g. a TradeDecision carrying candidate references) is retained BY REFERENCE on the LIVE result but is NOT persisted; on reload `existing_decision` reconstructs as None (regenerable by the caller) ÔÇö the `existing_decision_summary` projection preserves the audit view losslessly. Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `src/engine/reporting/decision_intelligence_integration.py`: `DecisionIntelligenceIntegrationFormatter(precision=2).format(context) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Required sections: Decision Intelligence Integration Report header (integration id, label, metadata), Existing Decision (represented without alteration ÔÇö shows original-object retention note + the read-only projection), Decision Intelligence (reused 12A context id / decision-context status / matched), Evidence Status, Strategy Interpretation, Observed Performance, Evidence Strength, Integration Status (status + is_attached), Contextual Factors, Limitations, Rationale. Unavailable values shown as "unavailable". Every report ends with the explicit WARNING: "Decision intelligence is contextual evidence attached to the existing decision. It is descriptive, does NOT guarantee future performance, does NOT constitute a trading recommendation, and does NOT modify the existing decision / scoring logic." No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Following the 11O-12A reporting convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- HONEST FALLBACKS (preserves 12A/11X/11Y/11Z semantics exactly via reused context): no DI supplied Ôćĺ UNAVAILABLE (evidence_status/strategy_interpretation/observed_performance/evidence_strength all None, contextual_factors () ÔÇö nothing fabricated); no existing decision Ôćĺ INVALID; no matching cohort Ôćĺ CONTEXT_ONLY (observed_performance None); BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous, realized_r None) never contributes to win/loss or R aggregates and is mentioned in limitations; NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no fabricated R values and are mentioned in limitations; unavailable metrics remain None (never converted to zero); observed zero never confused with not-available.
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the integration layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED 12A context and adds NO future information. (1) The public API `integrate(existing_decision, decision_intelligence, profile, label, metadata)` takes NO candle/future-market-data argument (signature inspected). (2) Works with `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` patched to raise (must not re-evaluate outcomes). (3) Works with `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise (must not re-run pipeline). (4) Mutating unrelated future candles does NOT change the integration (the engine never reads candles). (5) The existing decision / DI context / profile / evidence report / outcomes are taken verbatim, NEVER mutated. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): identical inputs always produce identical outputs; the integration_id hashes the canonical (existing-decision projection + 12A context_id + status + profile + factors + label/metadata) identity. Shuffled-equivalent outcomes produce the same integration id (via the 12A context_id which is shuffle-invariant via 11Y/11Z sorted identities). Two identical persisted datasets always produce the same integration status + id + factors.
- `scripts/test_decision_intelligence_integration.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves 9 demonstrations (successful integration INTEGRATED; existing decision preservation by reference `is`; context attachment DI reused by reference; unavailable intelligence UNAVAILABLE; context-only CONTEXT_ONLY; invalid INVALID + strict guard; determinism repeated + shuffled-input invariance; serialization round trip for all 4 statuses + heavy-ref-drops-on-load; full 11VÔćĺ11WÔćĺ11XÔćĺ11YÔćĺ11ZÔćĺ12AÔćĺ12B end-to-end chain with real evidence flowing to the boundary + no-look-ahead protection [OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise; outcomes not mutated] + pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged). Prints the full integration report. Explicitly states results are NOT predictions. Ends with "Sprint 12B demo completed successfully (36 checks passed)." Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_decision_intelligence_integration.py`: 115 tests across the required areas A-T (A valid integration; B existing decision preserved by reference `is` + projection-from-TradeDecision + classification-not-upgraded; C DI attached reused by reference + evidence/strategy/factors/observed-performance/evidence-strength surfaced as SAME objects; D integration status members/rank/ordering/INTEGRATED/CONTEXT_ONLY/CONTEXT_ONLY-evidence-unavailable; E missing intelligence UNAVAILABLE + no fabricated evidence + existing decision preserved; F invalid inputs INVALID + both-None + strict guard raises/consistent-allowed/non-strict-records/never-modifies-existing-decision; G deterministic ids repeated/label/decision/DI/invalid; H shuffle invariance same-id + same-status+factors; I serialization round-trip id+status+summary+DI+evidence+strategy+factors+rationale+metadata + heavy-ref-drops + context-only/unavailable/invalid + bytes + deterministic-bytes + canonical + header + future-schema-rejected + malformed-rejected; J input immutability existing-decision/DI/profile/outcomes/evidence-report + frozen; K no-look-ahead no-candle-param + OutcomeEvaluator-patched + pipeline-patched + no-future-info; L pipeline regression signals=4/trades=3; M 11X regression unchanged + not-touched; N 11Y regression unchanged + not-touched; O 11Z regression unchanged + not-touched; P 12A regression unchanged + not-touched + 12A-serialization-works; Q full 11VÔćĺ12B end-to-end supported/insufficient/no-match/serialization; R disclaimer/rationale mentions-status/not-modified/no-predictive/context-not-decision/no-recommendation/unavailable/invalid; S separation of concerns distinct-fields/no-single-score/existing-decision-separate-from-evidence/12A-context-separate-from-12B; T no recommendation no-buy-sell/limitations-explicit-no-recommendation/no-score-adjustment/no-upgrade-QUALIFIED-stays-QUALIFIED/no-downgrade/report-no-predictive-language/report-has-disclaimer/returns-str/required-sections/unavailable-shown/deterministic/precision-negative) + config defaults/frozen/snapshot/label-metadata + properties.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is an integration boundary + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from the reused Sprint 12A context, which itself reuses 11X/11Y/11Z; the existing decision is retained by reference and NEVER modified); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, matching 11O-12A convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. An integrated decision context is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee, never a buy/sell/enter/exit/hold recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/opportunity-selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE and preserved EXACTLY (by reference; classification/score/rank never upgraded/downgraded). The six concerns (market/opportunity, existing decision, observed performance, evidence strength, strategy interpretation, integrated decision context) kept distinct; BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); unavailable metrics remain None; no winner/score manufactured. No future leakage (integration consumes already-computed 12A context only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; shuffle-invariant via the 12A context_id embedded in the integration_id). Serialization round-trips losslessly for the audit view (heavy existing-decision ref drops on load, regenerable; ExistingDecisionSummary projection + 12A context + id + status preserved). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 11X/11Y/11Z/12A unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12A regression.

## Sprint 12C ÔÇö Robust Historical Backtesting & Adversarial Validation (added)
This sprint is the VALIDATION layer, NOT another intelligence / scoring layer. Sprint 12C asks: "Does the complete 11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A -> 12B architecture remain correct, deterministic, leak-free and statistically/accountingly consistent when subjected to broader historical replay and adversarial conditions?" The purpose is to establish a trustworthy backtesting/robustness foundation BEFORE Sprint 12D and 12E. Architecture (12C sits BELOW 12B; it consumes already-computed outputs of the existing chain): 11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A -> 12B -> 12C (this layer). It is intelligence + validation + reporting, NOT a new orchestration package (matching 11O-12B convention); `intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.backtest_validation import BacktestValidationEngine`. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12B public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files), and all 2132 prior tests still pass.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö reuse, do not re-invent: The validation layer REUSES the existing Sprint 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B engines (PerformanceAnalyticsEngine, HistoricalEvidenceEngine, StrategyIntelligenceEngine, DecisionIntelligenceEngine, DecisionIntelligenceIntegrationEngine) and, for the look-ahead checks, patches Sprint 11W `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` and Sprint 11F `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to raise. It performs INDEPENDENT CROSS-CHECKS of the already-computed outputs; it NEVER duplicates the trading/outcome/analytics/evidence/decision logic. Where accounting identities must be checked, the layer recomputes the EXPECTED values directly from the raw Sprint 11W `HistoricalOutcome` objects via a deliberately separate, simple validator `_recompute_statistics` (mirrors the Sprint 11X accounting rules EXACTLY so it is a meaningful cross-check, but never calls the Sprint 11X engine a second time to "check itself").
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö no leakage introduced: The validation engine NEVER supplies future candles to any component not supposed to receive them.
