The look-ahead/point-in-time checks PATCH `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` AND `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to raise, then re-run the downstream 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B chain and confirm no component attempts to access candles (proves the downstream chain has no hidden candle dependency).
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö no modification of existing decision semantics: The decision-authority checks build existing decision objects with each Sprint 11S classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED), integrate each with strong/weak/unavailable decision intelligence, and verify the existing decision classification is NEVER upgraded/downgraded/modified by the Sprint 12B integration (the existing decision is retained by reference; `is` identity preserved).
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö honest reporting: A check that cannot be performed (empty scenario, no outcomes) is reported as SKIPPED with a descriptive reason ÔÇö never a fake PASS. A failed check reports the specific failure detail. The overall status is PASS only when every non-skipped check passed and at least one check ran; FAIL if any check failed; SKIPPED if every check was skipped.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö deterministic: Identical scenarios always produce identical validation results. No wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration. The validation_id = "validation-"+sha256[:16] of canonical (label + metadata + sorted scenarios + checks) identity; scenarios ordered by name, checks ordered by (category, name), categories by name, outcome distribution by status name. Shuffled-equivalent outcome inputs yield the same downstream ids (inherited from 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B shuffle-invariance).
- `src/engine/config/backtest_validation_config.py`: `BacktestValidationConfig` (frozen+slots, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters): `accounting_tolerance` (default 1e-9, non-negative; a floating-point tolerance for R-aggregate reconciliation ONLY, never weakens count/status/evidence comparisons), `label`, `metadata`, `snapshot()`.
- `src/engine/models/backtest_validation.py` (frozen+slots): enums `ValidationCheckStatus` (PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED), `ValidationCategory` (14 categories mirroring scope areas A-O). Models `CheckResult` (name/category/status/detail), `ScenarioResult` (name/outcome_count/checks; `passed`/`failed_checks` properties), `CategorySummary` (category + total/passed/failed/skipped; `passed_category`), `BacktestValidationReport` (validation_id="validation-"+sha256[:16], scenarios, categories, label, metadata, scenario_count, check_count, passed_count, failed_count, skipped_count, overall_status, determinism_status, look_ahead_status, accounting_status, serialization_status, decision_authority_status, outcome_distribution, rationale; `passed`/`is_empty` properties).
- `src/engine/intelligence/backtest_validation.py`: `BacktestValidationEngine(config).validate(scenarios, label, metadata) -> BacktestValidationReport`. Each scenario is a mapping with `name` + `outcomes` (tuple of Sprint 11W `HistoricalOutcome`), optionally `profile` (OpportunityProfile for 12Z/12A/12B checks), `decision` (existing decision), `expected_classification`. Stateless across calls; inputs NEVER mutated; result DESCRIPTIVE. Check functions (module-level, returning `CheckResult`): `_check_scenario_construction` (well-formed HistoricalOutcome entries), `_check_accounting` (overall statistics reconcile independently), `_check_breakdown_accounting` (every breakdown group reconciles independently across all 7 dimensions), `_check_r_identity` (gross_positive+gross_negative reconcile with total_realized_r; no fabricated values), `_check_excluded_outcomes` (BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA contribute no R; valid_r_count reconciles), `_check_cohort_reconciliation` (sum of breakdown counts == overall total; no drop/double-count), `_check_determinism` (repeated 11X/11Y/11Z/12A ids identical), `_check_shuffle_invariance` (shuffled input identical ids+results), `_check_immutability` (source outcomes not mutated by downstream), `_check_look_ahead` (patch OutcomeEvaluator+pipeline to raise; downstream chain still works), `_check_serialization` (11Y evidence + 12A context + 12B integration round trips preserve identity/status/statistics), `_check_evidence_gating` (tiny impressive cohort stays INSUFFICIENT through 11Y->11Z->12A->12B; 12B never upgrades existing decision), `_check_decision_authority` (existing classification preserved by reference under every evidence regime). Also exports `_recompute_statistics` (independent validator used by tests/demo).
- `src/engine/intelligence/backtest_validation_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`+`__enum_class__`/`__dataclass__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `BACKTEST_VALIDATION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_validation`/`serialize_validation_bytes`/`deserialize_validation`/`parse_validation_header`/`canonical_validation_json`. Round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (validation_id, overall + per-category statuses, scenario/check counts, outcome distribution, label, metadata, rationale). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `src/engine/reporting/backtest_validation.py`: `BacktestValidationFormatter(width=60).format(report) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections: Backtest Validation Report header (validation id, label, metadata), Validation Status (overall + scenario/check counts + determinism/look-ahead/accounting/serialization/decision-authority statuses), Outcome Distribution, Category Summary, Scenario Detail (per-scenario per-check PASS/FAIL/SKIP + detail), Rationale. Every report ends with the explicit DISCLAIMER "Historical backtesting and robustness results are descriptive validation outputs. They do not predict future market behavior and do not guarantee profitability." No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Following 11O-12B convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- HONEST FALLBACKS (preserves 11W/11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B semantics exactly via reused engines + independent recomputation): empty scenario -> SKIPPED checks (never fake PASS); BOTH_TOUCHED (ambiguous, realized_r None) never contributes to win/loss or R aggregates; NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no fabricated R values; tiny cohort (sample < 11Y min_sample_total) stays INSUFFICIENT regardless of observed win rate; 12B never modifies the existing decision classification/score/rank; unavailable metrics remain None (never converted to zero); observed zero never confused with not-available.
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): the validation layer consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED outcomes/objects and adds NO future information. (1) The validation engine takes scenarios of `HistoricalOutcome` objects, never candles. (2) Works with `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` patched to raise (downstream 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B must not re-evaluate outcomes). (3) Works with `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise (downstream must not re-run pipeline). (4) Source outcomes are taken verbatim, NEVER mutated (regression-tested). The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- `scripts/test_backtest_validation.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 12 required demonstrations (mixed historical replay; accounting reconciliation; point-in-time protection [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise]; look-ahead protection; shuffle invariance; deterministic repeated evaluation; serialization round trip; immutability; adversarial scenarios [enormous win/loss, tiny impressive cohort, all BOTH_TOUCHED, all NO_GEOMETRY, empty, single-outcome]; evidence hard-gate preservation [1-trade +2R 100%-win cohort stays INSUFFICIENT through 11Y->11Z->12A->12B]; 12B decision authority preservation [QUALIFIED/PREFERRED/WATCH/REJECTED never modified under every evidence regime]; complete 11V->12B->12C chain) + pipeline regression baseline 4/3 unchanged. Prints the full Backtest Validation Report. Explicitly states results are NOT predictions. Ends with "Sprint 12C demo completed successfully (33 checks passed)." Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_backtest_validation.py`: 102 tests across the required areas A-O (A scenario construction empty-skipped/well-formed-passes/non-outcome-fails; B end-to-end replay full-matrix-passes/scenario-count/outcome-distribution; C point-in-time look-ahead-status-pass/evaluator-patched/pipeline-patched; D look-ahead no-hidden-candle-dependency/chain-consumes-outcomes-only; E accounting overall/target-hit-r/stop-hit-r/expired-r/both-touched-no-r/no-geometry-no-r/insufficient-data/gross-positive+negative-reconciles/valid-r-count/breakdown-groups/across-dimensions; F cohort breakdown-totals-equal-overall/no-drop-double-count/no-status-overlap/unavailable-rank; G shuffle analytics-id/evidence-id/12a-context-id/check-passes; H deterministic repeated-validation/analytics-id/validation-id-prefixed/different-scenarios-different-id/check-passes; I serialization round-trip-id/status/scenarios/deterministic-bytes/canonical/schema-version/header/future-schema-rejected/empty; J immutability outcomes/evidence/12a-context/12b-integration/reference-identity; K adversarial enormous-win/loss/many-tiny-wins/losses/100%-tiny-insufficient/0%-tiny-insufficient/all-both-touched/all-no-geometry/empty/single/duplicate/same-instrument/mixed-ranks/missing-metadata/extreme-numerics/zero-risk/long-expiration; L evidence-gating tiny-never-strong/chain-preserves-insufficient/check-passes/sufficient-can-be-strong; M decision authority qualified/preferred/watch/rejected-preserved-under-strong-evidence/unavailable/no-match/check-passes; N end-to-end full-chain-passes/no-lookahead-introduced/pipeline-regression-4-3/chain-11v-to-12c; O report returns-str/required-sections/disclaimer/no-predictive-language/skipped-shown/deterministic/empty/negative-width; model+config immutability frozen/negative-tolerance-rejected/snapshot-sorted/category-members/check-status-properties; package surface engine/serialization/models/reporting/config importable + intelligence/__init__-empty).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is validation + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-12B engines; accounting recomputed by an INDEPENDENT validator from raw outcomes, never by re-invoking the Sprint 11X engine); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, matching 11O-12B convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. Backtest validation results are DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a buy/sell recommendation. BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); tiny cohort never strong regardless of observed win rate (11Y hard-gate inherited); 12B never modifies existing decision (classification/score/rank preserved by reference). No future leakage (validation consumes already-computed outcomes only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; shuffle-invariant via inherited 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B sorted identities). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12B regression.

## Sprint 12D ÔÇö Robustness, Failure-Mode & Edge-Case Hardening (added)
This sprint is the downstream VALIDATION/HARDENING layer. Sprint 12C asked "Does the complete architecture remain correct under broader historical replay and adversarial conditions?"; Sprint 12D asks "Does it remain correct and safe under difficult boundary conditions, malformed-but-representable inputs, empty data, partial data, serialization edge cases, unusual cohort structures, deterministic replay variations, and failure isolation?". The goal is NOT a new scoring/prediction/recommendation/intelligence layer ÔÇö only to harden the system without changing the semantics or authority of any previous sprint. Architecture (12D sits BELOW 12C; it consumes already-computed outputs of the existing chain): 11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A -> 12B -> 12C -> 12D (this layer). It is intelligence + validation + reporting, NOT a new orchestration package (matching 11O-12C convention); `intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.robustness_validation import RobustnessValidationEngine`. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12C public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files + the shared 12C `_recompute_statistics`/`_stats_equal`/`_breakdown_for` helpers are IMPORTED, not modified), and all 2234 prior tests still pass.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö reuse, do not re-invent: The 12D engine REUSES the existing Sprint 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B engines AND the Sprint 12C independent accounting validator (`_recompute_statistics`/`_stats_equal`/`_breakdown_for` imported from `engine.intelligence.backtest_validation` ÔÇö SINGLE source of truth for the accounting rules). It performs INDEPENDENT CROSS-CHECKS of already-computed outputs; it NEVER duplicates trading/outcome/analytics/evidence/decision logic. Where accounting is checked, it recomputes EXPECTED values directly from raw 11W `HistoricalOutcome` objects (the 12C independent validator) and compares to 11X reported statistics ÔÇö it does NOT call the 11X engine a second time to "check itself".
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö no leakage introduced: The look-ahead/point-in-time checks PATCH `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` AND `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` to raise, then re-run the downstream 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B chain and confirm no component attempts to access candles (proves the downstream chain has no hidden candle dependency). The 12D public API `validate(scenarios, label, metadata)` takes NO candle/future-market-data argument.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö no modification of existing decision semantics: Existing decisions remain AUTHORITATIVE (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED never upgraded/downgraded/re-scored/re-ranked by 12D). The integration-isolation + cross-layer checks verify the 12B integration retains the existing decision BY REFERENCE and never modifies its classification.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö honest reporting: A check that cannot be performed is `SKIPPED`/`UNAVAILABLE`/`INVALID` with a descriptive reason ÔÇö never a fake `PASS`. A SKIPPED check is always distinguishable from PASS. The overall status is `PASS` only when every non-skipped check passed and at least one check ran; `FAIL` if any check failed; `SKIPPED` if every check was not-run.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö failure isolation: A failure in one validation scenario must not corrupt unrelated scenarios. Each scenario is validated independently via the per-scenario check runner; the engine never shares mutable state across scenarios. The failure-isolation check validates each scenario independently and confirms a malformed/invalid scenario does not crash the others.
- `src/engine/config/robustness_validation_config.py`: `RobustnessValidationConfig` (frozen+slots, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters): `accounting_tolerance` (default 1e-9, non-negative; floating-point tolerance for R-aggregate reconciliation ONLY, never weakens count/status/evidence comparisons), `evidence_min_sample_total` (mirrors 11Y default 30; used ONLY for descriptive boundary-scenario context, NEVER overrides the real 11Y classification), `evidence_strong_min_sample` (mirrors 11Y default 50; >= min_sample_total), `lookup_max_dimensions` (mirrors 11Z default 2; in [1,2]; descriptive lookup-cap context only), `label`/`metadata` + `snapshot()`.
- `src/engine/models/robustness_validation.py` (frozen+slots): enum `RobustnessCheckStatus` (PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID ÔÇö UNAVAILABLE = required upstream artifact absent; INVALID = malformed-but-representable input rejected by the public contract; both NEVER silently converted to PASS; SKIPPED/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID all count as "not-ran" and are distinguishable from PASS), enum `RobustnessCategory` (15 categories mirroring scope areas A-M + pipeline-regression + no-look-ahead). Models `RobustnessCheckResult` (name/category/status/detail; `passed`/`failed`/`ran`/`skipped`/`not_run` properties), `RobustnessScenarioResult` (name/outcome_count/checks/edge_case; `passed` = every non-skipped check passed AND >=1 ran; `failed_checks`/`not_run_count`), `RobustnessCategorySummary`, `RobustnessValidationReport` (validation_id="robustness-"+sha256[:16]; scenarios/categories/counts; overall + per-area statuses: determinism/look_ahead/accounting/serialization/integration/cross_layer/pipeline_regression; outcome_distribution; edge_case_coverage; `report_checks` tuple for REPORT-LEVEL checks [pipeline regression + failure isolation] that count toward totals + category summaries but do NOT affect any scenario's `passed` property ÔÇö so an empty scenario cannot auto-pass merely because the global pipeline baseline is intact; rationale; `limitations` = the fixed `ROBUSTNESS_VALIDATION_LIMITATIONS` constant, NOT configurable). `ROBUSTNESS_VALIDATION_LIMITATIONS` = "Robustness validation verifies implementation invariants and historical accounting behavior; it does not establish predictive validity, statistical significance, or future profitability. Results are descriptive only."
- `src/engine/intelligence/robustness_validation.py`: `RobustnessValidationEngine(config).validate(scenarios, label="", metadata=None) -> RobustnessValidationReport`. Each scenario is a mapping with `name` + `outcomes` (tuple of 11W `HistoricalOutcome`), optionally `profile` (OpportunityProfile), `decision` (existing decision), `edge_case` (descriptive tag). Stateless across calls; inputs NEVER mutated; result DESCRIPTIVE. Per-scenario check functions (module-level, returning `RobustnessCheckResult`): `_check_empty_minimal` (A: zero/single/all-excluded outcomes honest ÔÇö no fabricated counts/win_rate/R/div-by-zero; non-HistoricalOutcome entries reported INVALID via the scenario-construction guard and remaining checks skipped), `_check_boundary_sample_size` (B: below-11Y-min-sample cohort must be INSUFFICIENT regardless of observed win rate; at/above-min descriptive PASS), `_check_mixed_contamination` (C: BOTH_TOUCHED never win/loss; NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA carry no R; valid_r_count reconciles; overall stats reconcile via the 12C independent validator), `_check_adversarial_cohorts` (D: breakdown totals == overall; cohort keys come from actual outcome dimension values ÔÇö no invented metadata; unavailable sentinel sorts last), `_check_lookup_matching` (E: repeated lookup deterministic; matched spec uses ONLY profile-available dimensions; NO_MATCH fabricates no cohort/assessment), `_check_integration_isolation` (F: 12B retains existing decision by reference under available + unavailable intelligence; unavailable intelligence -> IntegrationStatus.UNAVAILABLE; classification never modified), `_check_serialization_adversarial` (G: 11Y evidence + 12A context + 12B integration round trips preserve id/status/statistics; deserialize->serialize stable; malformed schema-version payload rejected), `_check_determinism_shuffle` (H: repeated + shuffled [seeded] evaluation produce identical ids + overall stats), `_check_input_immutability` (I: source outcomes not mutated; reference identity preserved across integration), `_check_failure_isolation` (J: each scenario validated independently via the per-scenario runner [NOT the public validate API, which would recurse]; malformed scenario does not crash others ÔÇö REPORT-LEVEL check), `_check_cross_layer_consistency` (K: INSUFFICIENT stays INSUFFICIENT through 11Y->11Z->12A; existing decision stays authoritative through 12B; below-min sample not promoted at any layer), `_check_accounting_invariants` (L: overall stats reconcile; status counts reconcile with raw outcome statuses; valid_r_count reconciles; gross_positive+negative == total; breakdown totals == overall; excluded outcomes carry no R ÔÇö all via the 12C independent validator), `_check_reporting_honesty` (M: 11Y/11X reports use no predictive/guarantee/buy-sell/enter-exit-hold/recommendation language [phrase-matched so "thresholds" is not a false-positive for "hold"]; descriptive disclaimer present), `_check_no_look_ahead` (O: downstream chain works with OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise), `_check_pipeline_regression` (N: pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3 ÔÇö REPORT-LEVEL check). The scenario-construction guard at the start of `_run_scenario_checks` reports non-HistoricalOutcome entries as INVALID and skips remaining checks for that scenario (never crashes the engine).
- REPORT-LEVEL vs SCENARIO-LEVEL checks: `failure_isolation` and `pipeline_regression` are REPORT-LEVEL checks stored in `RobustnessValidationReport.report_checks` (NOT attached to any scenario). They count toward `check_count`/`passed_count`/`failed_count`/`skipped_count` and the category summaries, but do NOT affect any scenario's `passed` property. This preserves the honest semantics that an empty scenario cannot auto-pass merely because the global pipeline baseline is intact.
- `src/engine/intelligence/robustness_validation_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON (`__enum__`+`__enum_class__`/`__dataclass__`/`__tuple__` type tags), `ROBUSTNESS_VALIDATION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_robustness`/`serialize_robustness_bytes`/`deserialize_robustness`/`parse_robustness_header`/`canonical_robustness_json`. Round trip is LOSSLESS for every field (validation_id, overall + per-area statuses, scenarios + per-scenario checks, categories, counts, outcome_distribution, edge_case_coverage, report_checks, label, metadata, rationale, limitations). Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes. Deserialize->serialize stable.
- `src/engine/reporting/robustness_validation.py`: `RobustnessValidationFormatter(width=60).format(report) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections: Robustness Validation Report header (validation id, label, metadata), Validation Status (overall + scenario/check counts + determinism/look-ahead/accounting/serialization/integration-isolation/cross-layer/pipeline-regression statuses), Edge-Case Coverage, Outcome Distribution, Category Summary, Scenario Detail (per-scenario per-check PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID + detail), Report-Level Checks, Rationale, Limitations. Every report ends with the explicit `ROBUSTNESS_VALIDATION_LIMITATIONS` disclaimer. No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Skipped/unavailable/invalid shown explicitly and NEVER counted as PASS. Following 11O-12C convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- HONEST FALLBACKS (preserves 11W/11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B/12C semantics exactly via reused engines + independent recomputation): empty scenario -> all own checks not-run (scenario does NOT auto-pass); single outcome -> no div-by-zero; all-BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA -> no fabricated win_rate/R/valid_r; non-HistoricalOutcome entry -> INVALID (remaining checks skipped, engine never crashes); tiny impressive cohort stays INSUFFICIENT through the full chain (11Y hard-gate inherited); 12B never modifies existing decision classification (retained by reference); unavailable intelligence -> IntegrationStatus.UNAVAILABLE; NO_MATCH lookup fabricates nothing; unavailable metrics remain None (never converted to zero); observed zero never confused with not-available.
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): 12D is downstream-only. (1) The public API `validate(scenarios, label, metadata)` takes NO candle/future-market-data argument (signature inspected). (2) Works with `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` patched to raise. (3) Works with `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise. (4) Source outcomes taken verbatim, NEVER mutated. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): identical scenarios always produce identical results; the validation_id hashes the SORTED canonical (label + metadata + sorted scenarios [name/outcome_count/edge_case/checks] + report_checks) identity so a shuffled scenario order yields the same id. Shuffled-equivalent outcomes produce the same downstream ids (inherited from 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B shuffle-invariance).
- `scripts/test_robustness_validation.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all A-M demonstrations (empty/minimal input behavior; evidence sample-size boundary; mixed outcome statuses; adversarial cohort matching; lookup robustness; 12A/12B integration failure isolation; serialization round trip; determinism + shuffle invariance; input immutability; cross-layer consistency; accounting invariants; pipeline regression baseline 4/3; no-look-ahead [evaluator + pipeline patched to raise; OutcomeEvaluator restored]) + full robustness validation report. Every demo check prints explicit PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED; SKIPPED never counted as PASS; exits non-zero on failures. Ends with "Sprint 12D demo completed successfully (45 checks passed)." Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_robustness_validation.py`: 133 tests across areas A-N (A empty/minimal: empty-skipped/single-no-divbyzero/all-both-touched/no-geometry/insufficient-data/non-outcome-INVALID/non-outcome-no-crash/empty-distribution; B boundary: below-min-insufficient/at-min/above-min/100pct-wins-insufficient/100pct-losses-insufficient/excellent-r-insufficient/later-layers-no-promote; C mixed: all-target/target+stop/both-touched-excluded/no-geometry-no-r/insufficient-data-no-r/expired-valid-r/full-mix; D adversarial: same-instrument-diff-directions/same-direction-diff-instruments/overlapping-single/composite/composite-insufficient/unavailable-metadata/unknown-alignment/no-invented-metadata; E lookup: no-match-no-fabrication/single-dimension/composite/more-specific/dimension-cap/unavailable-metadata/deterministic/no-match-fabricates-nothing; F integration: missing-decision-UNAVAILABLE/sufficient-preserves/insufficient-preserves/no-match-preserves/unavailable-preserves/by-reference/all-classifications; G serialization: round-trip/empty-skipped/ids-preserved/stable/canonical/malformed-rejected/missing-schema-rejected/header/schema-version-1/bytes/unavailable-preserved; H determinism: repeated/empty-skipped/same-id/shuffled-same-id/status-pass/shuffled-same-overall; I immutability: not-mutated/check-passes/empty-skipped/reference-identity/repeated-no-mutation; J failure-isolation: two-scenarios/single-skipped/invalid-no-corrupt/empty-plus-valid; K cross-layer: sufficient/insufficient-stays/authoritative/empty-skipped/contextual-not-decision/below-min-no-promote; L accounting: mixed/empty-skipped/status-counts/valid-r/gross-pos-neg/breakdown-totals/ambiguous-excluded/unavailable-remain/status-pass; M reporting: passes/empty-skipped/no-predictive/limitations/no-buy-sell/distinguishes-statuses/shows-skipped; N pipeline-regression/no-look-ahead/model/config/end-to-end: baseline-4-3/check-pass/check-attached[report_checks]/no-look-ahead-passes/empty-skipped/evaluator-patched/status-pass/no-candle-param/model-members/frozen/limitations-constant/config-defaults/validation/frozen/snapshot/full-matrix/id-prefix/different-scenarios-different-id/repeated-same-id/shuffled-scenarios-same-id/edge-coverage/distribution/rationale-descriptive/no-global-state).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is validation + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-12C engines; accounting recomputed by the SHARED 12C independent validator imported, not duplicated); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, matching 11O-12C convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files; the 12C helpers are IMPORTED not modified). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. Robustness validation results are DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a buy/sell/enter/exit/hold recommendation. BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); tiny cohort never strong regardless of observed win rate (11Y hard-gate inherited); 12B never modifies existing decision (classification/score/rank preserved by reference); empty scenario never auto-passes; non-HistoricalOutcome entries reported INVALID (never crash). No future leakage (12D consumes already-computed outcomes only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; shuffle-invariant via inherited 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B sorted identities + sorted scenario/report-check identity in the validation_id). Serialization round-trips losslessly. Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B/12C unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12C regression.


## Sprint 12E ÔÇö PRODUCTION INTEGRATION + FINAL HARDENING (added)
This is the FINAL PLANNED SPRINT of the current architecture. Do NOT create Sprint 12F. Sprint 12E is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer and NOT a new orchestration package. It is the smallest clean PRODUCTION INTEGRATION BOUNDARY that bundles the ALREADY-COMPUTED outputs of the completed architecture (Sprint 11V through 12D) into ONE coherent, inspectable, production-facing artifact WITHOUT altering the meaning of any previous layer. Architecture (12E sits BELOW 12D; it consumes already-computed outputs of the existing chain): 11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A -> 12B -> 12C -> 12D -> 12E (this layer). It is intelligence + integration + reporting, NOT a new orchestration package (matching 11O-12D convention); `intelligence/__init__.py` stays intentionally empty ÔÇö import via `from engine.intelligence.production_intelligence import ProductionIntelligenceEngine`. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12D public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files), and all 2367 prior tests still pass.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö reuse, do not re-invent: The 12E engine REUSES the Sprint 12B `IntegratedDecisionContext` (which itself embeds the reused 12A decision-intelligence context, the reused 11Z strategy assessment, the reused 11Y evidence strength and the reused 11X statistics). It OPTIONALLY attaches the ALREADY-COMPUTED Sprint 12C `BacktestValidationReport` and/or Sprint 12D `RobustnessValidationReport` as OFFLINE / HISTORICAL validation state. It does NOT recompute statistics, re-classify evidence, re-interpret strategy, rebuild cohort matching, re-evaluate outcomes, re-read candles, re-run the pipeline, re-run validation or use future information. It only BUNDLES.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö the existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE: The engine retains the 12B integrated context BY REFERENCE and NEVER modifies it (and therefore never modifies the original existing decision it carries). The production integration status is MIRRORED from the reused 12B `IntegrationStatus` (never recomputed, never overridden, never made configurable ÔÇö fixed one-to-one `_INTEGRATION_STATUS_MIRROR`). There is NO BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation, NO probability, NO score adjustment, NO hidden weight, NO re-ranking and NO re-selection. Evidence-supported context is NEVER interpreted as an upgrade (or downgrade) of the existing decision.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö historical vs live boundary (CRITICAL): The production runtime NEVER runs historical replay against future candles. The validation reports attached here are PRE-COMPUTED OFFLINE artifacts (produced by the 12C/12D engines over historical outcome corpora); 12E merely REFERENCES them by reference. The engine's public API takes NO candle / future-market-data argument. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged. (Reuses the established 12B boundary: a production caller supplies an ALREADY-COMPUTED 12B integrated context; 12E never reaches back into candles.)
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö eight separated concerns, never collapsed into one score: The production context surfaces, WITHOUT merging: (1) Market/opportunity information (reused OpportunityProfile), (2) Existing decision (reused ExistingDecisionSummary), (3) Historical observed performance (reused 11X statistics), (4) Evidence strength (reused 11Y EvidenceStrength), (5) Strategy interpretation (reused 11Z StrategyAssessmentStatus), (6) Decision intelligence context (reused 12A DecisionIntelligenceContext), (7) Controlled integration status (reused 12B IntegrationStatus mirrored), (8) Validation/robustness state (optional 12C/12D reports). These are independently inspectable through the reused 12B context (`integrated_context`) + delegating read-only properties + the optional validation references.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö honest fallbacks: No integrated context supplied (None) -> `INVALID`; nothing fabricated. Integrated context present but 12B status `UNAVAILABLE`/`CONTEXT_ONLY` -> surfaced unchanged; no evidence fabricated. No matching cohort -> observed_performance/evidence_strength/strategy_interpretation all None (never fabricated). No validation reports supplied -> `ProductionValidationState.NONE`; the production result notes that no offline validation state is attached (never a fake PASS). Validation reports carry their own honest SKIPPED/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID statuses (inherited from 12C/12D); 12E never converts a SKIPPED into a PASS. BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); unavailable metrics remain None; observed zero never confused with not-available. Empty inputs do not become successful results.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö fail-safe error handling: Type-mismatched inputs raise `TypeError` explicitly (integrated_context must be a 12B `IntegratedDecisionContext` or None; backtest_validation must be a 12C `BacktestValidationReport` or None; robustness_validation must be a 12D `RobustnessValidationReport` or None). A type error does NOT corrupt the engine (error-isolation regression-tested). Errors are never converted into apparently successful decisions.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö deterministic: Identical inputs always produce identical outputs. No wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration. The production_id = "prod-"+sha256[:16] of the canonical identity (12B integration id + 12C/12D validation ids + validation state + production status + label + metadata). Because the embedded 12B integration id is already shuffle-invariant (via 11Y/11Z), the production id is shuffle-invariant for equivalent evidence. Two identical persisted datasets always produce the same production id.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö input immutability + reference identity: The 12B integrated context, the 12C/12D validation reports and the original existing decision are retained BY REFERENCE and NEVER mutated (regression-tested: `ctx.existing_decision is original`, `ctx.integrated_context is integ`, `ctx.backtest_validation is bvr`, `ctx.robustness_validation is rvr`). The 12E contract reuses objects by reference exactly where the 12B/12C/12D contracts do; 12E never accidentally copies or replaces them.
- DESIGN PRINCIPLE ÔÇö preserve 11Y hard gate: A tiny cohort with excellent historical performance remains INSUFFICIENT through the full 11Y->11Z->12A->12B->12E chain (regression-tested: 1-trade +2R 100%-win cohort stays INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE; 12E never promotes it). The 11Y sample-size hard gate is inherited unchanged via the reused 12B context.
- `src/engine/config/production_intelligence_config.py`: `ProductionIntelligenceConfig` (frozen+slots, MINIMAL ÔÇö NO strategy/optimization/scoring parameters): `label`/`metadata` + `snapshot()`. The integration status semantics, the EVIDENCE_TO_STRATEGY mapping, the ASSESSMENT_TO_CONTEXT mapping and the 12B integration-status semantics are ALL intentionally NOT configurable (fixed reuse contracts).
- `src/engine/models/production_intelligence.py` (frozen+slots): enum `ProductionIntegrationStatus` (INTEGRATED/CONTEXT_ONLY/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID ÔÇö MIRRORED from the reused 12B `IntegrationStatus`; `rank_value`/`is_attached`/`is_integrated` properties), enum `ProductionValidationState` (NONE/BACKTEST_VALIDATION/ROBUSTNESS_VALIDATION/FULL_VALIDATION ÔÇö descriptive of which offline reports are attached; `has_validation` property). Model `ProductionIntelligenceContext` (production_id="prod-"+sha256[:16], integrated_context `IntegratedDecisionContext|None` [reused by reference], backtest_validation `BacktestValidationReport|None` [reused by reference], robustness_validation `RobustnessValidationReport|None` [reused by reference], integration_status, validation_state, rationale, limitations, label, metadata; delegating read-only properties surface the eight concerns from the reused 12B context: existing_decision/existing_decision_summary/profile/decision_intelligence/evidence_status/strategy_interpretation/observed_performance/evidence_strength/contextual_factors/has_evidence/evidence_supported; has_integrated_context/has_validation/is_empty properties). `PRODUCTION_INTELLIGENCE_LIMITATIONS` = the fixed, non-configurable disclaimer ("Production intelligence is a coherent bundle of already-computed descriptive artifacts; it does NOT establish predictive validity, statistical significance, or future profitability, does NOT constitute a trading recommendation, and does NOT modify the existing decision / scoring logic. Historical evidence and offline validation are descriptive and do not guarantee future performance.").
- `src/engine/intelligence/production_intelligence.py`: `ProductionIntelligenceEngine(config).assemble(integrated_context, backtest_validation=None, robustness_validation=None, label="", metadata=None) -> ProductionIntelligenceContext`. Pure orchestration/bundle; stateless across calls; inputs NEVER mutated; result DESCRIPTIVE. Public API takes NO candle/future-market-data argument. `_mirror_status` mirrors the reused 12B `IntegrationStatus` into the production status (never recomputed). `_validation_state` derives which offline reports are attached. Type-checks inputs (`_validate_inputs` -> TypeError on mismatch). Builds deterministic id + rationale + limitations. intelligence/__init__.py stays intentionally empty.
- `src/engine/intelligence/production_intelligence_serialization.py`: deterministic, self-describing JSON, `PRODUCTION_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1`. `serialize_production`/`serialize_production_bytes`/`deserialize_production`/`parse_production_header`/`canonical_production_json`. Serialization REUSES the existing 12B/12C/12D serializers: the reused 12B `IntegratedDecisionContext` is embedded BY VALUE by delegating to `serialize_integration`/`deserialize_integration`; the 12C `BacktestValidationReport` is embedded BY VALUE by delegating to `serialize_validation`/`deserialize_validation`; the 12D `RobustnessValidationReport` is embedded BY VALUE by delegating to `serialize_robustness`/`deserialize_robustness`. The local encoder only handles the two production enums + the metadata tuple; NO 12B/12C/12D reconstruction logic is duplicated. Serialization scope (intentional, matching the 12B discipline): the lightweight production projections are persisted IN FULL (production id, statuses, validation state, rationale, limitations, label, metadata, the embedded 12B/12C/12D audit views); the heavy ORIGINAL existing-decision reference object (carried by the 12B context) is retained BY REFERENCE on the LIVE result but is NOT persisted; on reload `existing_decision` reconstructs as None (regenerable by the caller); the `existing_decision_summary` projection + integration id + status + decision-intelligence context are all persisted losslessly, so the production audit view survives the round trip. Schema-version checked before model reconstruction; future versions rejected with ValueError. Deterministic sorted-key bytes.
- `src/engine/reporting/production_intelligence.py`: `ProductionIntelligenceFormatter(precision=2, width=60).format(context) -> str`. Stateless, deterministic, returns str (no print()). Sections STRICTLY separate the eight concerns: Production Intelligence Report header (production id, label, metadata), Market/Opportunity Information, Existing Decision (authoritative; represented without alteration), Historical Observed Performance (descriptive), Evidence Strength (sample-size-gated), Strategy Interpretation (evidence-conditioned), Decision Intelligence Context (contextual), Controlled Integration Status (explicit; shows reused 12B integration), Validation/Robustness State (offline; explicit ÔÇö shows 12C/12D overall status/counts or "not attached"), Contextual Factors (reused from 12A), Limitations, Rationale. Every report ends with the explicit WARNING: "Production intelligence is a coherent bundle of already-computed descriptive artifacts. It is descriptive, does NOT guarantee future performance, does NOT constitute a trading recommendation, does NOT imply live-trading readiness, and does NOT modify the existing decision / scoring logic." Unavailable values shown as "unavailable". No predictive/probability/statistical-significance/buy-sell-enter-exit-hold/trading-recommendation language. Following 11O-12D convention, imported via full path (NOT re-exported from reporting/__init__.py).
- POINT-IN-TIME / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally enforced + regression-tested): 12E consumes ALREADY-COMPUTED artifacts only and adds NO future information. (1) The public API `assemble(integrated_context, backtest_validation, robustness_validation, label, metadata)` takes NO candle/future-market-data argument (signature inspected). (2) Works with `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` patched to raise (must not re-evaluate outcomes). (3) Works with `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` patched to raise (must not re-run pipeline). (4) The 12B integrated context / 12C/12D validation reports are taken verbatim, NEVER mutated. The point-in-time correctness established in 11V/11W is preserved unchanged.
- `scripts/test_production_intelligence.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 12 demonstrations (production happy path; existing decision preservation by reference; evidence-supported; insufficient evidence [tiny 100%-win cohort stays INSUFFICIENT]; evidence unavailable [no matching cohort -> CONTEXT_ONLY]; invalid/mismatched context [no integrated context -> INVALID; type mismatch raises TypeError]; serialization round trip [incl. heavy existing_decision drops to None on load while summary survives]; determinism + shuffle invariance; immutability; no-look-ahead [OutcomeEvaluator + pipeline patched to raise; production API takes no candle argument]; full 11V->12D regression [12C PASS, 12D PASS, pipeline signals=4/trades=3]; final production integration [all eight concerns bundled; no BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD language]). Prints full production intelligence report. Every demo check prints explicit PASS/FAIL/SKIPPED; SKIPPED never counted as PASS; exits non-zero on failures. Ends with "Sprint 12E demo completed successfully (49 checks passed)." Demo exits 0.
- `tests/test_production_intelligence.py`: 98 tests across areas A-Z + end-to-end + reporting + model + backward-compat (A happy-path; B-E all four existing decision classifications REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED preserved unchanged + not upgraded/downgraded; F evidence-supported; G limited evidence [WEAK -> EVIDENCE_LIMITED]; H insufficient evidence; I evidence unavailable [CONTEXT_ONLY]; J invalid/mismatched [INVALID + no-DI UNAVAILABLE + 3 type-mismatch TypeErrors]; K missing historical evidence [NONE state + no fabricated metrics]; L existing decision preserved by identity [`is`]; M no decision mutation; N input immutability [frozen+slots+validation-report-not-mutated];
