R malformed serialization [unsupported-schema/missing-schema/not-json/malformed-mapping]; S config validation [defaults/frozen/slots/label-type/metadata-type/metadata-pair/snapshot/config-label-default]; T error isolation [type-mismatch-no-corrupt/invalid-does-not-prevent-valid]; U no-look-ahead [evaluator-patched/pipeline-patched]; V 11Y hard-gate [tiny-impressive-insufficient/insufficient-never-supported]; W BOTH_TOUCHED [excluded-from-win-loss/realized_r-None-not-zero]; X NO_GEOMETRY [no-fabricated-r]; Y INSUFFICIENT_DATA [no-fabricated-values]; Z pipeline regression [signals=4/trades=3 + 12C/12D PASS]; end-to-end full 11V->12E chain [all 12 spec requirements]; reporting [returns-str/8-concern-sections/disclaimer/unavailable/deterministic/negative-precision+width-rejected/shows-validation/shows-no-validation-honestly/no-predictive-language]; model [frozen/slots/enum-members/rank-ordering/limitations-constant/delegating-properties-both-cases/validation-state-backtest/robustness-only]; backward-compat [prior-apis-importable/intelligence-init-empty/reporting-init-not-extended/12b-serialization-unchanged]).
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (this is integration + reporting, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-12D engines; the production status is MIRRORED from the reused 12B status, never recomputed); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; reporting/__init__.py NOT extended (formatter imported via full path, matching 11O-12D convention). No existing engine/model modified (only additive new files). No print() in engines/formatters (formatter returns str; demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live/external-API/network-dependency-in-tests. A production intelligence context is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability of success, never a guarantee of profitability, never implies live-trading readiness, never a statistical-significance claim, never a buy/sell/enter/exit/hold recommendation, never a replacement of existing decision/scoring/ranking/selection/risk-geometry/execution logic. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE and preserved EXACTLY (by reference; classification/score/rank never upgraded/downgraded). Historical vs live boundary preserved (production runtime never runs historical replay against future candles; validation reports are pre-computed offline artifacts referenced by the production artifact). 11Y hard gate preserved (tiny cohort never strong regardless of observed win rate). BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA preserved honestly (no fabricated metrics); unavailable metrics remain None; empty inputs honest (never a fake PASS). No future leakage (12E consumes already-computed artifacts only; proven by patching the outcome evaluator AND the pipeline to raise; regression-tested). Deterministic (no wall-clock, no randomness; shuffle-invariant via inherited 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B sorted identities embedded in the production_id). Serialization round-trips losslessly for the audit view (heavy existing-decision ref drops on load, regenerable; ExistingDecisionSummary projection + 12B context + 12C/12D reports + id + status preserved). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 11X/11Y/11Z/12A/12B/12C/12D unchanged. No Sprint 11A-12D regression.

## SCOPE STOP ÔÇö FINAL ARCHITECTURE BOUNDARY
Sprint 12E is the FINAL planned sprint of the current architecture. The final architecture boundary is: models <- intelligence <- pipeline (unchanged in direction); the historical-evidence/decision-intelligence chain (11V -> 11W -> 11X -> 11Y -> 11Z -> 12A -> 12B) is OFFLINE / DESCRIPTIVE; the validation chain (12C -> 12D) is OFFLINE / DESCRIPTIVE; Sprint 12E is the single thin PRODUCTION INTEGRATION BOUNDARY that bundles already-computed 12B integration + optional 12C/12D validation into one coherent production-facing artifact. NO Sprint 12F is required for the current project scope. If something remains unresolved, classify it as: (1) a production bug requiring a fix, (2) a documented limitation, or (3) a future enhancement outside the current project scope. Do NOT manufacture additional architecture. Known limitations (documented, not bugs): (a) the production artifact is descriptive/contextual, never predictive ÔÇö it cannot and does not claim future performance; (b) the production runtime does not perform live trade execution / order generation / position sizing / portfolio management (intentionally out of scope); (c) the attached validation reports are point-in-time snapshots of historical corpora ÔÇö they must be re-run offline when the historical corpus changes; (d) no statistical hypothesis test is performed anywhere in the chain.

## PRODUCTIZATION PHASE ÔÇö Web Dashboard (added)
This phase is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer and NOT a new orchestration package. It is a THIN web presentation / orchestration layer (`dashboard/`) that makes the EXISTING completed architecture (Sprints 11A-12E) practically usable for intraday discretionary trading research. It implements NO trading, scoring, prediction, decision or geometry logic: every value it displays is read from the reused engine outputs. The dashboard is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö never a trade signal, never predictive, never a probability, never a guarantee of profitability, never a buy/sell recommendation, never a replacement of the existing decision / scoring logic.
- Repository state: STATE B (CORE ARCHITECTURE COMPLETE; PRODUCTIZATION REMAINS). The dashboard is the first productization deliverable. NO Sprint 12F was created; the completed 11A-12E intelligence architecture was NOT reopened or redesigned.
- Web stack: FastAPI + Uvicorn + Jinja2 + a lightweight vanilla-JS canvas candlestick chart. No large frontend framework. Dependencies declared in `requirements.txt` / `pyproject.toml` (`fastapi`, `uvicorn[standard]`, `jinja2`, `python-multipart`; optional `pandas`/`yfinance` for the live provider). The `dashboard/` package is a TOP-LEVEL package (importable as `import dashboard`); `pyproject.toml` `pythonpath = ["src", "."]` so tests resolve both `engine` and `dashboard`.
- Package layout: `dashboard/__init__.py`, `dashboard/views.py` (presentation models + `ActionabilityState`), `dashboard/data_provider.py` (market-data adapter), `dashboard/services.py` (`DashboardAnalysisService` orchestration), `dashboard/app.py` (FastAPI app + routes), `dashboard/templates/` (`base.html`, `dashboard.html`), `dashboard/static/` (`dashboard.css`, `chart.js`), `tests/test_dashboard.py` (67 tests), `scripts/test_dashboard.py` (demo).
- ENTRY POINT + how to run locally:
  - `python -m uvicorn dashboard.app:app --reload` (or `python -m dashboard.app`).
  - Env vars: `DASHBOARD_PROVIDER` (`fixture` [default] | `yahoo`), `DASHBOARD_HOST`, `DASHBOARD_PORT`, `DASHBOARD_EVIDENCE_PATH` (optional path to a serialized Sprint 11Y `HistoricalEvidenceReport` to enable the evidence section).
  - Default URL: `http://127.0.0.1:8000`.
- API ROUTES:
  - `GET /` ÔÇö HTML dashboard (query params `instrument`, `timeframe`).
  - `GET /health` and `GET /api/health` ÔÇö health JSON (provider, instruments, timeframes, evidence_attached).
  - `GET /api/analysis?instrument=&timeframe=` ÔÇö full JSON view (market_overview, decision, geometry, evidence, actionability, chart payload).
  - `GET /api/instruments` ÔÇö available instruments + timeframes.
  - `GET /static/{dashboard.css,chart.js}` ÔÇö static assets.
- HOW MARKET DATA FLOWS INTO THE DASHBOARD: `DashboardDataProvider` (default `FixtureDataProvider` reusing Sprint 11V `historical_fixtures`; optional `YahooDataProvider` reusing the existing `YahooFinanceProvider`) -> normalized `OHLCVCandle` tuples -> `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze` builds a Sprint 11U `InstrumentDataset` (context + setup candles) -> `MarketScanner.scan(datasets, evaluation_time, ScanEngines.default())` -> `InstrumentScanResult` (carrying reused `MarketContext`, `TradeDecision`->`TradeCandidate`, `TradeOpportunity`, `MTFAlignment`) -> projected into a `DashboardTradeView` presentation model. NO intelligence is recomputed; NO raw provider data is manipulated by the dashboard beyond normalization already done by the engine.
- DISTINCTION BETWEEN INTELLIGENCE ENGINE AND UI: the `engine/` packages remain the single source of truth. The `dashboard/` package only (a) fetches/normalizes data via the existing data layer, (b) calls the existing scanner, (c) projects results into read-only presentation models, (d) renders HTML/JSON. `dashboard/views.py` explicitly documents each field as OBSERVED (reused) vs DERIVED (presentation). The dashboard NEVER redefines engine models (`dashboard.views` has no `TradeDecision`/`TradeCandidate`).
- TRADE GEOMETRY SEMANTICS (answers the product question): the existing Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` ALREADY carries `entry_reference`, `stop_reference`, `target_reference`, `risk_distance`, `reward_distance`, `risk_reward_ratio`, `setup_type`, `geometry_complete` ÔÇö reached via `scan_result.decision.candidate`. The dashboard reuses these VERBATIM:
  - Entry = `entry_reference`; Stop Loss = `stop_reference`; Target 1 = `target_reference`; Risk = `risk_distance`; Reward = `reward_distance`; Risk/Reward = `risk_reward_ratio`; Invalidation = `stop_reference` (the stop IS the structural invalidation level ÔÇö honest reuse, not fabrication).
  - Target 2 = `None` with `target_2_supported = False` ÔÇö the architecture produces a SINGLE structural target; a second target is NOT supported and is NEVER invented.
  - When geometry is incomplete (e.g. a BREAKOUT where price is already beyond resistance so no opposing structural target exists), `geometry_available = False` and the panel shows "TRADE GEOMETRY UNAVAILABLE" ÔÇö no entry/stop/target is fabricated to make the UI look complete.
- ACTIONABILITY (presentation MIRROR, not a new score): `ActionabilityState` (UNAVAILABLE / NO_OPPORTUNITY / WATCH / QUALIFIED_SETUP / PREFERRED_SETUP) is a documented deterministic mapping from the AUTHORITATIVE existing outputs only (scan completeness + Sprint 11S decision classification + Sprint 11T opportunity status + eligibility). It NEVER re-scores, NEVER re-ranks, NEVER overrides the existing decision, NEVER produces BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD. Mapping documented in `derive_actionability`.
- INTRADAY USAGE: the workflow is Historical + current market data -> market structure -> setup identification -> existing decision -> historical evidence -> trade geometry -> actionable presentation. The dashboard surfaces: selected timeframe, latest candle timestamp, data-stale warning, current structure (recent HH/HL/LH/LL), HTF trend, MTF alignment, setup status, entry zone, stop, target, invalidation, historical evidence. No future candle may influence the current analysis.
- NO-LOOK-AHEAD GUARANTEES (NON-NEGOTIABLE, regression-tested): (1) the evaluation point is the close of the latest COMPLETED setup candle; (2) the scanner's own point-in-time guarantees (completed-HTF-candle-only via `_latest_completed_before`; setup slice `candles[:T+1]` via `_latest_completed_at_or_before`) are preserved unchanged ÔÇö the service feeds the SAME candle tuples + the explicit `evaluation_time`; (3) the service NEVER calls the Sprint 11W `OutcomeEvaluator` during current analysis (regression-tested: works with `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` patched to raise); (4) the public `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze` API accepts NO "future candles" argument; (5) at a FIXED evaluation timestamp T, appending/mutating future candles does NOT change the analysis (regression-tested: service analyze-at-T == direct scanner scan of prefix+future at evaluation_time=T).
- EVIDENCE: the dashboard does NOT compute evidence. An optional offline Sprint 11Y `HistoricalEvidenceReport` may be attached via `EvidenceSource` (or `DASHBOARD_EVIDENCE_PATH`); the dashboard then reuses the Sprint 11Z `StrategyIntelligenceEngine.lookup` to surface the matched cohort's evidence. When no corpus is attached, evidence is honestly UNAVAILABLE (never fabricated). The 11Y sample-size hard gate is preserved (a 1-trade 100% winner is never STRONG ÔÇö regression-tested).
- HONESTY / SAFETY: every page carries the disclaimer that the system provides technical-analysis and historical research context only, does not guarantee future performance, and that no trade is guaranteed to succeed. The UI distinguishes OBSERVED / CALCULATED / HISTORICAL / DERIVED / UNAVAILABLE. No fake confidence, probability, accuracy, entry, stop, target or evidence is ever displayed. Unavailable values render as "unavailable". The chart overlays ONLY backend-authored levels (entry/stop/target/support/resistance/invalidation); the frontend never invents technical levels.
- LIMITATIONS (documented): (a) only 15m setup + 1D context are available from the local deterministic fixtures; other intraday timeframes show an honest "unavailable" state (the optional Yahoo provider can extend this but is network/dependency dependent and degrades gracefully); (b) Target 2 is not supported by the architecture; (c) evidence requires a pre-computed offline corpus (not included by default); (d) fixture data is historical so the stale-data flag is informational; (e) no broker integration / order execution / live trading / position sizing / portfolio management (intentionally out of scope); (f) no LLM dependency.
- `dashboard/views.py`: `ActionabilityState` enum + `derive_actionability` (documented deterministic mirror), `GeometryView` / `EvidenceView` / `MarketOverviewView` / `DecisionView` / `DashboardTradeView` (frozen+slots presentation projections), `to_jsonable` (JSON serialization for the API/chart).
- `dashboard/data_provider.py`: `DashboardDataProvider` protocol, `FixtureDataProvider` (reuses Sprint 11V fixtures; only 15M/1D), `YahooDataProvider` (optional, reuses existing `YahooFinanceProvider`, graceful on any failure), `make_provider`, `SUPPORTED_TIMEFRAMES`, `FIXTURE_INSTRUMENTS`, `context_timeframe_for`.
- `dashboard/services.py`: `DashboardAnalysisService(provider, scanner, engines, evidence_source, min_history, staleness_seconds, max_chart_candles)` with `analyze(request) -> DashboardTradeView`, `chart_payload(request, view) -> ChartPayload`, `available_instruments/timeframes`. `EvidenceSource` (optional offline evidence via reused 11Z lookup). `AnalysisRequest`, `ChartPayload`. Pure projection; NO intelligence recomputed.
- `dashboard/app.py`: `create_app(service=None) -> FastAPI`, module-level `app`, `set_service`, `main`. Routes for `/`, `/health`, `/api/health`, `/api/analysis`, `/api/instruments`, `/static`. Jinja2 filters (`fmt_price`/`fmt_ratio`/`fmt_pct`/`fmt_r`/`fmt_ts`) render "unavailable" for None.
- `tests/test_dashboard.py`: 67 tests across areas A-S (startup, routes, health, instrument selection, timeframe selection, unavailable data, stale data, decision rendering, evidence rendering [incl. real 11Y report + insufficient-never-strong], trade geometry rendering, missing trade geometry, actionability mapping, no-look-ahead [outcome-evaluator-patched, no-future-argument, fixed-T-unaffected-by-future, future-mutation-unaffected], serialization/presentation model, deterministic output, input immutability, existing decision identity [no upgrade], regression against 11V->12E [pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3, scanner importable, no engine model redefinition], demo smoke, no-predictive-language).
- `scripts/test_dashboard.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 10 requirements (dashboard starts; health; instrument selection; timeframe selection; existing decision displayed authoritative; evidence honestly unavailable; trade geometry displayed; missing geometry honest; no-look-ahead [no future-candles arg, outcome evaluator NOT called, fixed-T unaffected by future]; pipeline unchanged signals=4/trades=3) + determinism. 30 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Sprint dashboard demo completed successfully."
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package and NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-12E engines; trade geometry reused verbatim from the 11R candidate; actionability is a documented deterministic mirror, not a new score); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; NO existing engine/model modified (only additive `dashboard/` package + tests + demo + requirements/pyproject + README/AGENTS docs). No print() in engines (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/position-sizing. The dashboard is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL). 11Y hard gate preserved. BOTH_TOUCHED/NO_GEOMETRY/INSUFFICIENT_DATA semantics untouched (the dashboard never re-evaluates outcomes). No future leakage (structurally enforced + regression-tested). Deterministic (repeated analysis identical). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 12C/12D/12E demos unchanged (33/45/49 PASS). No Sprint 11A-12E regression.

## PRODUCT PHASE 1 ÔÇö LIVE / NEAR-LIVE MARKET-DATA INTEGRATION (added)
This phase is NOT an intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a DATA INTEGRATION / PRODUCTIZATION increment that makes the existing Trading Workstation capable of receiving current/near-live OHLCV market data through a provider abstraction and feeding that data into the EXISTING analysis pipeline. The architecture remains: Market Data Provider -> OHLCV Normalization -> COMPLETED-CANDLE BOUNDARY -> Existing MarketScanner -> Existing analysis/decision architecture -> Existing TradeCandidate geometry -> Dashboard. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive fields with defaults on `InstrumentSeries`, an additive `DataSourceView`, additive `DashboardTradeView.data_source`, additive `DashboardAnalysisService`/provider constructor args, additive `data_provider` enums/helpers, additive template section, additive app health fields), and the prior 2563 tests still pass (now 2634 with 71 new in tests/test_live_data_integration.py).
- Goal: make the EXISTING intelligence architecture operate on reliable current/near-live market data. The provider only supplies trustworthy data; it implements NO trading/scoring/prediction/decision/geometry logic. Existing `TradeCandidate` geometry (`entry_reference`/`stop_reference`/`target_reference`/`risk_distance`/`reward_distance`/`risk_reward_ratio`/`setup_type`/`geometry_complete`) remains AUTHORITATIVE. Target 2 remains unsupported (`None` + `target_2_supported=False`). Existing decision classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) remains AUTHORITATIVE. No BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendations. No predictive model.
- Provider architecture (refined `dashboard/data_provider.py`): `DashboardDataProvider` Protocol (`is_timeframe_supported` / `fetch(instrument, setup_timeframe, lookback_bars, *, reference_now=None) -> InstrumentSeries` / `last_updated`). Two providers: (A) `FixtureDataProvider` (default, deterministic, no network ÔÇö reuses Sprint 11V fixtures, only 15M setup + 1D context); (B) `YahooDataProvider` (optional live/near-live, reuses the existing `engine.data.yahoo_provider.YahooFinanceProvider`, graceful on ANY failure). `make_provider(name, *, freshness_config, symbol_map)` factory. Provider-specific code is ISOLATED behind the abstraction; the analysis engine never sees provider internals.
- Live provider implementation (`YahooDataProvider`): reuses the existing Yahoo provider through a thin adapter. SYMBOL MAPPING is isolated inside the provider (`YAHOO_SYMBOL_MAP`: NIFTY->^NSEI, RELIANCE->RELIANCE.NS, ...; unknown instruments pass through verbatim; `resolve_symbol(instrument)`; configurable via `symbol_map`). Yahoo-supported intervals: 1m/2m/5m/15m/30m/60m/1h/90m/1D/1d (native provider intervals only ÔÇö NO resampling/fabrication of 5m/15m/30m/1H data; unsupported timeframes reported UNSUPPORTED). Heuristic bounded lookback honoring Yahoo intraday limits (<=5m: 60d, <=1h: 730d, 1d: 365d). Each fetched candle validated via the existing `DataValidator.validate_candle`; impossible candles dropped + reported (never crash). `last_successful_fetch_time` tracked. An optional `provider=` constructor arg lets tests inject a fake backend (NO network in tests). The Yahoo provider is only used when explicitly selected (`DASHBOARD_PROVIDER=yahoo`) AND `yfinance`/`pandas` are importable.
- COMPLETED-CANDLE BOUNDARY (NON-NEGOTIABLE, the core of Product Phase 1): the single deterministic `split_completed_candles(candles, timeframe, reference_now, *, duration_seconds=None) -> CandleBoundaryResult` helper. A candle is COMPLETED when its close time `timestamp + duration <= reference_now` (duration from `TIMEFRAME_DURATION_SECONDS`; explicit override for tests). The currently-FORMING candle (open <= now, close > now) is carried separately on `InstrumentSeries.forming_setup_candle` for DISPLAY ONLY and is NEVER fed to the intelligence engine. Future-dated candles (`timestamp > now`) are REJECTED and counted in `rejected_future_count` (never used). Naive/aware timestamp mixing handled defensively (compared on naive UTC). `reference_now` is an explicit parameter so tests are deterministic (no wall-clock). The `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze` sets `evaluation_time = series.latest_completed_candle_timestamp` ÔÇö NEVER the forming candle. The scanner's own point-in-time guarantees (`_latest_completed_before`, `_latest_completed_at_or_before`) are preserved unchanged.
- FRESHNESS / STALENESS (DATA QUALITY, not a trading signal): `FreshnessState` enum (CURRENT/STALE/UNAVAILABLE/INVALID) + `ProviderStatus` enum (OK/UNSUPPORTED/EMPTY/ERROR/NOT_READY) + `FreshnessConfig` (config-driven `default_staleness_seconds` + `timeframe_overrides`; replaces scattered magic constants). `classify_freshness(*, latest_completed_timestamp, reference_now, staleness_seconds, provider_status) -> FreshnessState` (pure, deterministic). Freshness NEVER alters the intelligence engine's decision semantics ÔÇö it only drives presentation warnings + the `DataSourceView` metadata. No fake "live" state invented; no stale data silently substituted for current; a failed live request NEVER silently falls back to fixture data.
- Dashboard integration (minimal): `DashboardTradeView.data_source: DataSourceView` (data_source/provider_status/freshness_state/latest_candle_timestamp/latest_completed_candle_timestamp/forming_candle_present/last_successful_fetch_time/rejected_future_count) surfaced in `to_jsonable` under a `data_source` block + a new "Data Source" template section. `/health` + `/api/health` expose `data_source` + `supported_timeframes` (honest capability reporting). The service surfaces STALE / rejected-future-candles / ERROR / NOT-READY warnings. The dashboard distinguishes Fixture/deterministic vs Live/near-live via the `data_source` label.
- Supported instruments (fixture): NIFTY/RELIANCE/TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK. Yahoo: the same set via symbol mapping PLUS any Yahoo-recognized ticker passed through verbatim (e.g. AAPL/MSFT).
- Supported live timeframes (Yahoo): 1m/2m/5m/15m/30m/60m/1h/90m/1D/1d (native only). Fixture: 15M setup + 1D context only. Each requested timeframe is honestly reported SUPPORTED/UNSUPPORTED via `is_timeframe_supported` + the health `supported_timeframes` list.
- Error / failure handling: network unavailable / timeout / invalid response / empty response / unsupported instrument / unsupported timeframe / malformed candle / rate limit / provider-side error are all caught and reported via `available=False` + a structured `ProviderStatus` + a human-readable `reason` ÔÇö the dashboard NEVER crashes, NEVER returns fake market data, NEVER reports stale data as current, NEVER converts a provider failure into a successful analysis, and NEVER silently falls back from a failed live provider to fixture data. `YahooDataProvider(provider=None)` (no yfinance installed) -> NOT_READY, honest unavailable state.
- NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, regression-tested in tests/test_live_data_integration.py): (1) current analysis uses ONLY completed candles (the boundary strips the forming candle); (2) adding a future candle does not change the analysis at a fixed cutoff (entry/stop/target/decision/score unchanged); (3) a forming candle does not change the completed-candle analysis; (4) provider-returned future timestamps are rejected/ignored (`rejected_future_count`); (5) `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` is NOT invoked by live dashboard analysis (patched-to-raise test passes); (6) `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` is NOT invoked by live dashboard analysis (patched-to-raise test passes); (7) the public `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze` + provider `fetch` APIs accept NO `future`/`future_candles` argument (signature inspected).
- DETERMINISM: fixture-based tests remain fully deterministic ÔÇö the fixture provider NEVER calls wall-clock `datetime.now()` (it uses a far-future aware sentinel for the boundary and anchors `last_successful_fetch_time` to the data's own latest completed candle timestamp; freshness is honestly STALE). Same fixture input -> same normalized candles -> same scanner result -> same decision -> same geometry -> same dashboard representation. No IDs/results depend on wall-clock/random/dict-ordering. Live metadata timestamps are allowed where they represent actual provider/data timestamps but never alter deterministic fixture behavior.
- `tests/test_live_data_integration.py`: 71 tests across areas A-X (provider abstraction; fixture compatibility; live normalization; valid/malformed candle handling; empty response; provider failure; unsupported instrument/timeframe; completed-candle detection; forming-candle exclusion; future-timestamp protection; stale/fresh detection; dashboard live-data integration; unavailable state; stale state; no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise, no future-candle arg, fixed-T unaffected by future, forming-candle not in engine input]; existing decision preservation; existing trade geometry preservation; Target 2 unsupported; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3; existing fixture behavior; serialization/backward compatibility). Tests use a `_FakeYahooBackend` (NO real network calls).
- `scripts/test_live_data_integration.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 10 requirements (provider abstraction + factory; fixture compatibility; live normalization; symbol mapping; completed-candle boundary; freshness; failure handling; unsupported instrument/timeframe; dashboard integration + no-look-ahead; pipeline baseline 4/3). 33 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Product Phase 1 demo completed successfully."
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (data integration + productization, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every intelligence value still read from reused Sprint 11A-12E engines; the provider only supplies completed candles); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; NO existing engine/model modified (only additive defaulted fields + additive new helpers/enums/views/template-section/health-fields). No print() in engines (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/position-sizing/automated-trading. Live data integration is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö "live data" does NOT mean "live trading"; it does NOT predict the market, does NOT guarantee future performance, does NOT constitute a trading recommendation, does NOT imply live-trading readiness. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed). Existing `TradeCandidate` geometry reused VERBATIM (never recomputed in the dashboard/provider). Target 2 remains unsupported. 11Y hard gate preserved. No future leakage (completed-candle boundary structurally enforced + regression-tested for forming-candle exclusion, future-timestamp rejection, fixed-T stability, evaluator-not-called, pipeline-not-called). Deterministic fixture path (no wall-clock). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 12C/12D/12E/dashboard demos unchanged (33/45/49/38 PASS). No Sprint 11A-12E regression.

## PRODUCT PHASE 2 ÔÇö MULTI-INSTRUMENT SCANNER & WATCHLIST (added)
This phase is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a MULTI-INSTRUMENT SCANNER + WATCHLIST orchestration/presentation increment built ON TOP of the existing Product Phase 1 dashboard/provider infrastructure. The scanner orchestrates the EXISTING single-instrument intelligence pipeline over a watchlist of instruments and presents the resulting opportunities in one coherent, deterministically ordered view. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive new files `dashboard/watchlist.py` + additive view models in `dashboard/views.py` + additive `scan_watchlist`/`ScanRequest` in `dashboard/services.py` + additive routes in `dashboard/app.py` + additive `scanner.html` template + nav link in `base.html` + additive CSS), and the prior 2635 tests still pass (now 2710 with 75 new in tests/test_watchlist_scanner.py).
- Goal: make the EXISTING intelligence architecture useful across a WATCHLIST of instruments. The scanner implements NO new market-analysis, decision, scoring, geometry or evidence logic; every per-row value is read from the reused `DashboardTradeView` produced by the existing `DashboardAnalysisService.analyze`. It is a human-trader research view that lets a trader answer: which instruments are currently worth reviewing; which have REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED decisions; which have complete trade geometry; which have sufficient historical evidence; which are ready for review vs waiting/unavailable; which have fresh/current data; which are unavailable or stale; what are the existing entry/stop/target/R:R values.
- `dashboard/watchlist.py`: `Watchlist` (mutable, deterministic, validated collection of instrument names ÔÇö add/remove idempotent, duplicate prevention, canonical stripped+upper-cased names, lexicographic ordering so INPUT ORDER NEVER AFFECTS RESULTS, `__contains__`, `__iter__`, `__len__`, `__eq__`, not hashable). `WatchlistSpec` (frozen+slots immutable snapshot for serialization ÔÇö sorted/deduped instruments + label, `to_jsonable`). `DEFAULT_WATCHLIST` (the local fixture instruments). `Watchlist.default()` builds the default watchlist. `_validate_name` rejects empty/whitespace/non-string names (ValueError/TypeError). NO persistence (local workstation only ÔÇö no user accounts/database/filesystem).
- Scanner presentation (`dashboard/views.py`): `WatchlistRowView` (frozen+slots ÔÇö wraps the reused `DashboardTradeView` BY REFERENCE for one instrument + adds `error` flag + `rank`; delegating read-only properties surface decision/actionability/evidence/freshness/geometry/setup/direction/entry/stop/target_1/risk_reward_ratio WITHOUT recomputing). `WatchlistScanView` (frozen+slots ÔÇö watchlist_instruments, setup/context timeframe, rows tuple ordered by the ranking key, total/analyzed/errored/actionable_count, warnings, rationale; `is_empty`/`has_errors` properties). `scanner_rank_key(row)` builds the deterministic PRESENTATIONAL ordering key ÔÇö a 6-tuple read ONLY from existing fields: (1) decision classification strength (PREFERRED<QUALIFIED<WATCH<REJECTED<none), (2) actionability/readiness mirror (READY_FOR_REVIEW<INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE<WAIT<TRADE_GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE<NO_OPPORTUNITY<INVALID), (3) evidence strength (STRONG<MODERATE<WEAK<INSUFFICIENT<UNAVAILABLE ÔÇö missing sorts LAST, never promoted on absent evidence), (4) geometry availability (complete first), (5) freshness (CURRENT<STALE<UNAVAILABLE/INVALID ÔÇö DATA QUALITY tie-break only), (6) instrument name ascending (final deterministic tie-break). Direction (LONG/SHORT) is DELIBERATELY NOT a ranking key. The ranking maps are FIXED and intentionally NOT configurable (configuring would risk silently re-ranking the AUTHORITATIVE decision). `scan_view_to_jsonable(scan)` converts to a JSON-serializable dict keeping the five concerns (decision/geometry/evidence/actionability/data source) SEPARATE ÔÇö never collapsed into one signal/score; each row carries a `review_url` linking to the single-instrument trade-review view.
- Scanner orchestration (`dashboard/services.py`): `DashboardAnalysisService.scan_watchlist(ScanRequest) -> WatchlistScanView` ORCHESTRATES the existing single-instrument `analyze` per instrument. When `request.watchlist is None` it uses `default_watchlist()`; an explicitly EMPTY watchlist produces an empty scan (honest, not a fallback). GUARANTEES: reuse-only; failure isolation (one failing instrument -> honest `INVALID` error row, scan CONTINUES ÔÇö exceptions caught in `_scan_one` and converted to an unavailable view, never raised, never fabricated); completed-candle guarantee inherited from `analyze` (latest COMPLETED setup candle only; forming candle never fed; no future candle read); determinism (rows sorted by `scanner_rank_key`; input watchlist ordering NEVER changes output order); no look-ahead (accepts NO `future`/`future_candles` argument; never calls `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate`; never calls `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate`); decision authority (existing classification reused VERBATIM, never renamed to BUY/SELL, never upgraded/downgraded). `ScanRequest` (frozen dataclass ÔÇö watchlist, setup_timeframe, context_timeframe). `default_watchlist()` returns `Watchlist.default()`.
- API routes (`dashboard/app.py`): `GET /scan` (HTML scanner/watchlist view; query params `timeframe`, `instruments` comma-separated) and `GET /api/scan` (structured JSON: watchlist_instruments, setup/context timeframe, rows [rank/instrument/error/complete/data_source/freshness/decision_classification/decision_score/opportunity_status/eligible/actionability/actionability_reason/evidence_strength/evidence_available/setup_type/direction/geometry_available/entry/stop/target_1/target_2/target_2_supported/risk_distance/reward_distance/risk_reward_ratio/mtf_alignment/htf_trend/ltf_trend/review_url], total/analyzed/errored/actionable_count, warnings, rationale). Existing routes (`/`, `/health`, `/api/health`, `/api/analysis`, `/api/instruments`) UNCHANGED.
- Dashboard UI: `templates/scanner.html` (scan summary card + watchlist opportunities table with columns #/Instrument/Data/Fresh/Decision/Actionability/Evidence/Setup/Dir/Geom/Entry/Stop/Target1/R:R/Review; each row links to the single-instrument trade-review view via `review_url`; the table class is `actionability-{{ state }}` for visual cueing). `templates/base.html` EXTENDED with a nav (`Trade Review` / `Scanner / Watchlist`). `static/dashboard.css` EXTENDED with `.nav`, `.scanner-table`, decision/direction color helpers (additive). The existing single-instrument trade-review experience is NOT removed.
- PRESENTATIONAL ORDERING (NOT a new intelligence layer): the spec requires the scanner may ORDER results using deterministic existing classifications + existing presentation metadata; it must NOT calculate probability / predictive score / confidence / weighted confluence / expected return / a new ranking score. The `scanner_rank_key` is explicitly documented as PRESENTATIONAL ORDERING ÔÇö a sort, NOT a prediction. A PREFERRED setup sorts before QUALIFIED before WATCH before REJECTED purely as a display convenience; it does NOT imply the higher-ranked row is more likely to be profitable. The rationale string on every `WatchlistScanView` states this explicitly.
- SUPPORTED INSTRUMENTS/TIMEFRAMES: the scanner uses the EXISTING provider/timeframe capability. Fixture provider: NIFTY/RELIANCE/TCS/HDFCBANK/ICICIBANK, 15M setup + 1D context only. Yahoo provider (optional): the same set via symbol mapping plus any Yahoo-recognized ticker, native intervals `1m`/`2m`/`5m`/`15m`/`30m`/`60m`/`1h`/`90m`/`1D`. NO resampling / fabrication of unsupported timeframes; an unsupported timeframe -> structured error rows (every instrument INVALID, not a crash). A custom watchlist may include any instrument name (unknown instruments are reported honestly as INVALID error rows via the provider's unsupported-instrument path ÔÇö failure isolation).
- FAILURE ISOLATION: provider timeout / unsupported instrument / unsupported timeframe / empty data / malformed provider data / invalid analysis / a raised exception in `analyze` are all caught per-instrument in `_scan_one` and converted to an honest `INVALID` error row (`error=True`, `actionability=INVALID`, `complete=False`); the scan CONTINUES with the remaining instruments; the `WatchlistScanView.warnings` surfaces the count and reason. One bad symbol never aborts the scan and never fabricates an opportunity.
- NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, regression-tested): (1) each instrument is evaluated using the latest COMPLETED setup candle (inherited from `analyze`); (2) appending a future candle does not change a fixed-T scan (the completed-candle boundary rejects it); (3) a forming candle is never fed to the engine; (4) `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` is NOT invoked by the scanner (patched-to-raise test passes); (5) `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` is NOT invoked by the scanner (patched-to-raise test passes); (6) the public `scan_watchlist` + `ScanRequest` APIs accept NO `future`/`future_candles` argument (signature inspected).
- DECISION AUTHORITY: the existing Sprint 11S decision classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) is reused VERBATIM on every row (`row.view.decision` is the reused `DashboardTradeView`'s `DecisionView`). Never renamed to BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD; never upgraded or downgraded by the scanner or the presentational ordering. Tests assert decision classification equals the single-instrument `analyze` output and is never a BUY/SELL label.
- GEOMETRY/EVIDENCE PRESERVATION: entry/stop/target_1/risk_reward_ratio/setup_type/geometry_complete are reused VERBATIM from the Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` (reached via the reused trade view). Target 2 remains `None` + `target_2_supported=False` on every row. Evidence is reused from the optional offline Sprint 11Y corpus; without a corpus every row is `UNAVAILABLE` (NOT `INSUFFICIENT`, never fabricated). The 11Y sample-size hard gate is preserved. UNAVAILABLE evidence is never conflated with INSUFFICIENT evidence.
- `tests/test_watchlist_scanner.py`: 75 tests across areas A-Z (A watchlist creation; B duplicates; C invalid instruments; D deterministic ordering; E single-instrument scan; F multi-instrument scan; G multi-timeframe; H unsupported timeframe; I unsupported instrument; J empty provider result; K provider failure; L one-symbol failure isolation; M fresh data; N stale data; O forming-candle exclusion; P future-candle rejection; Q no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise, no future-candle argument]; R decision preservation; S geometry preservation + target 2 unsupported; T evidence preservation + unavailable-not-insufficient; U actionability preservation + not BUY/SELL; V deterministic ranking/presentation ordering [decision rank, direction NOT a key, rank key shape]; W shuffle-invariance + repeated-scan; X API schema [row fields, custom watchlist, custom timeframe, review_url, target_2 unsupported, no predictive language]; Y dashboard rendering [scan page, nav, instruments, custom instruments, no predictive language, jsonable deterministic]; Z existing dashboard regression [existing routes present, pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3, trade-review view still works, scanner does not corrupt single analysis]).
- `scripts/test_watchlist_scanner.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 12 requirements (default watchlist scan; multiple instruments; decision classifications authoritative; actionability mirror; geometry reused + target 2 unsupported; evidence UNAVAILABLE-not-INSUFFICIENT; freshness STALE data-quality-only; one-instrument failure isolation; deterministic ordering + shuffle-invariance; no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise then restored]; existing pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3; API + HTML rendering + no predictive language). 49 PASS / 0 SKIPPED / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Product Phase 2 demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (multi-instrument orchestration + presentation, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every per-row value read from reused Sprint 11A-12E engines via the existing single-instrument `analyze`; the scanner only orchestrates + projects + sorts); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; NO existing engine/model modified (only additive new files + additive view models + additive service method/dataclass + additive routes + additive template/CSS/nav). No print() in engines (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/position-sizing/portfolio-management/background-polling/WebSockets. The scanner is DESCRIPTIVE ONLY ÔÇö it does NOT predict the market, does NOT guarantee future performance, does NOT constitute a trading recommendation, does NOT imply live-trading readiness, and does NOT invent a new trading score / probability / predictive ranking. The existing decision is AUTHORITATIVE (never upgraded/downgraded/renamed to BUY/SELL). Existing `TradeCandidate` geometry reused VERBATIM (never recomputed). Target 2 remains unsupported. 11Y hard gate + UNAVAILABLE-vs-INSUFFICIENT evidence distinction preserved. No future leakage (completed-candle boundary inherited from `analyze`; regression-tested for forming-candle exclusion, future-candle rejection, evaluator-not-called, pipeline-not-called, no-future-argument). Deterministic (input watchlist order never changes output order; identical data -> identical results + row order). Existing pipeline regression unchanged (signals=4, trades=3); 12C/12D/12E/Product-Phase-1/dashboard demos unchanged (33/45/49/33/38 PASS). No Sprint 11A-12E / Product-Phase-1 regression.

## PRODUCT PHASE 3 ÔÇö LIVE TRADING WORKSTATION / DASHBOARD (added)
This phase is NOT another intelligence / scoring layer, NOT a new orchestration package, and does NOT reopen the completed 11A-12E architecture. It is a coherent LIVE TRADING WORKSTATION presentation/orchestration increment that bundles the EXISTING Product Phase 1 single-instrument analysis + Product Phase 2 multi-instrument scanner into one coherent human-trader monitoring + trade-review surface. The workstation implements NO new market-analysis, decision, scoring, geometry, evidence or prediction logic; every value is read from the reused `DashboardAnalysisService.scan_watchlist` + `analyze` outputs. All changes are ADDITIVE and backward-compatible: no Sprint 11A-12E public API signature was changed, no existing engine/model was modified (only additive view models + additive service method/dataclass + additive routes + additive templates/CSS/nav + a refactored reusable trade-review include), and the prior 2710 tests still pass (now 2805 with 95 new in tests/test_workstation.py).
- Goal: make the EXISTING intelligence architecture + live data + scanner into a coherent LIVE TRADING WORKSTATION for human market monitoring and trade review. The workstation lets a trader: monitor the watchlist; see current/near-live data status; identify instruments requiring attention; select an instrument; inspect its chart; inspect current market structure + setup; inspect the authoritative decision; inspect trade geometry; inspect historical evidence; inspect actionability; understand WHY the instrument is in its current state; know whether the displayed data is current/stale/unavailable; refresh/re-scan deliberately; move between scanner and detailed review efficiently. It remains a DESCRIPTIVE / DECISION-SUPPORT presentation layer ÔÇö NOT a prediction engine.
- `src/dashboard/views.py` EXTENDED with the `WorkstationView` (frozen+slots) presentation bundle + `workstation_why(view) -> str` + `workstation_view_to_jsonable(view) -> dict`. `WorkstationView` bundles: `selected_instrument`, `setup_timeframe`/`context_timeframe`, the reused `WatchlistScanView` (BY REFERENCE, never modified), the reused `DashboardTradeView` for the selected instrument (BY REFERENCE, never modified; `None` only when nothing could be analysed), a deterministic `refresh_token` (the latest completed candle timestamp ISO of the selected view ÔÇö the honest evaluation boundary, NEVER a wall-clock value during fixture analysis; `""` when unavailable), a descriptive `rationale`, and a consolidated `limitations` tuple. Properties: `has_selected`, `is_empty`. `workstation_why` synthesizes a descriptive "why is this in its current state?" explanation from the REUSED outputs only (actionability reason + decision classification + scan reason + warnings) ÔÇö PRESENTATION TEXT, never new intelligence, never a BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD recommendation. `workstation_view_to_jsonable` reuses `scan_view_to_jsonable` + `to_jsonable` for the embedded scan + trade view; no value recomputed; the five concerns stay separate.
- `src/dashboard/services.py` EXTENDED with `WorkstationRequest` (frozen dataclass: `instrument`, `setup_timeframe`, `context_timeframe`, `watchlist`) + `DashboardAnalysisService.workstation(request) -> WorkstationView`. PURE ORCHESTRATION: reuses `scan_watchlist` (which reuses `analyze`) + `analyze` for the selected instrument. Implements NO new market-analysis/decision/scoring/geometry/evidence logic. GUARANTEES: reuse-only; completed-candle guarantee inherited from `analyze`/`scan_watchlist` (latest COMPLETED setup candle; forming candle never fed; no future candle read); no-look-ahead (accepts NO `future`/`future_candles` argument; never calls Sprint 11W `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate`; never runs `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate`); decision authority (existing classification reused VERBATIM, never renamed to BUY/SELL, never upgraded/downgraded; watchlist row order is the reused PRESENTATIONAL ordering ÔÇö a sort, not a score); DETERMINISTIC instrument selection (when the requested instrument is empty / not in the watchlist, deterministically selects the first analyzed non-error row's instrument ÔÇö never invents an opportunity; when nothing is analyzable the selected view is an honest unavailable view); MANUAL REFRESH ONLY (no background polling / no WebSocket streaming; the `refresh_token` is the honest evaluation boundary so a deliberate refresh re-runs the analysis over the latest completed candle). Failure isolation: an exception during the selected-instrument `analyze` is caught and converted to an honest unavailable view (never raises, never fabricates).
- `src/dashboard/app.py` EXTENDED with `GET /workstation` (HTML) + `GET /api/workstation` (JSON) routes. ALL existing routes preserved (`/`, `/health`, `/api/health`, `/api/analysis`, `/api/instruments`, `/scan`, `/api/scan`). The workstation route builds the `WorkstationView` via the service, builds the chart payload via the existing `chart_payload` (backend-authored only), and renders `workstation.html`. The `_workstation_why_text` helper renders the why explanation. The `_parse_watchlist` helper is shared by the workstation + scanner routes (watchlist parsing consolidated; no duplication). API responses remain structured + deterministic.
- `src/dashboard/templates/_trade_review.html` (NEW reusable include): the SINGLE-INSTRUMENT trade-review detail sections (data source / freshness, actionability, market overview, decision, trade geometry, evidence, setup details, chart, warnings) extracted from `dashboard.html` so the detail markup has ONE source of truth. `dashboard.html` now `{% include "_trade_review.html" %}` (behavior-identical; verified by the 98 existing dashboard tests). `workstation.html` also includes it for the selected-instrument detail. No intelligence in the include; every value reused verbatim; target 2 unsupported; no R:R recomputed in the frontend.
- `src/dashboard/templates/workstation.html` (NEW): the unified workstation layout ÔÇö top controls (timeframe / watchlist / focus instrument / Refresh button), Market / Watchlist Status summary + the reused scanner table (each row links to focus that instrument in the workstation via `/workstation?instrument=...`; the selected row is highlighted), Selected Instrument data block (provider / freshness / latest completed candle / forming candle / rejected future candles), the reused `_trade_review.html` detail, a "Why is this in its current state?" section, a consolidated Limitations section, and a navigation card (Back to Scanner / Trade Review / JSON). No predictive language; honest unavailable/INCOMPLETE states; explicit disclaimer that the workstation is descriptive only.
- `src/dashboard/templates/base.html` EXTENDED: nav now offers Workstation / Scanner / Trade Review (existing nav preserved). `src/dashboard/templates/scanner.html` EXTENDED: scanner row "Review" column now links to BOTH the workstation (`/workstation?instrument=...`) and the trade-review (`/?instrument=...`) ÔÇö improved navigation per spec; existing review link contract preserved. `src/dashboard/static/dashboard.css` EXTENDED (additive) with workstation styles (`.workstation-controls`, `.selected-instrument`, `.row-selected`, `.why-card`, `.nav-card`) + a responsive `@media` block; existing styles untouched.
- COMPLETED-CANDLE / NO-LOOK-AHEAD (HARD REQUIREMENT, structurally inherited + regression-tested): (1) the evaluation point is the latest COMPLETED setup candle (inherited from `analyze`); (2) a forming candle is never fed to the engine (boundary inherited from Product Phase 1); (3) future-dated candles are rejected; (4) the workstation never calls `OutcomeEvaluator.evaluate` (test patches it to raise); (5) the workstation never calls `HistoricalEvaluationPipeline.evaluate` (test patches it to raise); (6) the public `workstation()` + `WorkstationRequest` APIs accept NO `future`/`future_candles` argument (signature inspected); (7) a fixed-T analysis is unaffected by appending a future candle (entry/stop/target/decision/refresh_token unchanged).
- DECISION AUTHORITY (regression-tested): the existing Sprint 11S decision classification (REJECTED/WATCH/QUALIFIED/PREFERRED) is reused VERBATIM on the selected view + every scanner row ÔÇö never renamed to BUY/SELL/ENTER/EXIT/HOLD, never upgraded/downgraded. The watchlist row order is the reused PRESENTATIONAL ordering (a sort, NOT a predictive score / probability / new ranking layer). Direction (LONG/SHORT) is deliberately NOT a ranking key.
- TRADE GEOMETRY (regression-tested): entry/stop/target_1/risk_distance/reward_distance/risk_reward_ratio/setup_type/geometry_complete are reused VERBATIM from the Sprint 11R `TradeCandidate` (reached via the reused scan decision). The chart payload levels (entry/stop/target/support/resistance/invalidation) are backend-authored and rendered verbatim; the frontend never recomputes R:R / derives another stop / derives another target. Target 2 remains `None` + `target_2_supported=False` (in the geometry, the API, the HTML, and the consolidated limitations). Geometry-unavailable state surfaced honestly ("TRADE GEOMETRY UNAVAILABLE"; no fabricated level).
- EVIDENCE (regression-tested): evidence is reused from the optional offline Sprint 11Y corpus; without a corpus it is honestly `UNAVAILABLE` (NOT `INSUFFICIENT`, never fabricated). Observed historical result + evidence strength stay SEPARATE (never merged into a "confidence score"). The 11Y sample-size hard gate is preserved.
- ACTIONABILITY (regression-tested): the existing `ActionabilityState` (INVALID/NO_OPPORTUNITY/TRADE_GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE/INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE/READY_FOR_REVIEW/WAIT) is reused VERBATIM ÔÇö no new actionability system. It is a documented deterministic presentation mirror, NOT a predictive score / probability / BUY/SELL recommendation.
- REFRESH MODEL: a deliberate manual **Refresh** button (GET form submit) re-runs the analysis over the latest COMPLETED candle. There is NO background polling, NO WebSocket streaming, NO `setInterval` / `new WebSocket` (regression-tested). The `refresh_token` is the honest evaluation boundary, deterministic for identical data.
- DETERMINISM (no wall-clock, no randomness, no unordered iteration): repeated + shuffled-watchlist `workstation()` calls produce identical JSON output + identical row order; the selected instrument is chosen deterministically when not specified (first analyzed row). The `WorkstationView` is frozen+slots.
- `tests/test_workstation.py`: 95 tests across areas A-AJ (A workstation page loads; B existing dashboard still loads; C scanner still loads; D navigation [workstation nav link, scanner rows link to workstation, back-to-scanner, all existing routes preserved]; E instrument selection [override, unknown falls back, empty selects first analyzed, selected matches single analysis]; F timeframe selection; G refresh [refresh_token==evaluation boundary, deterministic, refresh button, no background polling]; H current data display; I stale data display; J provider unavailable [ERROR/NOT_READY honest]; K unsupported timeframe; L unsupported instrument [fall back + custom watchlist failure isolation]; M chart payload [candles, levels match geometry]; N/O/P/Q entry/stop/target/R:R preservation [match single analysis + reused from candidate]; R decision preservation [match single, never BUY/SELL, authoritative label]; S evidence preservation [unavailable without corpus, not fabricated]; AG geometry unavailable state [honest, no fabricated]; AH evidence unavailable vs insufficient [UNAVAILABLE != INSUFFICIENT]; AI responsive/template sections [all spec sections, responsive CSS, disclaimer]; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3).
- `scripts/test_workstation.py`: demo ÔÇö visibly proves all 18 required demonstrations (workstation starts; health; scanner; instrument selection; provider metadata; freshness; completed-candle analysis; decision authoritative-not-BUY/SELL; actionability mirror; trade geometry reused + target 2 unsupported; evidence UNAVAILABLE-not-INSUFFICIENT; chart payload backend-authored; refresh deliberate/manual + no polling; unsupported timeframe handling; provider failure isolation; no-look-ahead [evaluator+pipeline patched to raise, no future argument]; decision authority [existing vocabulary preserved]; pipeline baseline signals=4/trades=3). 54 PASS / 0 FAIL. Ends with "Product Phase 3 demo completed successfully." Demo exits 0.
Architectural invariants preserved: NO new orchestration package (workstation is presentation + orchestration over existing `scan_watchlist` + `analyze`, not an architectural layer); NO new intelligence/trading logic (every value read from reused Sprint 11A-12E engines via the existing Product Phase 1/2 service methods; the workstation only orchestrates + projects + bundles); models <- intelligence <- pipeline unchanged in direction; intelligence/__init__.py remains intentionally empty; NO existing engine/model modified (only additive view models + additive service method/dataclass + additive routes + additive templates/CSS/nav + a refactored reusable trade-review include that is behavior-identical to the prior inline dashboard markup). No print() in engines (demo may print). No ML/LLM/broker/live-trading/order-execution/portfolio-management/background-polling/WebSockets/autonomous-trading/paper-trading. Deterministic (input watchlist order never changes output order; identical data -> identical results + row order; deterministic instrument selection).
